📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期登革病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • DENV 和 CHIKV 是非洲之角重要且可能未被充分认识的虫媒病毒威胁,有证据表明 传播 持续存在且广泛暴露。汇集的 DENV 患病率 IgG 为 20%,IgM 为 10%,NS1 为 6%,PCR 为 4%。IgG 的 CHIKV 患病率 为 5%,IgM 的 CHIKV 患病率 为 8%,PCR 为 4%。所有四种 DENV 血清型均在肯尼亚和埃塞俄比亚传播,而 DENV-2 和 DENV-3 则在索马里报告。埃及伊蚊是主要的 媒介。
  • 将建立两个独立的模型:一个预测报告的登革热病例的住院情况,另一个预测住院登革热患者的 死亡率。 [10.64898/2026.08.03.26359583]。
  • DENV-4 感染会导致 DNA 损伤并下调 DNA 修复基因转录,导致持久的生物“疤痕”,从而增加慢性疾病风险。 [10.1073/pnas.2536909123]。
  • 与瑞德西韦核苷 (RVn) 相比,V2043 是多种黄病毒的更有效的体外抑制剂,包括登革热病毒、寨卡病毒、西尼罗河病毒和日本脑炎病毒。每日一次口服 V2043 可显着降低小鼠模型中的登革热血清病毒血症、临床严重程度、体重减轻和 死亡率。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • DENV and CHIKV are important and likely underrecognized arboviral threats in the Horn of Africa, with evidence of ongoing transmission and widespread exposure.
  • Two independent models will be built: one predicting hospitalization among reported dengue cases and another predicting mortality among hospitalized dengue patients. [10.64898/2026.08.03.26359583].
  • DENV-4 infection causes DNA damage and downregulates DNA repair gene transcription, leading to durable biological 'scars' that increase chronic disease risk. [10.1073/pnas.2536909123].
  • V2043 was a more potent in vitro inhibitor of multiple flaviviruses, including dengue, Zika, West Nile, and Japanese encephalitis viruses, compared to remdesivir nucleoside (RVn).

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期登革病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 北弗吉尼亚州居民确诊一例本地获得的 登革病毒 感染病例。这是弗吉尼亚州第一例有记录的本地感染登革热病例。该患者在发病前没有已知的弗吉尼亚州以外的旅行经历。 [news-0b105fd470ff1a92]。
  • 自 2025 年 1 月 1 日以来,多个太平洋岛屿国家和地区 (PIC) 确诊登革热 暴发,已报告 12 例 暴发。暴发 于 2026 年在新喀里多尼亚、汤加和谢法省(瓦努阿图)正式宣布。截至 2026 年 8 月 3 日,暴发 在瓦努阿图谢法省仍在进行中。
  • 弗吉尼亚州卫生官员报告了首例本地感染的 登革病毒 病例,这是一种蚊媒疾病。该事件已得到官方国家报告的证实。 [news-f4b5ace1cd4ccd33]。
  • 弗吉尼亚州卫生部 (VDH) 确认该州首例由蚊子传播的本地 登革病毒 病例。 [news-32ce8ecabab38d4c]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Dengue virus news developments.
  • A confirmed case of locally acquired dengue virus infection in a Northern Virginia resident. This is the first documented case of locally acquired dengue in Virginia.
  • Confirmed dengue outbreaks across multiple Pacific Island Countries and Areas (PICs) since 1 January 2025, with 12 outbreaks reported.
  • Virginia state health officials reported the first locally acquired case of dengue virus, a mosquito-borne illness. The event is confirmed by official state reporting. [news-f4b5ace1cd4ccd33].
  • Virginia Department of Health (VDH) confirmed the first locally acquired case of dengue virus transmitted by mosquitoes in the state. [news-32ce8ecabab38d4c].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: BMC Infectious Diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-31  |  DOI: 10.1186/s12879-026-14111-x  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 26, 1, 1414
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

一项多中心前瞻性观察多组学研究方案,用于识别 重症登革热 的生物标志物:COMBAT 研究临床方案。

A multicenter prospective observational multi-omics study protocol to identify biomarkers for severe dengue: COMBAT study clinical protocol.
作者： Piya Paul Mudgal, Silvia Patricia Zuniga Veliz, Magda Lourda, Trupti Satish Kadni, Muralidhar Varma, Anup Mazumder, Sandeep Budhiraja, Namita Jaggi, Chiranjay Mukhopadhyay, Sagar Sengupta
摘要中文翻译背景:登革病毒 (DENV) 感染给全球公共卫生带来了重大负担,特别是在反复接触会增加严重疾病风险的流行地区。尽管世界卫生组织 (WHO) 登革热分类进行了修订,但由于临床和实验室特征重叠,早期预测 重症登革热 的进展仍然具有挑战性。目前的管理策略主要依赖于支持性护理和反应性监测,强调需要能够进行早期风险分层和及时干预的预测性生物标志物。方法:COMBAT 是一项在危地马拉和印度登革热流行地区进行的前瞻性、多中心、观察性纵向研究。根据 WHO 2009 年标准,患者将被分为无预警症状的登革热、有预警症状的登革热和 重症登革热,以及年龄和性别匹配的健康对照。将从非住院患者身上采集一份血液样本,而每个参与者在住院期间采集两份血液样本,在出院时采集一份血液样本。多组学分析,包括转录组学、蛋白质组学、糖组学和代谢组学,将在发现 队列研究 中进行,并在独立的 队列研究 中进行验证。综合系统生物学方法将用于在基于机制的预后生物标志物发现中识别与登革热严重程度相关的 宿主 免疫和代谢途径。讨论:本研究旨在对导致登革热严重程度的 宿主 病毒相互作用产生全面的系统级见解,并确定预测疾病进展的生物标志物。这些发现可能有助于改进患者分诊、早期干预策略以及确定新的治疗靶点。试验注册:ClinicalTrials.gov ID NCT06751836 注册日期:2024 年 12 月 13 日,CTRI/2022/10/046293 (MAHE) 注册日期:2022 年 10 月 10 日。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 感染构成了全球重大公共卫生负担,特别是在反复接触会增加严重疾病风险的流行地区。
设计与对象
根据 WHO 2009 年标准,患者将被分为无预警症状的登革热、有预警症状的登革热和 重症登革热,以及年龄和性别匹配的健康对照。
核心方法
COMBAT 是一项在危地马拉和印度登革热流行地区进行的前瞻性、多中心、观察性纵向研究。
主要结果
综合系统生物学方法将用于在基于机制的预后生物标志物发现中识别与登革热严重程度相关的 宿主 免疫和代谢途径。
解释与创新
这些发现可能有助于改进患者分诊、早期干预策略以及确定新的治疗靶点。
科研与公卫意义
目前的管理策略主要依赖于支持性护理和反应性监测,强调需要能够进行早期风险分层和及时干预的预测性生物标志物。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取基于检索到的全文证据。消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

A multicenter prospective observational multi-omics study protocol to identify biomarkers for severe dengue: COMBAT study clinical protocol.

Authors: Piya Paul Mudgal, Silvia Patricia Zuniga Veliz, Magda Lourda, Trupti Satish Kadni, Muralidhar Varma, Anup Mazumder, Sandeep Budhiraja, Namita Jaggi, Chiranjay Mukhopadhyay, Sagar Sengupta
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue virus (DENV) infection poses a major global public health burden, particularly in endemic regions where repeated exposure increases the risk of severe disease. Despite revisions to the World Health Organization (WHO) dengue classification, early prediction of progression to severe dengue remains challenging due to overlapping clinical and laboratory features. Current management strategies rely primarily on supportive care and reactive monitoring, underscoring the need for predictive biomarkers that enable early risk stratification and timely intervention. METHODS: COMBAT is a prospective, multicenter, observational longitudinal study conducted in dengue-endemic regions of Guatemala and India. Patients will be classified according to WHO 2009 criteria into dengue without warning signs, dengue with warning signs, and severe dengue, alongside age- and sex-matched healthy controls. A single blood sample will be collected from non-hospitalized patients while two blood samples per participant will be collected during hospitalization and one at discharge. Multi-omics analyses, including transcriptomics, proteomics, glycomics and metabolomics, will be performed in a discovery cohort and validated in an independent cohort. Integrated systems biology approaches will be used to identify host immune and metabolic pathways associated with dengue severity in a mechanism-based prognostic biomarker discovery. DISCUSSION: This study aims to generate comprehensive systems-level insights into host-virus interactions driving dengue severity and to identify biomarkers predictive of disease progression. The findings may inform improved patient triage, early intervention strategies, and the identification of novel therapeutic targets. TRIAL REGISTRATION: ClinicalTrials.gov ID NCT06751836 Registration Date: December 13, 2024, CTRI/2022/10/046293 (MAHE) Registration Date: October 10, 2022.
View research seven-element analysis

Dengue virus (DENV) infection poses a major global public health burden, particularly in endemic regions where repeated exposure increases the risk of severe disease.
Design and population
Patients will be classified according to WHO 2009 criteria into dengue without warning signs, dengue with warning signs, and severe dengue, alongside age- and sex-matched healthy controls.
Core methods
COMBAT is a prospective, multicenter, observational longitudinal study conducted in dengue-endemic regions of Guatemala and India.
Main results
Integrated systems biology approaches will be used to identify host immune and metabolic pathways associated with dengue severity in a mechanism-based prognostic biomarker discovery.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings may inform improved patient triage, early intervention strategies, and the identification of novel therapeutic targets.
Scientific and public-health significance
Current management strategies rely primarily on supportive care and reactive monitoring, underscoring the need for predictive biomarkers that enable early risk stratification and timely intervention.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on retrieved fulltext evidence. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

Journal: Antimicrobial Agents and Chemotherapy  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-03  |  DOI: 10.1128/aac.00401-26  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e0040126
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

口服脂质前药 V2043(1-O-十八烷基-2-O-苄基-sn-甘油基-P-瑞德西韦核苷)可保护小鼠免受致命的登革热感染。

Oral lipid prodrug V2043 (1- O -octadecyl-2- O -benzyl- sn -glyceryl-P-remdesivir nucleoside) protects mice from lethal dengue infection.
作者： Alex E. Clark, Jonathan R. Erlich, James R. Beadle, Rachel E. McMillan, Jialei Xie, Aaron F. Garretson, Shae Atkins, Anoushka Sharma, Suraj Manohar Rajan, Ben A. Croker
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒 和相关黄病毒对全球健康构成重大威胁,目前尚未获得批准的 抗病毒药物 治疗。瑞德西韦三磷酸 (RVn-TP) 是一种核苷酸类似物,可抑制许多临床重要 RNA 病毒(包括 登革病毒)的 RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶。一种可有效递送 RVn-TP 的口服生物可利用前药可以促进非住院登革热患者的早期 治疗。为此,我们开发了 1-O-十八烷基-2-O-苄基-sn-甘油基-P-瑞德西韦核苷(V2043,ODBG-P-RVn),这是一种磷脂前药,通过改善口服生物利用度、效力和药代动力学来实现 RVn-TP 递送。在这里,我们表明,与母体化合物瑞德西韦核苷 (RVn) 相比,V2043 是多种黄病毒的更有效的体外抑制剂,包括登革热、寨卡病毒、西尼罗河病毒和日本脑炎病毒。在概括人类 重症登革热 关键方面的致死性预防和治疗小鼠模型中,每日一次口服 V2043 显着降低登革热血清病毒血症、临床严重程度、体重减轻和 死亡率。此外,口服 V2043 在连续 14 天每天给药时具有良好的耐受性。这些结果证明了 V2043 对治疗几种临床相关黄病毒的潜在治疗功效,包括 登革病毒(世界上最流行和最广泛的虫媒病毒感染)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 和相关黄病毒对全球健康构成重大威胁,目前尚无批准的 抗病毒药物 治疗 可用。
设计与对象
该研究使用致命性预防性和治疗性小鼠模型,概括了 重症登革热 在人类中的关键方面。
核心方法
开发了一种口服生物利用度前药 1-O-十八烷基-2-O-苄基-sn-甘油基-P-瑞德西韦核苷(V2043,ODBG-P-RVn),通过改善口服生物利用度、效力和药代动力学来递送瑞德西韦三磷酸盐(RVn-TP)。
主要结果
与瑞德西韦核苷 (RVn) 相比,V2043 是多种黄病毒的更有效的体外抑制剂,包括登革热病毒、寨卡病毒、西尼罗河病毒和日本脑炎病毒。每日一次口服 V2043 可显着降低小鼠模型中的登革热血清病毒血症、临床严重程度、体重减轻和 死亡率。
解释与创新
结果证明了 V2043 对治疗几种临床相关黄病毒的潜在治疗功效,包括 登革病毒(世界上最流行和最广泛的虫媒病毒感染)。
科研与公卫意义
口服 V2043 可以促进非住院登革热患者的早期 抗病毒药物,解决这一全球健康威胁的 抗病毒药物 选择中的关键差距。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在小鼠模型中进行的,该模型可能无法完全复制人类登革热病理生理学,但由于多个终点的一致且显着的结果,证据强度很高。

Oral lipid prodrug V2043 (1- O -octadecyl-2- O -benzyl- sn -glyceryl-P-remdesivir nucleoside) protects mice from lethal dengue infection.

Authors: Alex E. Clark, Jonathan R. Erlich, James R. Beadle, Rachel E. McMillan, Jialei Xie, Aaron F. Garretson, Shae Atkins, Anoushka Sharma, Suraj Manohar Rajan, Ben A. Croker
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue virus and related flaviviruses are significant threats to global health, with no approved antiviral treatments available. Remdesivir triphosphate (RVn-TP) is a nucleotide analog that inhibits RNA-dependent RNA polymerases from many clinically important RNA viruses, including dengue virus. An orally bioavailable prodrug that efficiently delivers RVn-TP could facilitate early treatment for non-hospitalized dengue patients. To that end, we developed 1- O -octadecyl-2- O -benzyl- sn -glyceryl-P-remdesivir nucleoside (V2043, ODBG-P-RVn), a phospholipid prodrug that achieves RVn-TP delivery through improved oral bioavailability, potency, and pharmacokinetics. Here, we show that when compared to the parent compound remdesivir nucleoside (RVn), V2043 is a more potent in vitro inhibitor of multiple flaviviruses, including dengue, Zika, West Nile, and Japanese encephalitis viruses. In lethal prophylactic and therapeutic mouse models that recapitulate key aspects of severe dengue in humans, once-daily oral V2043 significantly decreased dengue serum viremia, clinical severity, weight loss, and mortality. Additionally, oral V2043 was well tolerated when administered daily for 14 days. These results demonstrate the potential therapeutic efficacy of V2043 to treat several clinically relevant flaviviruses, including dengue virus, the most prevalent and widespread arbovirus infection in the world.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus and related flaviviruses are significant threats to global health, with no approved antiviral treatments available.
Design and population
The study used lethal prophylactic and therapeutic mouse models that recapitulate key aspects of severe dengue in humans.
Core methods
An orally bioavailable prodrug, 1-O-octadecyl-2-O-benzyl-sn-glyceryl-P-remdesivir nucleoside (V2043, ODBG-P-RVn), was developed to deliver remdesivir triphosphate (RVn-TP) through improved oral bioavailability, potency, and pharmacokinetics.
Main results
V2043 was a more potent in vitro inhibitor of multiple flaviviruses, including dengue, Zika, West Nile, and Japanese encephalitis viruses, compared to remdesivir nucleoside (RVn). Once-daily oral V2043 significantly decreased dengue serum viremia, clinical severity, weight loss, and mortality in mouse models.
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrate the potential therapeutic efficacy of V2043 to treat several clinically relevant flaviviruses, including dengue virus, the most prevalent and widespread arbovirus infection in the world.
Scientific and public-health significance
Oral V2043 could facilitate early treatment for non-hospitalized dengue patients, addressing a critical gap in antiviral options for this global health threat.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in mouse models, which may not fully replicate human dengue pathophysiology, but the evidence strength is high due to consistent and significant results across multiple endpoints.

Journal: Cell Reports  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-01  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.celrep.2026.117764  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 45, 8, 117764
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

原发性登革热感染期间种系编码抗体的建立。

Establishment of germline-encoded antibodies during primary dengue infection.
作者： Thomas-Wolf Verdonckt, Marek Sahulčík, Johanna Bouckaert, Anne-Sofie Vermeersch, Caroline Struyfs, Filip Van Nieuwerburgh, Adam Waickman, Stephen J. Thomas, Ole Lagatie, Kevin K. Ariën
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 给全球健康带来了重大负担,而不完善的 疫苗 则引发了人们的担忧,即未接触过登革热的个体的免疫接种可能会通过免疫病理机制加剧疾病。使用原发性 DENV1 感染的受控人类挑战模型,我们纵向分析了 9 名未接触过黄病毒的参与者的 B 细胞受体库。尽管原发感染具有血清学特征,但 4/9 的参与者中,急性反应主要由记忆衍生克隆主导。我们还鉴定了来自初始 B 细胞的公共收敛克隆,这些克隆在 7 个参与者中具有相同的序列特征,并且富含富含酪氨酸的 CDRH3 基序。感染后 28 天,这些类别转换的 IgG 克隆在携带这些公共谱系的所有参与者中仍然保持相对种系样,这与生发中心成熟之前的早期类别转换输出或不依赖 GC 的途径一致。这些发现完善了登革热早期抗体反应模型,并鉴定了类似种系的公共 BCR 谱系,包括与 DENV 结合非中和抗体相关的基序,其特异性和功能作用值得进一步研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 给全球健康带来了重大负担,而不完善的 疫苗 则引发了人们的担忧,即未接触过登革热的个体的免疫接种可能会通过免疫病理机制加剧疾病。
设计与对象
使用原发性 DENV1 感染的受控人类挑战模型,我们纵向分析了 9 名未接触过黄病毒的参与者的 B 细胞受体库。
核心方法
尽管原发感染具有血清学特征,但 4/9 的参与者中,急性反应主要由记忆衍生克隆主导。我们还鉴定了来自初始 B 细胞的公共收敛克隆,这些克隆在 7 个参与者中具有相同的序列特征,并且富含富含酪氨酸的 CDRH3 基序。
主要结果
感染后 28 天,这些类别转换的 IgG 克隆在携带这些公共谱系的所有参与者中仍然保持相对种系样,这与生发中心成熟之前的早期类别转换输出或不依赖 GC 的途径一致。
解释与创新
这些发现完善了登革热早期抗体反应模型,并鉴定了类似种系的公共 BCR 谱系,包括与 DENV 结合非中和抗体相关的基序,其特异性和功能作用值得进一步研究。
科研与公卫意义
该研究鉴定了登革热感染中类似种系的公共 BCR 谱系,这可能为 疫苗 的设计和免疫病理学机制的理解提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量较小(9 名参与者)和使用受控挑战模型的限制,该模型可能无法完全代表自然感染动态。由于样本量有限,证据强度中等,但由于受控实验设计,证据强度较高。

Establishment of germline-encoded antibodies during primary dengue infection.

Authors: Thomas-Wolf Verdonckt, Marek Sahulčík, Johanna Bouckaert, Anne-Sofie Vermeersch, Caroline Struyfs, Filip Van Nieuwerburgh, Adam Waickman, Stephen J. Thomas, Ole Lagatie, Kevin K. Ariën
Original abstract (English)
Dengue viruses impose a major global health burden, while imperfect vaccines raise concerns that the immunization of dengue-naïve individuals may exacerbate disease through immunopathologic mechanisms. Using a controlled human challenge model of primary DENV1 infection, we longitudinally profile B cell receptor repertoires in 9 flavivirus-naïve participants. Despite serological features of primary infection, acute responses are dominated by memory-derived clones in 4/9 participants. We also identify public convergent clones from naive B cells that share sequence features across 7 participants and are enriched for tyrosine-rich CDRH3 motifs. 28 days post infection, these class-switched IgG clones remain relatively germline-like in all participants carrying these public lineages, consistent with early class-switched output before germinal center maturation or a GC-independent pathway. These findings refine models of early antibody responses in dengue and identify germline-like public BCR lineages, including motifs linked to DENV-binding non-neutralizing antibodies, whose specificity and functional role warrant further study.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue viruses impose a major global health burden, while imperfect vaccines raise concerns that the immunization of dengue-naïve individuals may exacerbate disease through immunopathologic mechanisms.
Design and population
Using a controlled human challenge model of primary DENV1 infection, we longitudinally profile B cell receptor repertoires in 9 flavivirus-naïve participants.
Core methods
Despite serological features of primary infection, acute responses are dominated by memory-derived clones in 4/9 participants. We also identify public convergent clones from naive B cells that share sequence features across 7 participants and are enriched for tyrosine-rich CDRH3 motifs.
Main results
28 days post infection, these class-switched IgG clones remain relatively germline-like in all participants carrying these public lineages, consistent with early class-switched output before germinal center maturation or a GC-independent pathway.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings refine models of early antibody responses in dengue and identify germline-like public BCR lineages, including motifs linked to DENV-binding non-neutralizing antibodies, whose specificity and functional role warrant further study.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study identifies germline-like public BCR lineages in dengue infection, which may inform vaccine design and understanding of immunopathologic mechanisms.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by a small sample size (9 participants) and the use of a controlled challenge model, which may not fully represent natural infection dynamics. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limited sample size but high due to the controlled experimental design.

2020-2025 年墨西哥四种登革热血清型的时空共同循环:时空扫描分析。

Spatiotemporal co-circulation of four dengue serotypes across Mexico, 2020-2025: A space-time scan analysis.
作者： Oliver Mendoza-Cano, Xóchitl Trujillo, Mónica Ríos-Silva, Jaime Alberto Bricio-Barrios, Agustin Lugo-Radillo, Rosa Tapia-Vargas, Jesús Venegas-Ramírez, Eder Fernando Ríos-Bracamontes, Herguin Benjamin Cuevas-Arellano, Yolitzy Cárdenas
摘要中文翻译背景:墨西哥经历了由四种抗原不同血清型(DENV-1 至 DENV-4)共同循环驱动的登革热 传播 升级。多血清型 传播 具有流行病学相关性,但其在次国家分辨率下的时空模式仍知之甚少。方法:我们分析了 2020 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间墨西哥 2,471 个城市中 1,547 个城市的 103,426 例 PCR 确诊的血清型登革热病例。离散泊松模型下的 Kulldorff 时空扫描统计数据独立应用于每种血清型和所有血清型组合。共循环被定义为同一城市内至少两种血清型的显着集群在≥1个流行病学周内的时空重叠。结果:我们在所有血清型中确定了 159 个具有统计显着性的簇。DENV-3 占主导地位(64.4% 的病例;每年 发病率 59.9/100,000),与长期缺失的血清型的重新出现一致。2024-2025年季节在地理上遥远的地区发生了全国同步流行病。1,545 个城市中出现了至少两种血清型的共同传播;217 人同时经历了所有四种血清型的聚集。311 个研究周中有 275 周存在活跃的同循环。平均成对时间重叠范围为 8.0 至 12.0 周,最大重叠为 29-30 周。结论:市政府决议记录了四种血清型共循环,集中在墨西哥湾沿岸、尤卡坦半岛和墨西哥东北部。同时聚集所有四种血清型的 217 个城市可能代表了需要进一步调查的流行病学领域。这个市级时空框架为跟踪多血清型活动和支持国家以下范围内的血清型敏感登革热监测提供了操作上相关的解决方案。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于四种抗原性不同的血清型(DENV-1至DENV-4)的共同传播,墨西哥面临登革热传播的升级,但其在次国家分辨率下的时空模式仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
使用 2020 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间墨西哥 1,547 个城市的 103,426 例 PCR 确诊、血清型登革热病例进行了时空扫描分析。
核心方法
离散泊松模型下的库尔多夫时空扫描统计数据独立应用于每种血清型和所有血清型,以识别共循环集群,定义为同一城市内至少两种血清型的显着集群的时空重叠≥1个流行病学周。
主要结果
在所有血清型中总共确定了 159 个具有统计学意义的集群,其中 DENV-3 占主导地位(占病例的 64.4%;年度 发病率 59.9/100,000),并且在 1,545 个城市中存在至少两种血清型的共同循环,其中包括 217 个城市同时出现所有四种血清型的集群。
解释与创新
该研究记录了市政决议中四种血清型的共同传播,集中在墨西哥湾沿岸、尤卡坦半岛和墨西哥东北部,突出了流行病学感兴趣的地区以供进一步调查。
科研与公卫意义
该市级时空框架为跟踪多血清型登革热活动提供了可操作的相关解决方案,并支持在次国家范围内进行血清型感知监测。
局限与证据强度
市级聚类的证据很充分,但由于缺乏昆虫学或 媒介 数据而受到限制,这限制了对 传播 驱动因素和生态决定因素的推断。

Spatiotemporal co-circulation of four dengue serotypes across Mexico, 2020-2025: A space-time scan analysis.

Authors: Oliver Mendoza-Cano, Xóchitl Trujillo, Mónica Ríos-Silva, Jaime Alberto Bricio-Barrios, Agustin Lugo-Radillo, Rosa Tapia-Vargas, Jesús Venegas-Ramírez, Eder Fernando Ríos-Bracamontes, Herguin Benjamin Cuevas-Arellano, Yolitzy Cárdenas
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Mexico has experienced escalating dengue transmission driven by the co‐circulation of four antigenically distinct serotypes (DENV‐1 through DENV‐4). Multi‐serotype transmission is epidemiologically relevant, yet its spatiotemporal patterns at sub‐national resolution remain poorly characterized. METHODS: We analyzed 103,426 PCR‐confirmed, serotyped dengue cases across 1,547 of 2,471 Mexican municipalities from January 2020 to December 2025. Kulldorff's space‐time scan statistic under a discrete Poisson model was applied independently to each serotype and to all serotypes combined. Co‐circulation was defined as the spatiotemporal overlap of significant clusters from at least two serotypes within the same municipality for ≥ 1 epidemiological week. RESULTS: We identified 159 statistically significant clusters across all serotypes combined. DENV‐3 was dominant (64.4% of cases; annual incidence 59.9/100,000), consistent with the reemergence of a long‐absent serotype. The 2024-2025 season produced a nationally synchronized epidemic across geographically distant regions. Co‐circulation of at least two serotypes occurred in 1,545 municipalities; 217 experienced simultaneous clustering of all four serotypes. Active co‐circulation was present in 275 of 311 study weeks. Mean pairwise temporal overlap ranged from 8.0 to 12.0 weeks, with maximum overlaps of 29-30 weeks. CONCLUSIONS: Four‐serotype co‐circulation was documented at municipal resolution, concentrated in the Gulf coast, the Yucatán Peninsula, and northeastern Mexico. The 217 municipalities with simultaneous clustering of all four serotypes may represent areas of epidemiological interest for further investigation. This municipality‐level spatiotemporal framework offers operationally relevant resolution for tracking multi‐serotype activity and supporting serotype‐aware dengue monitoring at sub‐national scale.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mexico faces escalating dengue transmission due to the co-circulation of four antigenically distinct serotypes (DENV-1 through DENV-4), yet their spatiotemporal patterns at sub-national resolution remain poorly characterized.
Design and population
A space-time scan analysis was conducted using 103,426 PCR-confirmed, serotyped dengue cases from 1,547 municipalities across Mexico between January 2020 and December 2025.
Core methods
Kulldorff's space-time scan statistic under a discrete Poisson model was applied independently to each serotype and to all serotypes combined to identify co-circulation clusters, defined as spatiotemporal overlap of significant clusters from at least two serotypes within the same municipality for ≥ 1 epidemiological week.
Main results
A total of 159 statistically significant clusters were identified across all serotypes combined, with DENV-3 dominant (64.4% of cases; annual incidence 59.9/100,000), and co-circulation of at least two serotypes occurring in 1,545 municipalities, including 217 municipalities with simultaneous clustering of all four serotypes.
Interpretation and novelty
The study documents four-serotype co-circulation at municipal resolution, concentrated in the Gulf coast, the Yucatán Peninsula, and northeastern Mexico, highlighting areas of epidemiological interest for further investigation.
Scientific and public-health significance
This municipality-level spatiotemporal framework provides operationally relevant resolution for tracking multi-serotype dengue activity and supports serotype-aware monitoring at sub-national scale.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is strong for municipal-level clustering but limited by the absence of entomological or vector data, which constrains inference on transmission drivers and ecological determinants.

重新利用治疗登革出血热和相关自身免疫性疾病的药物

Repurposing drugs for treating dengue haemorrhagic fever and associated autoimmune diseases
作者： Govinda Rao Dabburu, Anjali Garg, Anchal Vishnoi, Neelja Singhal, Manish Kumar
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 (DENV) 通常会引起轻微的发热性疾病,某些患者可能会突然出现登革出血热 (DHF) 或登革休克综合征。在表现出长期登革热症状的患者中观察到自身免疫标志物表达的改变和自身免疫性疾病的发展,这表明登革病毒和自身免疫性疾病之间可能存在相关性。DENV 对凝血途径蛋白的分子模拟可能是 DHF 临床表现的潜在因素。抑制 DENV 拟态蛋白和人类蛋白中的蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用 (PPI) 可以潜在地治疗 DHF 和相关的自身免疫性疾病。在这项研究中,我们对与 DENV 拟态蛋白 (HPIDMP) 相互作用的人类蛋白质作为新的药物靶点进行了系统的计算机分析。HPIDMP 的潜在抑制剂是按照严格的参数从 DrugBank 数据库中识别出来的。使用分子对接预测蛋白质-配体相互作用,并通过基于诱饵的验证、分子动力学模拟和 MM-PBSA 结合自由能计算进行评估。RAF1 靶向药物索拉非尼和瑞戈非尼在所有计算分析中表现出最一致的相互作用特征,其次是 SIRT1 靶向化合物 selisistat 和白藜芦醇,它们表现出中等但一致的计算支持。相比之下,MYH9-青蒿醇和HSPE1-异硫氰酸苯乙酯系统显示出相对较弱的支持。根据我们的结果,我们建议索拉非尼和瑞戈非尼作为 DHF 和/或 DENV 相关自身免疫性疾病 治疗 体外或临床研究最有希望的候选药物,其次是 Selisistat 和白藜芦醇。因此,这些 DrugBank 分子可以纳入 DHF 和/或 DENV 相关自身免疫性疾病的 治疗 的临床研究或体外测试中。此外,本研究中采用的方法可以指导已知药物的重新利用,以治疗其他病原体相关的自身免疫性疾病。图解摘要 补充信息 在线版本包含可在 10.1007/s40203-026-00699-0 获取的补充材料。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
重症登革热 (DENV) 可引起轻度发热性疾病,但可能进展为 重症登革热,包括登革出血热 (DHF) 和登革热休克综合征。自身免疫标志物表达的改变和登革热症状的延长表明登革热病毒感染与自身免疫性疾病之间可能存在联系。
设计与对象
该研究对作为新药物靶点的 DENV 拟态蛋白 (HPIDMP) 相互作用的人类蛋白质进行了系统的计算机分析。使用严格的参数从 DrugBank 数据库中识别出潜在的抑制剂。
核心方法
使用分子对接预测蛋白质-配体相互作用,并通过基于诱饵的验证、分子动力学模拟和 MM-PBSA 结合自由能计算进行评估。
主要结果
RAF1 靶向药物索拉非尼和瑞戈非尼在所有计算分析中表现出最一致的相互作用特征。SIRT1 靶向化合物 Selisistat 和白藜芦醇表现出中等但一致的计算支持,而 MYH9-青蒿醇和 HSPE1-异硫氰酸苯乙酯系统表现出相对较弱的支持。
解释与创新
作者提出,索拉非尼和瑞格非尼是 DHF 和/或 DENV 相关自身免疫性疾病 治疗 体外或临床研究最有希望的候选药物,其次是 Selisistat 和白藜芦醇。该研究引入了一个计算框架来指导针对 DENV 相关疾病的药物再利用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,索拉非尼、瑞戈非尼、Selisistat 和白藜芦醇值得进一步研究,作为 DHF 和 DENV 相关自身免疫性疾病的潜在治疗候选药物。该方法也可能适用于重新利用药物治疗其他病原体相关的自身免疫性疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅依赖于计算机分析,没有实验或临床验证,限制了证据的强度。计算预测需要体外和体内确认来评估治疗效果和安全性。

Repurposing drugs for treating dengue haemorrhagic fever and associated autoimmune diseases

Authors: Govinda Rao Dabburu, Anjali Garg, Anchal Vishnoi, Neelja Singhal, Manish Kumar
Original abstract (English)
Dengue viruses (DENVs) usually cause a mild febrile illness which might flare up in some patients as dengue haemorrhagic fever (DHF) or dengue shock syndrome. Altered expression of autoimmune markers and development of autoimmune diseases was observed in patients exhibiting prolonged dengue symptoms, suggesting a possible correlation between DENVs and autoimmune diseases. Molecular mimicry of the blood coagulation pathway proteins by DENVs might be a potential factor underlying clinical manifestations of DHF. Inhibition of protein-protein interactions (PPIs) in DENV mimicry proteins and human proteins can potentially treat both DHF and, associated autoimmune diseases. In this study, we have performed a systematic in silico analysis of human proteins interacting with DENV mimicry proteins (HPIDMP) as novel drug targets. Potential inhibitors of the HPIDMP were discerned from the DrugBank database following stringent parameters. The protein-ligand interactions were predicted using molecular docking and evaluated with decoy-based validation, molecular dynamics simulations, and MM-PBSA binding free energy calculations. RAF1 targeting drugs Sorafenib and Regorafenib exhibited the most consistent interaction profile across all computational analyses, followed by the SIRT1-targeting compounds selisistat and resveratrol, which demonstrated moderate but consistent computational support. In contrast, the MYH9-artenimol and HSPE1-phenethyl isothiocyanate systems showed comparatively weaker support. On the basis of our results, we propose Sorafenib and Regorafenib as the most promising candidates for in vitro or clinical studies for treatment of DHF and/or DENV-associated autoimmune diseases, followed by Selisistat and Resveratrol. Thus these DrugBank molecules can be incorporated in clinical studies or in vitro testing for treatment of DHF and/or DENV-associated autoimmune diseases. Also, the methods adopted in this study can guide the repurposing of known drugs to treat other pathogen-associated autoimmune diseases. Graphical abstract Supplementary information The online version contains supplementary material available at 10.1007/s40203-026-00699-0.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue viruses (DENVs) can cause mild febrile illness but may progress to severe dengue, including dengue haemorrhagic fever (DHF) and dengue shock syndrome. Altered expression of autoimmune markers and prolonged dengue symptoms suggest a possible link between DENV infection and autoimmune diseases.
Design and population
The study performed a systematic in silico analysis of human proteins interacting with DENV mimicry proteins (HPIDMP) as novel drug targets. Potential inhibitors were discerned from the DrugBank database using stringent parameters.
Core methods
Protein-ligand interactions were predicted using molecular docking and evaluated with decoy-based validation, molecular dynamics simulations, and MM-PBSA binding free energy calculations.
Main results
RAF1-targeting drugs Sorafenib and Regorafenib exhibited the most consistent interaction profile across all computational analyses. SIRT1-targeting compounds Selisistat and Resveratrol demonstrated moderate but consistent computational support, while MYH9-artenimol and HSPE1-phenethyl isothiocyanate systems showed comparatively weaker support.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors propose Sorafenib and Regorafenib as the most promising candidates for in vitro or clinical studies for treatment of DHF and/or DENV-associated autoimmune diseases, followed by Selisistat and Resveratrol. The study introduces a computational framework to guide drug repurposing for DENV-associated conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that Sorafenib, Regorafenib, Selisistat, and Resveratrol warrant further investigation as potential therapeutic candidates for DHF and DENV-associated autoimmune diseases. The methodological approach may also be applicable to repurposing drugs for other pathogen-associated autoimmune diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies solely on in silico analyses without experimental or clinical validation, limiting the strength of evidence. The computational predictions require in vitro and in vivo confirmation to assess therapeutic efficacy and safety.

使用 SIAN 数据预测登革热住院和 死亡率 模型:开发、时间验证和性能评估的研究方案

Predictive models for hospitalization and mortality in dengue using SINAN data: study protocol for development, temporal validation, and performance evaluation
作者： Felipe Mendes Mendes Delpino, Magalhaes, D., Igor Tona Peres, Tomoe Gusberti, Carlos José de Lima, Fernando A. Bozza, Otavio Ranzani, Leonardo Bastos, Daniela Lima Magalhães, Delpino, F. M. M.
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热继续给卫生保健系统带来沉重的临床和组织负担,特别是在流行期间,大量病例增加了分诊、住院决策以及对严重后果风险较高患者的监测的压力。尽管有关登革热预测的文献越来越多,但许多研究仍然表现出结果的异质性、分析设计不够详细以及缺乏验证。目的:描述报告病例住院结果和住院患者 死亡率 预测模型的开发和验证研究方案。方法:将使用巴西法定疾病信息系统 (SINAN) 的二手数据进行一项回顾性研究,涵盖 2017 年至 2025 年期间。我们将建立两个独立的模型:一个用于预测报告的登革热病例的住院情况,另一个用于预测因登革热住院患者的 死亡率。该协议将遵循 TRIPOD+AI 指南,包括合格预测因子的事先定义、对临床适当决策时间可用变量的限制、缺失数据的处理、回归和机器学习算法之间的比较、内部和时间验证以及歧视、校准和临床效用的评估。结论:该研究旨在建立透明且可重复的分析方案,以支持可应用于登革热临床筛查和 监测 的风险模型的开发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热造成了沉重的临床和组织负担,特别是在流行期间,病例量大,对分类和住院决策造成压力,但现有的预测研究是异质的,缺乏详细的设计和验证。
设计与对象
该研究将回顾性分析巴西法定疾病信息系统 (SINAN) 2017-2025 年的二手数据,以开发登革热病例住院治疗和 死亡率 模型。
核心方法
该协议遵循 TRIPOD+AI 指南,定义合格的预测变量,限制决策时可用的变量,处理缺失数据,比较回归和机器学习算法,并通过评估辨别、校准和临床效用来执行内部和时间验证。
主要结果
将建立两个独立的模型:一个预测报告的登革热病例的住院情况,另一个预测住院登革热患者的 死亡率。
解释与创新
该研究旨在建立透明、可重复的分析方案,以支持登革热的临床筛查和 监测。
科研与公卫意义
通过提供经过验证的风险模型框架,这项工作可以为登革热 暴发 期间的分诊决策和资源分配提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据不报告证据强度的任何限制或评估。

Predictive models for hospitalization and mortality in dengue using SINAN data: study protocol for development, temporal validation, and performance evaluation

Authors: Felipe Mendes Mendes Delpino, Magalhaes, D., Igor Tona Peres, Tomoe Gusberti, Carlos José de Lima, Fernando A. Bozza, Otavio Ranzani, Leonardo Bastos, Daniela Lima Magalhães, Delpino, F. M. M.
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue continues to place a heavy clinical and organizational burden on health care systems, particularly during epidemics, when the high volume of cases adds pressure on triage, decisions regarding hospitalization, and the monitoring of patients at higher risk of severe outcomes. Despite the growing body of literature on dengue prediction, many studies still exhibit heterogeneity in outcomes, insufficiently detailed analytical designs, and a lack of validation. Objective: To describe the protocol for a study on the development and validation of predictive models for the outcomes of hospitalization among reported cases and mortality among hospitalized patients. Methods: A retrospective study will be conducted using secondary data from the Brazilian Notifiable Diseases Information System (SINAN), covering the period from 2017 to 2025. We will build two independent models: one to predict hospitalization among reported dengue cases and another to predict mortality among patients hospitalized for dengue. The protocol will follow TRIPOD+AI guidelines and include prior definition of eligible predictors, restriction to variables available at the clinically appropriate time of decision-making, handling of missing data, comparison between regression and machine learning algorithms, internal and temporal validation, and assessment of discrimination, calibration, and clinical utility. Conclusion: The study aims to establish a transparent and reproducible analytical protocol to support the development of risk models that could be applied to clinical screening and surveillance for dengue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue imposes a heavy clinical and organizational burden, especially during epidemics when high case volumes strain triage and hospitalization decisions, yet existing predictive studies are heterogeneous and lack detailed designs and validation.
Design and population
The study will retrospectively analyze secondary data from Brazil’s Notifiable Diseases Information System (SINAN) covering 2017–2025 to develop models for hospitalization and mortality among dengue cases.
Core methods
The protocol follows TRIPOD+AI guidelines, defining eligible predictors, restricting to variables available at decision time, handling missing data, comparing regression and machine learning algorithms, and performing internal and temporal validation with assessment of discrimination, calibration, and clinical utility.
Main results
Two independent models will be built: one predicting hospitalization among reported dengue cases and another predicting mortality among hospitalized dengue patients.
Interpretation and novelty
The study aims to establish a transparent, reproducible analytical protocol that could support clinical screening and surveillance for dengue.
Scientific and public-health significance
By providing a validated risk model framework, the work could inform triage decisions and resource allocation during dengue outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report any limitations or assessment of evidence strength.

DENV-4感染抑制DNA修复基因的转录

DENV-4 infection suppresses transcription of DNA repair genes
作者： Erica N. Lamkin, Jessica Reich, Josh A. Victor, Madison Guyette, Naveen Kothandaraman, Vihit Gupta, Alfred T. Harding, Tristan X. Jordan, Lee Gehrke, Pei Zhou
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 依赖性 宿主 发病机制 背后的分子机制,特别是 基因组 不稳定性,目前仍不清楚。这种 RNA 病毒在活动性感染期间会导致一种使人衰弱的疾病,并在未来带来登革热综合症、白血病和感染患者血细胞 DNA 损伤的风险,其潜在机制尚不清楚。在这项研究中,我们表明DENV-4感染会诱导显着的DNA损伤,并抑制参与DNA修复和选择诱变跨损伤合成(TLS)聚合酶的基因转录,表明DENV-4依赖性病理学会留下持久的生物“疤痕”,从而逐渐增加慢性疾病风险,包括致癌和登革热后综合征。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 依赖性 宿主 发病机制 背后的分子机制,特别是 基因组 不稳定性,目前仍不清楚。
设计与对象
本研究调查了 DENV-4 感染对感染患者 DNA 修复基因转录和 DNA 损伤的影响。
核心方法
研究表明,DENV-4 感染会诱导显着的 DNA 损伤,并抑制 DNA 修复基因的转录和选择诱变跨损伤合成聚合酶。
主要结果
DENV-4 感染会导致 DNA 损伤并下调 DNA 修复基因转录,导致持久的生物“疤痕”,从而增加慢性疾病风险。
解释与创新
作者解释说,DENV-4 依赖性病理学会产生持久的生物效应,逐渐增加致癌和登革热后综合症等慢性疾病的风险。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于理解 登革病毒 发病机制 并强调潜在的长期健康风险,有助于 监测 和登革热后并发症的预防策略。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究确定了机制,但可能无法完全捕捉所有登革热血清型的长期影响或普遍性。

DENV-4 infection suppresses transcription of DNA repair genes

Authors: Erica N. Lamkin, Jessica Reich, Josh A. Victor, Madison Guyette, Naveen Kothandaraman, Vihit Gupta, Alfred T. Harding, Tristan X. Jordan, Lee Gehrke, Pei Zhou
Original abstract (English)
The molecular mechanisms behind Dengue virus-dependent host pathogenesis, especially genome instability, remain largely unclear. This RNA virus causes a debilitating disease during active infection and presents future risks of postdengue syndromes, leukemia, and DNA damage in the blood cells of infected patients, with the underlying mechanisms unknown. In this study, we show that DENV-4 infection induces significant DNA damage and suppresses the transcription of genes involved in DNA repair and select mutagenic translesion synthesis (TLS) polymerases, indicating that DENV-4-dependent pathobiology leaves durable biological “scars” that incrementally increase chronic disease risk, including carcinogenesis and postdengue syndromes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The molecular mechanisms behind Dengue virus-dependent host pathogenesis, especially genome instability, remain largely unclear.
Design and population
This study investigates the effects of DENV-4 infection on DNA repair gene transcription and DNA damage in infected patients.
Core methods
The study demonstrates that DENV-4 infection induces significant DNA damage and suppresses transcription of DNA repair genes and select mutagenic translesion synthesis polymerases.
Main results
DENV-4 infection causes DNA damage and downregulates DNA repair gene transcription, leading to durable biological 'scars' that increase chronic disease risk.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that DENV-4-dependent pathobiology creates lasting biological effects that incrementally increase risks for chronic diseases like carcinogenesis and postdengue syndromes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform understanding of dengue virus pathogenesis and highlight potential long-term health risks, aiding in surveillance and prevention strategies for postdengue complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as the study identifies mechanisms but may not fully capture the breadth of long-term effects or generalizability across all dengue serotypes.

2023-2024 年巴西圣埃斯皮里图州登革热流行期间 DENV-1 和 DENV-2 共循环的基因组和流行病学特征。

Genomic and Epidemiological Characterization of DENV-1 and DENV-2 Co-Circulation During the 2023-2024 Dengue Epidemic in Espírito Santo, Brazil.
作者： Nicolli Ribeiro de Jesus, Caio Santos de Souza, Mariene R Amorim, João Paulo Cola, Raquel Gomes Catozo, Bruno Luiz M Guedes, Bruna Caetano Pimenta, Julia Sthefany Nunes Zordan, Yasmim Barcellos Madeira Rosa, Henrique Tamanini Silva Moschen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2023年和2024年,巴西圣埃斯皮里图州经历了30年来最大规模的登革热疫情,病例数和死亡人数创历史新高。本研究旨在描述流行期间和流行前时期确诊的登革热病例和 登革病毒 (DENV) 的特征。
设计与对象
使用 eSUS Health 监测 系统(2020-2024 年)的数据和血清样本的分子分析(2023-2024 年)进行了一项基于人群的描述性研究,与横截面基因组采样相结合。
核心方法
获得完整的26个DENV 基因组并结合公开数据进行系统发育重建。
主要结果
2023年,报告了127,070例登革热病例;2.8%出现警告症状或重症登革热,2.6%需要住院治疗,99例导致死亡。2024 年,报告了 131,170 例病例,其中 2.0% 出现警告信号或 重症登革热,2.3% 需要住院治疗,42 人死亡。检测到四个谱系:DENV-1基因型V谱系1V_A和1V_E.1、DENV-2基因型II谱系2II_F.1.1.2和基因型III谱系2III_C.1.1。
解释与创新
我们的研究结果强调了圣埃斯皮里图州 DENV 的复杂动态,并假设多种引入的 DENV 谱系和遗传多样性可能导致了连续几年观察到的 传播 的规模和持续。
科研与公卫意义
流行的 DENV 谱系及其遗传多样性的特征为 监测、暴发 预测以及圣埃斯皮里图州和类似地区的有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供了重要见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究的优势在于它将基于人群的数据与基因组分析相结合,尽管测序的样本量(26 基因组)可能会限制谱系多样性研究结果的普遍性。

Genomic and Epidemiological Characterization of DENV-1 and DENV-2 Co-Circulation During the 2023-2024 Dengue Epidemic in Espírito Santo, Brazil.

Authors: Nicolli Ribeiro de Jesus, Caio Santos de Souza, Mariene R Amorim, João Paulo Cola, Raquel Gomes Catozo, Bruno Luiz M Guedes, Bruna Caetano Pimenta, Julia Sthefany Nunes Zordan, Yasmim Barcellos Madeira Rosa, Henrique Tamanini Silva Moschen
Original abstract (English)
In 2023 and 2024, Espírito Santo state, Brazil, experienced its largest dengue epidemic in 30 years, with record numbers of cases and deaths. This study aimed to characterize confirmed dengue cases and the dengue virus (DENV) during the epidemic and pre-epidemic periods. A population-based descriptive study integrated with cross-sectional genomic sampling was conducted using data from the eSUS Health Surveillance System (2020-2024) and molecular analysis of serum samples (2023-2024). Whole 26 DENV genomes were obtained and combined with public data for phylogenetic reconstruction. In 2023, 127 070 dengue cases were reported; 2.8% showed warning signs or severe dengue, 2.6% required hospitalization, and 99 cases resulted in death. In 2024, 131 170 cases were reported, with 2.0% showing warning signs or severe dengue, 2.3% requiring hospitalization, and 42 fatalities. Four lineages were detected: DENV-1 genotype V lineages 1V_A and 1V_E.1, DENV-2 genotype II lineage 2II_F.1.1.2, and genotype III lineage 2III_C.1.1. These lineages were closely related to viruses from other regions in Brazil and South America. Our findings highlight the complex dynamics of DENV in Espírito Santo state and hypothesize that the multiple introduced DENV lineages and genetic diversity may have contributed to the magnitude and sustained transmission observed over consecutive years.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In 2023 and 2024, Espírito Santo state, Brazil, experienced its largest dengue epidemic in 30 years, with record numbers of cases and deaths. This study aimed to characterize confirmed dengue cases and the dengue virus (DENV) during the epidemic and pre-epidemic periods.
Design and population
A population-based descriptive study integrated with cross-sectional genomic sampling was conducted using data from the eSUS Health Surveillance System (2020-2024) and molecular analysis of serum samples (2023-2024).
Core methods
Whole 26 DENV genomes were obtained and combined with public data for phylogenetic reconstruction.
Main results
In 2023, 127,070 dengue cases were reported; 2.8% showed warning signs or severe dengue, 2.6% required hospitalization, and 99 cases resulted in death. In 2024, 131,170 cases were reported, with 2.0% showing warning signs or severe dengue, 2.3% requiring hospitalization, and 42 fatalities. Four lineages were detected: DENV-1 genotype V lineages 1V_A and 1V_E.1, DENV-2 genotype II lineage 2II_F.1.1.2, and genotype III lineage 2III_C.1.1.
Interpretation and novelty
Our findings highlight the complex dynamics of DENV in Espírito Santo state and hypothesize that the multiple introduced DENV lineages and genetic diversity may have contributed to the magnitude and sustained transmission observed over consecutive years.
Scientific and public-health significance
The characterization of circulating DENV lineages and their genetic diversity provides critical insights for surveillance, outbreak prediction, and targeted public health interventions in Espírito Santo and similar regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's strength lies in its integration of population-based data with genomic analysis, though the sample size for sequencing (26 genomes) may limit the generalizability of lineage diversity findings.

2022年至2025年Warangal Telangana三级护理中心登革病毒感染的流行病学趋势和季节性模式

Epidemiological Trends and Seasonal Patterns of Dengue Virus Infection in a Tertiary Care Centre in Warangal Telangana from 2022 to 2025
作者： Krishna Gudikandula, Sreenivas N, N S
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介:登革病毒 (DENV) 感染是热带和亚热带地区的一个重大公共卫生问题,快速城市化、气候变化和伊蚊 媒介 的扩散导致 发病率 的上升。了解当地的流行病学和季节性模式对于实施有效的 监测 和控制措施至关重要。本研究旨在评估 2022 年至 2025 年四年期间,特伦甘纳邦瓦朗加尔三级护理中心实验室确诊登革热感染的流行病学特征和季节性趋势。材料和方法:使用 2022 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间诊断的实验室确诊登革热病例进行了一项回顾性观察研究。浦那国家病毒学研究所提供了 ELISA 试剂盒,用于检测血清样本中是否存在登革热感染。登革热 IgM 抗体。采用描述性统计分析季节性趋势、年度和每月病例分布以及人口特征。采用卡方检验比较分类变量,统计显着性定义为 p 结果:在研究期间,发现了 3,484 例实验室确诊的登革热病例。发病率的峰值出现在2023年(1,091;31.31%),其次是2024年(956;27.44%)、2022年(723;20.75%)和2025年(714;20.49%),呈现周期性流行趋势。8 月至 11 月出现明显的季节性集中,占所有病例的 73.45%,其中 9 月出现 发病率 月度高峰(923 例;26.49%)(χ2 = 484.40,p 结论:瓦朗加尔的登革热 传播 在儿科人群中表现出显着的负担,并具有明显的季风后季节性模式。加强季风前的意义流行病的周期性和病例集中在 8 月至 11 月期间,强调了 媒介 控制、早期 监测 和医疗机构的准备。通过以儿童为重点的有针对性的干预措施和持续的流行病学监测,可以大大促进登革热相关发病率的降低和区域公共卫生结果的提高。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于城市化、气候变化和伊蚊扩散等因素,登革病毒 感染是热带和亚热带地区日益严重的公共卫生问题。该研究旨在确定实验室确诊的登革热感染的当地流行病学特征和季节性趋势,为监测和控制策略提供信息。
设计与对象
一项回顾性观察研究分析了 2022 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间在特伦甘纳邦瓦朗加尔的三级护理中心诊断的实验室确诊登革热病例。描述性统计用于检查季节性趋势、年度和每月病例分布以及人口特征。
核心方法
使用浦那国家病毒学研究所提供的 ELISA 试剂盒测试血清样本中的登革热 IgM 抗体。采用卡方检验来比较分类变量,统计显着性定义为 p < 0.05。
主要结果
总共发现了 3,484 例实验室确诊的登革热病例,峰值出现在 2023 年(1,091 例;31.31%),其次是 2024 年(956 例;27.44%)、2022 年(723 例;20.75%)和 2025 年(714 例;20.49%)。季节性集中发生在 8 月至 11 月,占所有病例的 73.45%,其中 9 月 发病率 月度最高(923 例;26.49%)。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了瓦朗加尔的周期性流行趋势和明显的季风后季节性模式,给儿科人群带来了巨大负担。该研究强调需要有针对性的干预措施和持续的流行病学监测。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持加强季风前 媒介 控制、早期 监测 和医疗机构的准备工作,以减少登革热相关发病率并改善区域公共卫生结果,特别是通过针对儿童的干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其回顾性设计、依赖于特异性较低的 IgM ELISA 检测以及缺乏血清型或严重程度分层。由于这些限制以及缺乏详细的人口统计或临床结果数据,证据是中等的。

Epidemiological Trends and Seasonal Patterns of Dengue Virus Infection in a Tertiary Care Centre in Warangal Telangana from 2022 to 2025

Authors: Krishna Gudikandula, Sreenivas N, N S
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction: Dengue virus (DENV) infection is a significant public health issue in tropical and subtropical areas, with rising incidence attributed to rapid urbanization, climate change, and the proliferation of Aedes mosquito vectors. Understanding local epidemiological and seasonal patterns is crucial for implementing effective surveillance and control measures. This study sought to assess the epidemiological characteristics and seasonal trends of laboratory-confirmed dengue infections at a tertiary care center in Warangal, Telangana, over a four-year period from 2022 to 2025. Material and Methods: A retrospective observational study was conducted using laboratory-confirmed dengue cases diagnosed between January 2022 and December 2025. The National Institute of Virology, Pune, provided ELISA kits to test serum samples for dengue IgM antibodies. Descriptive statistics were employed to analyze seasonal trends, annual and monthly case distributions, and demographic characteristics. The Chi-square test was employed to compare categorical variables, with statistical significance defined as p Results: During the study period, 3,484 laboratory-confirmed cases of dengue were found. The peak incidence occurred in 2023 (1,091; 31.31%), followed by 2024 (956; 27.44%), 2022 (723; 20.75%), and 2025 (714; 20.49%), indicating a cyclical epidemic trend. A significant seasonal concentration was noted from August to November, comprising 73.45% of all cases, with September exhibiting the peak monthly incidence (923 cases; 26.49%) (χ2 = 484.40, p Conclusion: Dengue transmission in Warangal exhibits a significant burden among pediatric populations and a pronounced post-monsoon seasonal pattern. The significance of enhancing pre-monsoon vector control, early surveillance, and preparedness of healthcare facilities is underscored by the cyclical nature of epidemics and the concentration of cases during August–November. The reduction of dengue-related morbidity and the enhancement of regional public health outcomes can be substantially facilitated by targeted interventions that concentrate on children and continuous epidemiological monitoring.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus infection is a growing public health problem in tropical and subtropical regions due to factors such as urbanization, climate change, and Aedes mosquito proliferation. The study aimed to determine local epidemiological characteristics and seasonal trends of laboratory-confirmed dengue infections to inform surveillance and control strategies.
Design and population
A retrospective observational study analyzed laboratory-confirmed dengue cases diagnosed between January 2022 and December 2025 at a tertiary care center in Warangal, Telangana. Descriptive statistics were used to examine seasonal trends, annual and monthly case distributions, and demographic characteristics.
Core methods
Serum samples were tested for dengue IgM antibodies using ELISA kits provided by the National Institute of Virology, Pune. The Chi-square test was employed to compare categorical variables, with statistical significance defined as p < 0.05.
Main results
A total of 3,484 laboratory-confirmed dengue cases were identified, with peak incidence in 2023 (1,091 cases; 31.31%), followed by 2024 (956 cases; 27.44%), 2022 (723 cases; 20.75%), and 2025 (714 cases; 20.49%). A significant seasonal concentration occurred from August to November, comprising 73.45% of all cases, with September having the highest monthly incidence (923 cases; 26.49%).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight a cyclical epidemic trend and a pronounced post-monsoon seasonal pattern in Warangal, with a significant burden among pediatric populations. The study underscores the need for targeted interventions and continuous epidemiological monitoring.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results support enhancing pre-monsoon vector control, early surveillance, and healthcare facility preparedness to reduce dengue-related morbidity and improve regional public health outcomes, particularly through interventions focused on children.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, reliance on IgM ELISA testing which may have lower specificity, and lack of serotype or severity stratification. The evidence is moderate due to these constraints and the absence of detailed demographic or clinical outcome data.

种群瓶颈和时间限制影响埃及伊蚊中肠的 登革病毒 感染

Population bottlenecks and temporal constraints shape dengue virus infection of Aedes aegypti midguts
作者： Godfrey Nattoh, Philip M. Armstrong, Doug E. Brackney
摘要中文翻译节肢动物传播的病毒(虫媒病毒),例如 登革病毒,对全世界人类健康构成重大且日益严重的威胁。虫媒病毒维持在节肢动物 媒介 和脊椎动物 宿主 之间的 传播 循环中,在 传播 期间经历强大的瓶颈,这可以极大地改变病毒种群的组成和适应度。在媒介中,严重的群体瓶颈发生在感染的初始部位——中肠,但驱动这一过程的具体因素仍不清楚。为了研究这些机制,我们需要更好地了解早期感染事件;然而,传统的检测方法缺乏必要的灵敏度。基于分子信号放大的方法的最新进展现在使得详细研究这些早期阶段成为可能。在这项研究中,我们检查了伊蚊的早期 登革病毒 2 (DENV-2) 感染。使用多重杂交链式反应技术的埃及伊蚊中肠。我们证明这些技术比传统的免疫荧光测定灵敏得多,并且最早可以在感染后 6 小时可靠地检测蚊子中肠中的 DENV-2。此外,我们观察到了明显的瓶颈,因为只有少数病毒粒子引发中肠感染。信号放大策略的应用现在可以对控制早期感染事件的细胞和分子相互作用进行严格评估,这可以为新型干预措施的开发提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
节肢动物传播的病毒(虫媒病毒),例如 登革病毒,对全世界人类健康构成重大且日益严重的威胁。传播 期间导致人口瓶颈的具体因素仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究检查了伊蚊的早期 登革病毒 2 (DENV-2) 感染。使用多重杂交链式反应技术的埃及伊蚊中肠。旨在研究传播期间人口瓶颈的机制。
核心方法
该研究采用基于分子信号放大的方法,特别是多重杂交链式反应技术,最早在感染后 6 小时内检测蚊子中肠中的 DENV-2。这些技术被证明比传统的免疫荧光测定更灵敏。
主要结果
该研究观察到了明显的瓶颈,只有少数病毒粒子引发中肠感染。
解释与创新
信号放大策略的应用可以对控制早期感染事件的细胞和分子相互作用进行严格评估,这可以为新型干预措施的开发提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为了解蚊子 媒介 感染 登革病毒 的早期阶段提供了见解,这可能有助于开发更有效的登革热疾病预防干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于检查伊蚊的早期感染事件。埃及伊蚊中肠使用特定的分子技术,证据仅基于抽象数据。研究结果的可信度为中等。

Population bottlenecks and temporal constraints shape dengue virus infection of Aedes aegypti midguts

Authors: Godfrey Nattoh, Philip M. Armstrong, Doug E. Brackney
Original abstract (English)
Arthropod-borne viruses (arboviruses) such as dengue virus pose a significant and growing threat to human health worldwide. Maintained in a transmission cycle between arthropod vectors and vertebrate hosts, arboviruses experience strong bottlenecks during transmission which can drastically alter virus population composition and fitness. In vectors, severe population bottlenecks occur at the initial site of infection, the midgut, yet the specific factors driving this process remain unclear. To investigate these mechanisms, we need a better understanding of early infection events; however, traditional detection methods lack the necessary sensitivity. Recent advances in molecular signal amplification-based methods now make it possible to study these early stages in detail. In this study, we examined early dengue virus 2 (DENV-2) infection of Ae. aegypti midguts using multiple hybridization chain reaction techniques. We demonstrate that these techniques are much more sensitive than the traditional immunofluorescence assay and can reliably detect DENV-2 in mosquito midguts as early as 6 hours post infection. Further, we observed significant bottlenecks as only a handful of virions initiate infection of the midgut. The application of signal amplification strategies now enables critical assessment of the cellular and molecular interactions governing early infection events which could inform the development of novel interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Arthropod-borne viruses (arboviruses) such as dengue virus pose a significant and growing threat to human health worldwide. The specific factors driving population bottlenecks during transmission remain unclear.
Design and population
The study examined early dengue virus 2 (DENV-2) infection of Ae. aegypti midguts using multiple hybridization chain reaction techniques. It aimed to investigate the mechanisms of population bottlenecks during transmission.
Core methods
The study employed molecular signal amplification-based methods, specifically multiple hybridization chain reaction techniques, to detect DENV-2 in mosquito midguts as early as 6 hours post infection. These techniques were demonstrated to be more sensitive than traditional immunofluorescence assays.
Main results
The study observed significant bottlenecks, with only a handful of virions initiating infection of the midgut.
Interpretation and novelty
The application of signal amplification strategies enables critical assessment of the cellular and molecular interactions governing early infection events, which could inform the development of novel interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the early stages of dengue virus infection in mosquito vectors, which could contribute to the development of more effective interventions for dengue disease prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to examining early infection events in Ae. aegypti midguts using specific molecular techniques, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data. The confidence in the findings is moderate.

缺乏 N153 糖基化的 登革病毒 2 通过中和抗体表现出增强的识别能力

Dengue virus 2 lacking N153 glycosylation displayed enhanced recognition by neutralizing antibodies
作者： Guntur Fibriansah, Thiam-Seng Ng, Xin-Ni Lim, Aaron W. K. Tan, Donald Heng Rong Ting, Gavin R. Screaton, James E. Crowe, Sylvie Alonso, Shee-Mei Lok, Heng Rong Donald Ting
摘要中文翻译摘要 去糖基化 (N153Q) 登革病毒 (DENV) 突变体在小鼠模型中显示出感染减弱,这主要是由于其抗体敏感性增加。这与中和测定一致,中和测定显示人单克隆抗体 2D22 和 C10 对突变体比野生型 (WT) 病毒更有效。在这里,我们比较了 WT 和与这些 Fab 复合的突变病毒的冷冻电镜结构(2.7-3.2 Å 分辨率)。我们观察到突变病毒上两个 Fab 的占有率增加,这表明先前被糖基化阻断的表位的可及性更高。使用生物层干涉测量法,我们发现虽然 Fab 与突变体的结合速率比 WT 病毒慢,但它们的解离速率也较慢。缓慢的解离速率可能会导致 Fab 占据率较高,因为一旦结合,它们仍然与病毒相关。N153Q 突变体可能是一个很好的 疫苗 候选者,因为重要的表位更容易刺激高效抗体。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究调查了登革病毒 2 N153位点的糖基化如何影响抗体识别和病毒感染性,旨在了解增强中和敏感性的结构基础。
设计与对象
该研究在小鼠模型中使用结构和生物物理测定将野生型 登革病毒 2 与去糖基化的 N153Q 突变体进行比较。
核心方法
以 2.7-3.2 Å 分辨率测定与单克隆抗体 2D22 和 C10 复合的野生型和突变病毒的冷冻电镜结构,并使用生物层干涉测量法分析结合动力学。
主要结果
尽管结合率较慢,但 N153Q 突变体由于更容易接近的表位而表现出抗体占有率增加,并且通过人单克隆抗体增强的中和作用,在小鼠中表现出减弱的感染。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,N153 的糖基化掩盖了关键表位,其去除提高了抗体结合稳定性,有可能使该突变体成为可行的 疫苗 候选者。
科研与公卫意义
N153Q 突变体可以作为 疫苗 候选者,通过暴露重要的表位进行有效的抗体刺激,从而有可能改善登革热 疫苗 设计。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于小鼠模型的结构和生物物理分析,由于直接实验观察而具有很高的可信度,但仅限于体外和动物数据。

Dengue virus 2 lacking N153 glycosylation displayed enhanced recognition by neutralizing antibodies

Authors: Guntur Fibriansah, Thiam-Seng Ng, Xin-Ni Lim, Aaron W. K. Tan, Donald Heng Rong Ting, Gavin R. Screaton, James E. Crowe, Sylvie Alonso, Shee-Mei Lok, Heng Rong Donald Ting
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Deglycosylated (N153Q) dengue virus (DENV) mutant shows attenuated infection in a mouse model, mainly due to its increased antibody susceptibility. This is consistent with the neutralization assay showing human monoclonal antibodies 2D22 and C10 are more potent towards the mutant than the wild-type (WT) virus. Here, we compared the cryoEM structures of WT and mutant viruses complexed with these Fabs (2.7-3.2 Å resolution). We observed increased occupancies for both Fabs on the mutant virus, suggesting higher accessibility of epitopes that were previously blocked by glycosylation. Using biolayer interferometry, we showed although the Fabs have slower binding rate to the mutant than WT virus, they also have slower dissociation rate. The slow dissociation rate might contribute to higher Fab occupancies, as once bound, they remain associated with the virus. N153Q mutant might be a good vaccine candidate, as important epitopes are made more accessible for stimulating highly potent antibodies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study investigated how glycosylation at position N153 of dengue virus 2 affects antibody recognition and viral infectivity, aiming to understand the structural basis for enhanced neutralization susceptibility.
Design and population
The research compared wild-type dengue virus 2 with a deglycosylated N153Q mutant using structural and biophysical assays in a mouse model.
Core methods
CryoEM structures of wild-type and mutant viruses complexed with monoclonal antibodies 2D22 and C10 were determined at 2.7-3.2 Å resolution, and biolayer interferometry was used to analyze binding kinetics.
Main results
The N153Q mutant showed increased antibody occupancies due to more accessible epitopes, despite slower binding rates, and exhibited attenuated infection in mice with enhanced neutralization by human monoclonal antibodies.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that glycosylation at N153 masks key epitopes, and its removal improves antibody binding stability, potentially making the mutant a viable vaccine candidate.
Scientific and public-health significance
The N153Q mutant could serve as a vaccine candidate by exposing important epitopes for potent antibody stimulation, potentially improving dengue vaccine design.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on structural and biophysical analyses in a mouse model, with high confidence due to direct experimental observations but limited to in vitro and animal data.

儿科登革热的抗体反应表明缅甸 DENV-3 基因型 I 和 III 的出现和共同循环以及正黄病毒交叉反应。

Antibody Responses in Pediatric Dengue Indicate Emergence and Co-Circulation of DENV-3 Genotypes I and III in Myanmar and Orthoflavivirus Cross-Reactivity.
作者： Jean Claude Balingit, Khine Mya Nwe, Htin Lin, Wah Wah Aung, Ryosuke Suzuki, Yuki Takamatsu, Takeshi Urano, Aye Aye Khin, Kyaw Zin Thant, Hlaing Myat Thu
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 (DENV) 仍然是东南亚的一个主要公共卫生问题,对儿童的影响尤为严重。在缅甸,DENV-3 基因型 III 在 2017 年之前占主导地位,此后出现了基因型 I。为了评估这种现象对抗体反应的影响,我们分析了 2017 年至 2019 年间收集的 38 名儿童登革热患者的配对急性和恢复期血清样本以及 2004 年存档的样本。针对 DENV-3 基因型 I 和 III 以及其他血清型评估中和作用和体外 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE),并对寨卡病毒和日本脑炎病毒进行交叉反应分析。2004 年的样本显示,基因型 I 的中和作用减少,ADE 较高,这与当时不存在的情况一致。相比之下,2017-2019 年的样本对两种基因型表现出相当的中和作用,具有基因型和年份依赖性的 ADE 差异,表明基因型 I 的出现与不断发展的功能性抗体谱相关。因此,为 DENV-3 基因型 I 和 III 在缅甸持续共循环提供了免疫学证据。未来对循环正黄病毒及其基因型进行连续 监测 的前瞻性儿科 队列研究 研究对于阐明流行地区预先存在的登革热免疫如何影响基因型出现和交叉正黄病毒免疫至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 仍然是东南亚的一个主要公共卫生问题,对儿童的影响尤其严重,但 DENV-3 基因型 I 的出现以及基因型 III 在缅甸的持续传播所产生的免疫影响尚不清楚。
设计与对象
为了评估 DENV-3 基因型出现的免疫学影响,对 2017 年至 2019 年间收集的 38 名儿科登革热患者的配对急性和恢复期血清样本与 2004 年存档样本进行了分析。
核心方法
针对 DENV-3 基因型 I 和 III 以及其他登革热血清型测量了中和作用和体外 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE),并对寨卡病毒和日本脑炎病毒进行了额外的交叉反应分析。
主要结果
2004 年的样本显示,基因型 I 的中和作用降低,而 ADE 较高,而 2017-2019 年的样本,两种基因型的中和作用相当,具有基因型和年份依赖性的 ADE 差异。
解释与创新
这些发现为缅甸 DENV-3 基因型 I 和 III 的持续共循环提供了免疫学证据,表明基因型 I 的出现与儿科患者功能性抗体谱的演变有关。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果强调需要对流行的正黄病毒及其基因型进行持续的 监测,以了解预先存在的登革热免疫如何影响流行地区的基因型出现和交叉正黄病毒免疫。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和对存档样本的依赖的限制,这些样本可能无法完全捕捉人群水平的免疫动态,因此需要谨慎地将证据强度解释为中等。

Antibody Responses in Pediatric Dengue Indicate Emergence and Co-Circulation of DENV-3 Genotypes I and III in Myanmar and Orthoflavivirus Cross-Reactivity.

Authors: Jean Claude Balingit, Khine Mya Nwe, Htin Lin, Wah Wah Aung, Ryosuke Suzuki, Yuki Takamatsu, Takeshi Urano, Aye Aye Khin, Kyaw Zin Thant, Hlaing Myat Thu
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) remains a major public health concern in Southeast Asia, disproportionately affecting children. In Myanmar, DENV-3 Genotype III predominated before 2017, with Genotype I emerging thereafter. To assess the impact of this emergence on antibody responses, we analyzed paired acute and convalescent serum samples from 38 pediatric dengue patients collected between 2017 and 2019 alongside archived 2004 samples. Neutralization and in vitro antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE) were assessed against DENV-3 Genotypes I and III and other serotypes, with cross-reactivity analysis to Zika and Japanese encephalitis viruses. Samples from 2004 showed reduced neutralization and higher ADE for Genotype I, consistent with its absence at the time. In contrast, 2017-2019 samples exhibited comparable neutralization for both genotypes, with genotype- and year-dependent ADE differences, indicating that Genotype I emergence was associated with evolving functional antibody profiles. Hence, providing immunological evidence for continued co-circulation of DENV-3 Genotypes I and III in Myanmar. Future prospective pediatric cohort studies with continuous surveillance of circulating orthoflaviviruses and their genotypes, are essential to elucidate how pre-existing dengue immunity shapes genotype emergence and cross-orthoflavivirus immunity in endemic regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus remains a major public health concern in Southeast Asia, disproportionately affecting children, yet the immunological impact of the emergence of DENV-3 Genotype I alongside continued circulation of Genotype III in Myanmar was not well understood.
Design and population
To assess the immunological impact of DENV-3 genotype emergence, paired acute and convalescent serum samples from 38 pediatric dengue patients collected between 2017 and 2019 were analyzed alongside archived samples from 2004.
Core methods
Neutralization and in vitro antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE) were measured against DENV-3 Genotypes I and III and other dengue serotypes, with additional cross-reactivity analysis to Zika and Japanese encephalitis viruses.
Main results
Samples from 2004 showed reduced neutralization and higher ADE for Genotype I, whereas 2017–2019 samples exhibited comparable neutralization for both genotypes, with genotype- and year-dependent ADE differences.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide immunological evidence for continued co-circulation of DENV-3 Genotypes I and III in Myanmar, indicating that the emergence of Genotype I was associated with evolving functional antibody profiles in pediatric patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight the need for ongoing surveillance of circulating orthoflaviviruses and their genotypes to understand how pre-existing dengue immunity influences genotype emergence and cross-orthoflavivirus immunity in endemic regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and reliance on archived samples, which may not fully capture population-level immunological dynamics, warranting cautious interpretation of the evidence strength as moderate.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

在印度尼西亚,嵌合黄热病四价登革热 疫苗 (CYD-TDV) 和登革热抗体与 SARS-CoV-2 交叉反应 10 年后中和登革热抗体。

Neutralizing Dengue Antibodies after 10 years of the Chimeric Yellow Fever Tetravalent Dengue Vaccine (CYD-TDV) and Dengue Antibody Cross-reactivity with SARS-CoV-2 in Indonesia.
作者： Mulya Rahma Karyanti, Hindra Irawan Satari, Sri Rezeki Hadinegoro, Anggraini Alam, Indah Suci Widyahening, Zakiudin Munasir, R Tedjo Sasmono
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热流行国家受到了 COVID-19 传播的影响。2011年,印度尼西亚参与了嵌合黄热病四价登革热疫苗(CYD-TDV)的多中心III期临床试验。本研究评估了 COVID-19 大流行期间 10 年后 CYD-TDV 的中和登革热抗体。目的:评估印度尼西亚免疫 10 年后接受 CYD-TDV 的个体针对所有四种 登革病毒 (DENV) 血清型和登革热 发病率 的中和抗体。此外,还比较了登革热疫苗接种组和未接种疫苗组之间的 SARS-CoV-2 中和抗体滴度和 COVID-19 发病率。方法:这项研究在雅加达的五个医疗中心进行(2022 年 6 月至 12 月),涉及 419 名参与者(207 名接种了登革热疫苗,212 名未接种疫苗)。一项横断面研究对 79 名接种登革热疫苗和 80 名未接种疫苗的参与者分别测量了针对 DENV 和 SARS-CoV-2 的中和抗体。同时,所有参与者都收到了调查问卷,以评估登革热和 COVID-19 病史。结果:在青少年(12-17 岁)中,CYD-TDV 接受者针对 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 DENV-4 的中和抗体滴度显着较高,但针对 DENV-3 的中和抗体滴度则没有。然而,两组成年人(≥18 岁)没有观察到显着差异。登革热疫苗接种组 (4.8%) 和未接种疫苗组 (5.1%) 之间的登革热 发病率 相似。SARS-CoV-2 中和抗体水平在各组之间没有显着差异。然而,在登革热疫苗接种组中,住院的 COVID-19 的 发病率 较高。结论:十年后,CYD-TDV 在青少年中显着诱导较高的 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 DENV-4 中和抗体水平,但对 DENV-3 没有显着影响,但在成人中则不然。各组之间的 SARS-CoV-2 中和抗体水平相当,但接种登革热疫苗组的 COVID-19 住院率更高。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热流行国家受到了 COVID-19 传播的影响,本研究评估了 COVID-19 大流行期间 10 年后的 CYD-TDV 中和登革热抗体。
设计与对象
这项研究在雅加达的五个医疗中心进行(2022 年 6 月至 12 月),涉及 419 名参与者(207 名接种登革热疫苗的参与者,212 名未接种疫苗的参与者),其中横截面子集包括 79 名接种登革热疫苗的参与者和 80 名未接种疫苗的参与者。
核心方法
一项横断面研究测量了针对 DENV 和 SARS-CoV-2 的中和抗体,同时向所有参与者提供了调查问卷以评估登革热和 COVID-19 病史。
主要结果
在青少年(12-17岁)中,CYD-TDV接受者针对DENV-1、DENV-2和DENV-4的中和抗体滴度明显较高,但针对DENV-3的中和抗体滴度则没有;成人(≥18 岁)中未观察到显着差异。各组之间的登革热 发病率 相似(4.8% vs 5.1%),并且 SARS-CoV-2 中和抗体水平具有可比性,但在登革热疫苗接种组中,COVID-19 住院治疗的频率更高。
解释与创新
十年后,CYD-TDV 在青少年中显着诱导更高的 DENV-1、DENV-2 和 DENV-4 中和抗体水平,但对 DENV-3 没有显着影响,但在成人中则不然,与 SARS-CoV-2 中和抗体水平相当,但在接种疫苗的个体中,COVID-19 住院率更高。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为登革热 疫苗 的长期疗效以及与 SARS-CoV-2 的潜在交叉反应性提供了信息,强调了在疫苗接种人群中继续使用 监测 来治疗登革热和 COVID-19 结果的必要性。
局限与证据强度
尽管大样本量和中和抗体的直接测量提供了中等的证据强度,但该研究仍受到其横截面设计和潜在混杂因素的限制。

Neutralizing Dengue Antibodies after 10 years of the Chimeric Yellow Fever Tetravalent Dengue Vaccine (CYD-TDV) and Dengue Antibody Cross-reactivity with SARS-CoV-2 in Indonesia.

Authors: Mulya Rahma Karyanti, Hindra Irawan Satari, Sri Rezeki Hadinegoro, Anggraini Alam, Indah Suci Widyahening, Zakiudin Munasir, R Tedjo Sasmono
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue endemic countries were affected by the spread of COVID-19. In 2011, Indonesia participated in a multi-center phase III clinical trial of chimeric yellow fever tetravalent dengue vaccine (CYD-TDV). This study evaluates neutralizing dengue antibodies of CYD-TDV after 10 years during COVID-19 pandemic. OBJECTIVE: To assess neutralizing antibody against all four-dengue virus (DENV) serotypes and dengue incidence in individuals who received CYD-TDV after 10 years immunization in Indonesia. Additionally, to compare SARS-CoV-2 neutralizing antibody titers and COVID-19 incidence between dengue-vaccinated and non-vaccinated groups. METHODS: This study was conducted across five healthcare centers in Jakarta (June-December 2022) involving 419 participants (207 dengue-vaccinated, 212 non-vaccinated). A cross-sectional study in subset of 79 dengue-vaccinated and 80 non-vaccinated participants were measured neutralizing antibodies against DENV and SARS-CoV-2, respectively. Simultaneously, all participants were given questionnaires to assess dengue and COVID-19 history. RESULTS: In adolescents (12-17 years), CYD-TDV recipients had significantly higher neutralizing antibody titers against DENV-1, DENV-2, and DENV-4, but not DENV-3. While, no significant differences were observed in adults (≥18 years) in both groups. Dengue incidence was similar between dengue-vaccinated (4.8%) and non-vaccinated (5.1%) groups. The SARS-CoV-2 neutralizing antibody levels were not significantly different between groups. However, Incidence of hospitalized COVID-19 were higher in the dengue-vaccinated group. CONCLUSION: After a decade, CYD-TDV induced higher neutralizing antibody levels significantly for DENV-1, DENV-2, and DENV-4, but not for DENV-3 in adolescents but not in adults. The SARS-CoV-2 neutralizing antibody levels were comparable between groups, However, COVID-19 hospitalizations were more frequent in dengue-vaccinated group.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue endemic countries were affected by the spread of COVID-19, and this study evaluates neutralizing dengue antibodies of CYD-TDV after 10 years during the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
This study was conducted across five healthcare centers in Jakarta (June-December 2022) involving 419 participants (207 dengue-vaccinated, 212 non-vaccinated), with a cross-sectional subset of 79 dengue-vaccinated and 80 non-vaccinated participants.
Core methods
A cross-sectional study measured neutralizing antibodies against DENV and SARS-CoV-2, while all participants were given questionnaires to assess dengue and COVID-19 history.
Main results
In adolescents (12-17 years), CYD-TDV recipients had significantly higher neutralizing antibody titers against DENV-1, DENV-2, and DENV-4, but not DENV-3; no significant differences were observed in adults (≥18 years). Dengue incidence was similar between groups (4.8% vs 5.1%), and SARS-CoV-2 neutralizing antibody levels were comparable, but COVID-19 hospitalizations were more frequent in the dengue-vaccinated group.
Interpretation and novelty
After a decade, CYD-TDV induced higher neutralizing antibody levels significantly for DENV-1, DENV-2, and DENV-4, but not for DENV-3 in adolescents but not in adults, with comparable SARS-CoV-2 neutralizing antibody levels but higher COVID-19 hospitalizations in vaccinated individuals.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform long-term dengue vaccine efficacy and potential cross-reactivity with SARS-CoV-2, highlighting the need for continued surveillance of both dengue and COVID-19 outcomes in vaccinated populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and potential confounding factors, though the large sample size and direct measurement of neutralizing antibodies provide moderate evidence strength.

登革热 中的血清学和免疫学标记:特立尼达和多巴哥基于 ELISA 的回顾性初步研究

Serological and Immunological Markers in Dengue Fever: A Retrospective ELISA-Based Pilot Study from Trinidad and Tobago
作者： Angel A Justiz-Vaillant, Sachin Soodeen
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热 仍然是热带和亚热带地区主要的蚊媒病毒性疾病,并继续构成加勒比地区的重要公共卫生挑战。尽管 传播 在特立尼达和多巴哥流行,但有限的实验室研究评估了与近期登革热感染相关的血清学标志物。目的:通过使用酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 检测特立尼达和多巴哥血清样本中的抗 登革病毒 免疫球蛋白 M (IgM) 抗体,评估与近期 登革病毒 感染相关的血清学和免疫学标志物。方法:使用商业酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 检测抗 登革病毒 IgM 抗体。根据制造商推荐的标准,结果分为阳性、临界或阴性。进行描述性统计分析以确定匿名血清样本中的 血清流行率。结果:总共分析了 161 份血清样本。20 个样本 (12.4%) 表现出阳性抗登革热 IgM 反应性,29 个样本 (18.0%) 表现出边缘反应性,112 个样本 (69.6%) 呈阴性。根据保守分析方法,估计登革热 IgM 血清流行率 为 12.4%(20/161;95% CI:7.7-18.6%)。结论:本初步研究中登革热 IgM 的估计 血清流行率 高度依赖于边界 ELISA 结果的分析 治疗。保守分类提供了对近期登革热感染最谨慎和可重复的估计,而自由主义解释则大大增加了明显的疾病负担。早期病毒复制会引发炎症,进而发展为细胞因子驱动的血管损伤,而交叉反应性体液反应和 宿主 因素最终会影响登革热的严重程度。最终,了解 IgM 交叉反应性的驱动因素对于准确的登革热 监测、暴发 检测和临床决策至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 是加勒比地区的一项重大公共卫生挑战,但特立尼达和多巴哥缺乏评估近期感染血清学标志物的实验室研究。该研究旨在通过评估与近期 登革病毒 感染相关的血清学和免疫学标记物来解决这一差距。
设计与对象
一项回顾性试点研究使用 ELISA 分析了来自特立尼达和多巴哥的匿名血清样本,以检测抗 登革病毒 IgM 抗体。分析中总共包括 161 份血清样本。
核心方法
使用商业 ELISA 检测抗 登革病毒 IgM 抗体,根据制造商标准将结果分类为阳性、临界或阴性。进行描述性统计分析以确定血清流行率。
主要结果
20 个样本 (12.4%) 抗登革热 IgM 呈阳性,29 个样本 (18.0%) 呈临界值,112 个样本 (69.6%) 呈阴性。在保守分类下,估计的登革热 IgM 血清流行率 为 12.4%(20/161;95% CI:7.7-18.6%)。
解释与创新
估计的 血清流行率 高度依赖于边界 ELISA 结果的分析 治疗,保守分类提供了对近期登革热感染的最谨慎的估计。
科研与公卫意义
了解 IgM 交叉反应驱动因素对于登革热 监测、暴发 的准确检测和流行环境中的临床决策至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量较小,并且依赖 ELISA,没有进行验证性测试,限制了普遍性和精确度,因此评估的置信度较低。

Serological and Immunological Markers in Dengue Fever: A Retrospective ELISA-Based Pilot Study from Trinidad and Tobago

Authors: Angel A Justiz-Vaillant, Sachin Soodeen
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue fever remains a major mosquito-borne viral disease in tropical and subtropical regions and continues to represent an important public health challenge in the Caribbean. Despite endemic transmission in Trinidad and Tobago, limited laboratory-based studies have evaluated serological markers associated with recent dengue infection. Objective: To evaluate serological and immunological markers associated with recent dengue virus infection through the detection of anti-dengue virus immunoglobulin M (IgM) antibodies in serum samples obtained from Trinidad and Tobago using an enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA). Methods: Anti-dengue virus IgM antibodies were detected using a commercial enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA). Results were classified as positive, borderline or negative according to manufacturer-recommended criteria. Descriptive statistical analysis was performed to determine seroprevalence in anonymized serum samples. Results: A total of 161 serum samples were analyzed. Twenty samples (12.4%) demonstrated positive anti-dengue IgM reactivity, while 29 samples (18.0%) showed borderline reactivity and 112 samples (69.6%) were negative. Under the conservative analytical approach, the estimated dengue IgM seroprevalence was 12.4% (20/161; 95% CI: 7.7–18.6%). Conclusions: The estimated seroprevalence of dengue IgM in this pilot study was highly dependent on the analytical treatment of borderline ELISA results. Conservative classification provided the most cautious and reproducible estimate of recent dengue infection, while liberal interpretation substantially increased the apparent disease burden. Early viral replication triggers inflammation that progresses to cytokine-driven vascular damage, while cross-reactive humoral responses and host factors ultimately shape dengue severity. Ultimately, understanding the drivers of IgM cross-reactivity is essential for accurate dengue surveillance, outbreak detection, and clinical decision-making.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is a major public health challenge in the Caribbean, yet Trinidad and Tobago lack laboratory-based studies evaluating serological markers of recent infection. The study aimed to address this gap by assessing serological and immunological markers linked to recent dengue virus infection.
Design and population
A retrospective pilot study analyzed anonymized serum samples from Trinidad and Tobago using ELISA to detect anti-dengue virus IgM antibodies. A total of 161 serum samples were included in the analysis.
Core methods
Anti-dengue virus IgM antibodies were detected using a commercial ELISA, with results classified as positive, borderline, or negative per manufacturer criteria. Descriptive statistical analysis was performed to determine seroprevalence.
Main results
Twenty samples (12.4%) were positive for anti-dengue IgM, 29 (18.0%) were borderline, and 112 (69.6%) were negative. Under conservative classification, the estimated dengue IgM seroprevalence was 12.4% (20/161; 95% CI: 7.7–18.6%).
Interpretation and novelty
The estimated seroprevalence was highly dependent on the analytical treatment of borderline ELISA results, with conservative classification providing the most cautious estimate of recent dengue infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
Understanding IgM cross-reactivity drivers is essential for accurate dengue surveillance, outbreak detection, and clinical decision-making in endemic settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s small sample size and reliance on ELISA without confirmatory testing limit generalizability and precision, warranting a low confidence assessment.

沃尔巴克氏体介导的登革热控制在埃及伊蚊的杀虫剂胁迫下仍然有效

Wolbachia-mediated dengue control remains robust under insecticide stress in Aedes aegypti
作者： Benjamin DUPUIS, Laurence MOUSSON, Marie VAZEILLE, Chloé BOHERS, Sosiasi KILAMA, Nicolas POCQUET, Anna-Bella Failloux
摘要中文翻译摘要 基于沃尔巴克氏体的干预措施已成为减少埃及伊蚊 登革病毒 (DENV) 传播 的成功生物学策略。然而,这种共生体赋予的保护表型在环境压力下是否保持稳定仍知之甚少。在这里,我们研究了亚致死杀虫剂(溴氰菊酯)暴露对沃尔巴克氏体-埃及伊蚊-DENV系统多个组成部分的影响,包括病原体阻断、细胞质不相容性、宿主繁殖性状、共生体密度和病毒多样性。我们发现,接触杀虫剂会导致沃尔巴克氏体密度短暂降低,但会迅速恢复,且不会影响 DENV 阻断、细胞质不相容或繁殖性能。此外,接触杀虫剂对蚊子唾液中病毒遗传多样性的影响可以忽略不计。这些发现证明了沃尔巴克氏体介导的病原体阻断在化学胁迫下的稳健性,并提供了基于共生体的生物控制在环境挑战条件下仍然有效的证据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决有关环境压力下沃尔巴克氏体介导的登革热控制稳定性的知识差距,因为这种生物策略在现实条件下的有效性仍然知之甚少。研究问题集中在当暴露于亚致死杀虫剂水平时,沃尔巴克氏体所赋予的保护表型是否持续存在。
设计与对象
本研究调查了亚致死溴氰菊酯暴露对沃尔巴克氏体-埃及伊蚊-DENV系统的影响,包括病原体阻断、细胞质不相容性、宿主繁殖性状、共生体密度和病毒多样性。该研究是在受控实验室环境中使用感染沃尔巴克氏体和登革热病毒的埃及伊蚊进行的。
核心方法
研究人员将埃及伊蚊暴露于亚致死浓度的溴氰菊酯中,并评估了沃尔巴克氏体-DENV系统的各个组成部分,包括共生体密度、病原体阻断效率、细胞质不相容性、繁殖性能和蚊子唾液中的病毒遗传多样性。
主要结果
接触杀虫剂导致沃尔巴克氏体密度短暂降低,但随后又迅速恢复。DENV 阻断、细胞质不相容性和繁殖性能并未受到杀虫剂胁迫的影响。此外,蚊子唾液中的病毒遗传多样性并未受到影响。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,沃尔巴克氏体介导的登革热控制在化学胁迫下仍然有效,凸显了基于共生体的生物控制策略在环境挑战条件下有效发挥作用的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为公共卫生策略提供了重要见解,表明即使蚊子接触杀虫剂,基于沃尔巴克氏体的干预措施也能保持其功效,这对于可持续登革热控制计划至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在受控实验室环境中进行的,这可能会限制其对现实条件的推广。然而,证据确凿,直接支持沃尔巴克氏体介导的登革热控制在杀虫剂胁迫下的稳健性。

Wolbachia-mediated dengue control remains robust under insecticide stress in Aedes aegypti

Authors: Benjamin DUPUIS, Laurence MOUSSON, Marie VAZEILLE, Chloé BOHERS, Sosiasi KILAMA, Nicolas POCQUET, Anna-Bella Failloux
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Wolbachia -based interventions have become a successful biological strategy to reduce dengue virus (DENV) transmission by Aedes aegypti . However, whether the protective phenotypes conferred by this symbiont remain stable under environmental stress remains poorly understood. Here, we investigated the impact of sublethal insecticide (deltamethrin) exposure on multiple components of the Wolbachia – Aedes aegypti –DENV system, including pathogen blocking, cytoplasmic incompatibility, host reproductive traits, symbiont density, and viral diversity. We show that insecticide exposure induces a transient reduction in Wolbachia density, which rapidly recovers without compromising DENV blocking, cytoplasmic incompatibility, or reproductive performance. In addition, insecticide exposure has a negligible impact on viral genetic diversity in mosquito saliva. These findings demonstrate the robustness of Wolbachia -mediated pathogen blocking under chemical stress and provide evidence that symbiont-based biological control can remain effective under environmentally challenging conditions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the knowledge gap regarding the stability of Wolbachia-mediated dengue control under environmental stress, as the effectiveness of this biological strategy in real-world conditions remains poorly understood. The research question focused on whether the protective phenotypes conferred by Wolbachia persist when exposed to sublethal insecticide levels.
Design and population
This study investigated the impact of sublethal deltamethrin exposure on the Wolbachia–Aedes aegypti–DENV system, including pathogen blocking, cytoplasmic incompatibility, host reproductive traits, symbiont density, and viral diversity. The research was conducted in a controlled laboratory setting using Aedes aegypti mosquitoes infected with Wolbachia and DENV.
Core methods
The researchers exposed Aedes aegypti mosquitoes to sublethal concentrations of deltamethrin and assessed various components of the Wolbachia–DENV system, including symbiont density, pathogen blocking efficiency, cytoplasmic incompatibility, reproductive performance, and viral genetic diversity in mosquito saliva.
Main results
Insecticide exposure caused a transient reduction in Wolbachia density, which recovered rapidly. DENV blocking, cytoplasmic incompatibility, and reproductive performance were not compromised by the insecticide stress. Additionally, viral genetic diversity in mosquito saliva remained unaffected.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that Wolbachia-mediated dengue control remains robust under chemical stress, highlighting the potential of symbiont-based biological control strategies to function effectively in environmentally challenging conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide important insights for public health strategies, suggesting that Wolbachia-based interventions can maintain their efficacy even when mosquitoes are exposed to insecticides, which is critical for sustainable dengue control programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a controlled laboratory environment, which may limit its generalizability to real-world conditions. However, the evidence is strong and directly supports the robustness of Wolbachia-mediated dengue control under insecticide stress.
