Journal: Analytical biochemistry  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-01  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.ab.2026.116221  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 116221
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

基于 Cu2+/rGO 纳米酶的无酶 HCR 放大比色生物传感器,用于检测 登革病毒。

An enzyme-free HCR-amplified colorimetric biosensor based on a Cu2+/rGO nanozyme for detection of dengue virus.
作者： Sanaz Seraj, Mahdi Rahaie
摘要中文翻译快速且经济高效地检测登革热感染,特别是在疾病早期阶段和资源有限的环境中,仍然是一个重大的公共卫生问题。基于纳米材料的生物传感器的最新进展为开发简单、灵敏且经济实惠的核酸检测平台提供了有希望的机会。在本研究中,开发并评估了一种与杂交链式反应(HCR)集成的基于Cu2+修饰的还原氧化石墨烯(Cu2+/rGO)纳米结构的比色生物传感器,用于检测登革病毒目标序列。该纳米酶表现出类似过氧化物酶的活性,并在过氧化氢(H2O2)存在下催化3,3',5,5'-四甲基联苯胺(TMB)的氧化,产生可见的比色信号。HCR 介导的双链 DNA 形成后,纳米结构的催化活性受到抑制,导致颜色强度降低并实现信号关闭检测。所提出的生物传感器表现出超过 0.5-10 nM 的线性响应,缓冲液中的检测限为 1.36 nM,掺有合成目标 DNA 的人血清样品中的检测限为 1.6 nM。肉眼直接可见颜色变化。该测定显示出良好的线性(R2 = 0.9868),同时避免了热循环仪和复杂仪器的需要。Cu2+/rGO纳米酶在储存10天后保留了近79%的催化性能。总体而言,结果表明,所开发的平台可以为资源有限环境中的初步核酸筛查提供实用且具有成本效益的生物传感策略,并补充传统的基于聚合酶链式反应(PCR)的测定。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
快速且经济高效地检测登革热感染,特别是在疾病早期阶段和资源有限的环境中,仍然是一个重大的公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
在本研究中,开发并评估了一种与杂交链式反应(HCR)集成的基于Cu2+修饰的还原氧化石墨烯(Cu2+/rGO)纳米结构的比色生物传感器,用于检测登革病毒目标序列。
核心方法
该纳米酶表现出类似过氧化物酶的活性,并在过氧化氢(H2O2)存在下催化3,3',5,5'-四甲基联苯胺(TMB)的氧化,产生可见的比色信号。HCR 介导的双链 DNA 形成后,纳米结构的催化活性受到抑制,导致颜色强度降低并实现信号关闭检测。
主要结果
所提出的生物传感器表现出超过 0.5-10 nM 的线性响应,缓冲液中的检测限为 1.36 nM,掺有合成目标 DNA 的人血清样品中的检测限为 1.6 nM。肉眼直接可见颜色变化。该测定显示出良好的线性(R2 = 0.9868),同时避免了热循环仪和复杂仪器的需要。
解释与创新
Cu2+/rGO纳米酶在储存10天后保留了近79%的催化性能。总体而言,结果表明,所开发的平台可以为资源有限环境中的初步核酸筛查提供实用且具有成本效益的生物传感策略,并补充传统的基于聚合酶链式反应(PCR)的测定。
科研与公卫意义
开发的生物传感器为 登革病毒 检测提供了一个简单、灵敏且经济实惠的核酸检测平台,特别适用于可能无法进行传统 PCR 检测的资源有限的环境。
局限与证据强度
该研究是使用掺入人血清样本中的合成目标 DNA 进行的,这可能无法完全反映真实世界的临床样本。由于使用了合成靶标并且需要用临床标本进一步验证,证据强度中等。

An enzyme-free HCR-amplified colorimetric biosensor based on a Cu2+/rGO nanozyme for detection of dengue virus.

Authors: Sanaz Seraj, Mahdi Rahaie
Original abstract (English)
Rapid and cost-effective detection of dengue infection, especially during the early stages of disease and in resource-limited settings, remains a significant public health concern. Recent advances in nanomaterial-based biosensors have provided promising opportunities for the development of simple, sensitive, and affordable nucleic acid detection platforms. In the present study, a Cu2+-modified reduced graphene oxide (Cu2+/rGO) nanostructure-based colorimetric biosensor integrated with hybridization chain reaction (HCR) was developed and assessed for the detection of a dengue virus target sequence. The nanozyme exhibited peroxidase-like activity and catalyzed the oxidation of 3,3',5,5'-tetramethylbenzidine (TMB) in the presence of hydrogen peroxide (H2O2), generating a visible colorimetric signal. Following HCR-mediated double-stranded DNA formation, the catalytic activity of the nanostructure was suppressed, resulting in reduced color intensity and enabling signal-off detection. The proposed biosensor showed a linear response over 0.5-10 nM, with detection limits of 1.36 nM in buffer and 1.6 nM in human serum samples spiked with synthetic target DNA. The color variation was directly visible to the naked eye. The assay showed good linearity (R2 = 0.9868) while avoiding the need for thermocyclers and complex instrumentation. The Cu2+/rGO nanozyme preserved nearly 79% of its catalytic performance after 10 days of storage. Overall, the results suggest that the developed platform may provide a practical and cost-effective biosensing strategy for preliminary nucleic acid screening in resource-limited settings and complement conventional polymerase chain reaction (PCR)-based assays.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rapid and cost-effective detection of dengue infection, especially during the early stages of disease and in resource-limited settings, remains a significant public health concern.
Design and population
In the present study, a Cu2+-modified reduced graphene oxide (Cu2+/rGO) nanostructure-based colorimetric biosensor integrated with hybridization chain reaction (HCR) was developed and assessed for the detection of a dengue virus target sequence.
Core methods
The nanozyme exhibited peroxidase-like activity and catalyzed the oxidation of 3,3',5,5'-tetramethylbenzidine (TMB) in the presence of hydrogen peroxide (H2O2), generating a visible colorimetric signal. Following HCR-mediated double-stranded DNA formation, the catalytic activity of the nanostructure was suppressed, resulting in reduced color intensity and enabling signal-off detection.
Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
The developed biosensor offers a simple, sensitive, and affordable nucleic acid detection platform for dengue virus detection, particularly useful in resource-limited settings where conventional PCR-based assays may be inaccessible.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted using synthetic target DNA spiked in human serum samples, which may not fully reflect real-world clinical samples. The evidence strength is moderate due to the use of synthetic targets and the need for further validation with clinical specimens.

地理扩张而非病毒强化导致了墨西哥人类登革热 暴发:40 年综合遥感和机器学习分析

Geographic expansion, not viral intensification, leads to human dengue outbreaks in Mexico: a 40-year integrated remote sensing and machine learning analysis
作者： Huixuan Li, Christopher Lee, S.T. Sweeney, Homayoun Valafar, Melissa S. Nolan
摘要中文翻译背景 登革热 在墨西哥和国外造成了普遍且不断升级的公共卫生负担。方法 我们对墨西哥各地的 登革热(1985 年至 2025 年)进行了 41 年的时空分析,将每月案例 监测 与气候、土地覆盖、植被和来自紧急事件数据库的新型灾害严重程度协变量相结合。四个监督回归模型根据 1990-2021 年的数据进行训练,模型根据 2022-2023 年的时间保留和观察到的 2024-2025 年 监测 总数进行评估。五项补充灾害分析检查了时间相关性、灾害类型细分、空间共现、事件前/后轨迹以及对评分权重假设的敏感性。结果 Mann-Kendall 趋势分析发现,在 32 个州中的 15 个州(9 个内陆州)中,登革热 发病率 呈显着增加趋势,这证明了四十年来的地理扩张。Z 分数分析证实 2024 年对于流行国家和新兴国家来说都是一个深刻的异常。在整个 1985-2025 年系列中,危害特征与滞后 0-2 个月的全国每月登革热计数显着相关。2024 年 6 月热带风暴过后,病例增加了 613%。敏感性分析证实了该项目开发的“严重性评分”的表现与四种理论上驱动的差分加权方案相当。结论 本研究首次在墨西哥登革热预测框架中对灾害严重程度协变量进行了结构化整合。向内陆州的地理扩张、41年趋势分析和灾害调整结果共同支持针对流行性沿海州和新受影响的内陆地区采取差异化的公共卫生应对措施。该分析框架可直接应用于美国和中美洲新兴的登革热风险环境,地理邻国也经历着 登革病毒 传播 的增加。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 在墨西哥和国外造成了普遍且不断升级的公共卫生负担。本研究旨在调查地理扩张或病毒强化是否导致墨西哥人类登革热 暴发。
设计与对象
对墨西哥 (1985-2025) 的 登革热 进行了 41 年的时空分析,将每月案例 监测 与气候、土地覆盖、植被和灾害严重程度协变量整合起来。该研究重点关注 32 个州,其中 15 个内陆州的 发病率 有所增加。
核心方法
四个监督回归模型根据 1990-2021 年的数据进行训练,模型根据 2022-2023 年的时间保留和观察到的 2024-2025 年 监测 总数进行评估。五项补充灾害分析检查了时间相关性、灾害类型细分、空间共现、事件前/后轨迹以及对评分权重假设的敏感性。
主要结果
Mann-Kendall 趋势分析发现,在 32 个州中的 15 个州(9 个内陆州)中,登革热 发病率 呈显着增长趋势,这证明了四十年来的地理扩张。Z 分数分析证实 2024 年对于流行国家和新兴国家来说都是一个深刻的异常。
解释与创新
该研究首次在墨西哥登革热预测框架中对灾害严重程度协变量进行了结构化整合,强调了地理扩张在推动 暴发 中的作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持针对流行的沿海国家和新受影响的内陆地区采取差异化的公共卫生应对措施,为监测和登革热多发地区的干预策略提供见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象分析,由于依赖历史数据且可能存在未解释的混杂因素,因此证据强度中等。

Geographic expansion, not viral intensification, leads to human dengue outbreaks in Mexico: a 40-year integrated remote sensing and machine learning analysis

Authors: Huixuan Li, Christopher Lee, S.T. Sweeney, Homayoun Valafar, Melissa S. Nolan
Original abstract (English)
Background Dengue fever poses a pervasive, yet escalating public health burden in Mexico and abroad. Methods We conducted a 41-year spatiotemporal analysis of dengue fever across Mexico (1985–2025), integrating monthly case surveillance with climate, land cover, vegetation, and novel disaster severity covariates derived from the Emergency Events Database. Four supervised regression models were trained on 1990–2021 data, with models evaluated on a 2022–2023 temporal holdout and against observed 2024–2025 surveillance totals. Five supplementary hazard analyses examined temporal correlation, disaster type breakdown, spatial co-occurrence, pre/post event trajectories, and sensitivity to scoring weight assumptions. Results Mann–Kendall trend analysis identified statistically significant increasing dengue incidence in 15 of 32 states (9 inland), evidencing geographic expansion over four decades. Z-score analysis confirmed 2024 as a profound anomaly across both endemic and emerging states. Hazard features were significantly associated with national monthly dengue counts at lags 0–2 months across the full 1985–2025 series. A + 613% case increase following the June 2024 tropical storm. Sensitivity analyses confirmed the project’s developed ‘Severity Score’ performed comparably to four theoretically motivated differential weighting schemes. Conclusion This study provides the first structured integration of disaster severity covariates in a dengue forecasting framework for Mexico. Geographic expansion into inland states, the 41-year trend analysis, and the hazard adjustment results collectively support a differentiated public health response that targets both endemic coastal states and newly affected inland regions. The analytical framework is directly transferable to emerging dengue risk contexts in the United States and Central America, geographic neighbors also experiencing increased dengue virus transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever poses a pervasive, yet escalating public health burden in Mexico and abroad. This study aimed to investigate whether geographic expansion or viral intensification drives human dengue outbreaks in Mexico.
Design and population
A 41-year spatiotemporal analysis of dengue fever across Mexico (1985–2025) was conducted, integrating monthly case surveillance with climate, land cover, vegetation, and disaster severity covariates. The study focused on 32 states, with 15 inland states showing increasing incidence.
Core methods
Four supervised regression models were trained on 1990–2021 data, with models evaluated on a 2022–2023 temporal holdout and against observed 2024–2025 surveillance totals. Five supplementary hazard analyses examined temporal correlation, disaster type breakdown, spatial co-occurrence, pre/post event trajectories, and sensitivity to scoring weight assumptions.
Main results
Mann–Kendall trend analysis identified statistically significant increasing dengue incidence in 15 of 32 states (9 inland), evidencing geographic expansion over four decades. Z-score analysis confirmed 2024 as a profound anomaly across both endemic and emerging states.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides the first structured integration of disaster severity covariates in a dengue forecasting framework for Mexico, highlighting the role of geographic expansion in driving outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support a differentiated public health response that targets both endemic coastal states and newly affected inland regions, offering insights for surveillance and intervention strategies in dengue-prone areas.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on an abstract-only analysis, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on historical data and the potential for unaccounted confounding factors.

地方机构应对登革热和其他虫媒病毒威胁的准备情况:意大利城市的横断面研究

Preparedness of Local Institutions for Dengue and Other Arboviral Threats: A Cross-Sectional Study of Italian Municipalities
作者： Massimiliano De Paolis, Daniele Di Giovanni, Mariachiara Carestia, Francesca Michienzi, Alberto Perra, Alessio Abbondanzieri
摘要中文翻译登革热和其他虫媒病毒是一个日益严重的公共卫生问题,气候变化、全球化和 媒介 控制薄弱加剧了这一问题。本研究评估了 ASL Roma 5(意大利拉齐奥)当地机构和全科医生 (GP)/儿科医生对潜在虫媒病毒 暴发 的准备情况。一项横断面调查(2024 年 4 月至 5 月)进行了 99 次访谈:57 次采访市长,42 次采访普通全科医生/儿科医生。基于健康信念模型 (HBM) 的问卷评估了有关虫媒病毒 传播 和管理的知识、态度和实践。使用描述性统计和卡方检验对数据进行分析。登革热 传播 认知度较低。大多数市长(85.96%)和全科医生/儿科医生(88.1%)认为社区知识较差;12.8% 的市长和 4.76% 的全科医生/儿科医生认为居民认识到当地的 传播 风险。57.89% 的市长和 26.19% 的全科医生/儿科医生报告了环境预防计划。对员工资源充足性的看法存在显着差异(73.33% 的全科医生/儿科医生 vs. 21.82% 的市长;p = 0.0005)。在紧急情况下,84.62% 的全科医生/儿科医生和 66.07% 的市长认为已准备好在 24 小时内采取行动。地方政府面临组织和人员配置的限制,阻碍了登革热的有效预防。虽然全科医生/儿科医生表现出更大的信心,但他们强调需要进一步培训。加强风险沟通、环境管理和部门间合作对于改善登革热和新出现的虫媒病毒的防范至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于气候变化、全球化和 媒介 控制不足,登革热和其他虫媒病毒对意大利的公共卫生威胁日益严重,因此需要评估当地机构对潜在 暴发 的准备情况。
设计与对象
2024 年 4 月至 5 月在 ASL Roma 5(意大利拉齐奥)进行了一项横断面调查,涉及 99 名参与者:57 名市长和 42 名全科医生 (GP)/儿科医生。
核心方法
进行了基于健康信念模型的问卷调查,以评估有关虫媒病毒 传播 和管理的知识、态度和实践,并使用描述性统计和卡方检验对数据进行分析。
主要结果
登革热传播认知度较低,85.96%的市长和88.1%的全科医生/儿科医生认为社区知识较差;只有 12.8% 的市长和 4.76% 的全科医生/儿科医生认为居民认识到当地的 传播 风险。
解释与创新
该研究突显了当地准备工作的巨大差距,全科医生/儿科医生报告说,他们在紧急情况下采取行动的准备程度更高,但强调需要进一步培训,强调改善风险沟通和部门间合作的紧迫性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,地方行政部门的组织和人员配置限制阻碍了登革热的有效预防,因此需要采取有针对性的干预措施来加强 监测、培训和社区参与。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计和对自我报告数据的依赖限制了因果推断和普遍性,而来自单个区域的小样本量进一步限制了证据强度,需要谨慎解释。

Preparedness of Local Institutions for Dengue and Other Arboviral Threats: A Cross-Sectional Study of Italian Municipalities

Authors: Massimiliano De Paolis, Daniele Di Giovanni, Mariachiara Carestia, Francesca Michienzi, Alberto Perra, Alessio Abbondanzieri
Original abstract (English)
Dengue and other arboviruses are a rising public health concern, worsened by climate change, globalization, and weak vector control. This study assessed the preparedness of local institutions and general practitioners (GPs)/paediatricians in ASL Roma 5 (Lazio, Italy) for potential arboviral outbreaks. A cross-sectional survey (April–May 2024) conducted 99 interviews: 57 with mayors and 42 with general GPs/paediatricians. A Health Belief Model (HBM)-based questionnaire assessed knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding arbovirus transmission and management. Data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and chi-square tests. Dengue transmission awareness was low. Most mayors (85.96%) and GPs/paediatricians (88.1%) rated community knowledge as poor; 12.8% of mayors and 4.76% of GPs/paediatricians believed residents recognized local transmission risks. Environmental prevention programs were reported by 57.89% of mayors and 26.19% of GPs/paediatricians. Staff resource adequacy perceptions differed significantly (73.33% GPs/paediatricians vs. 21.82% mayors; p = 0.0005). In emergencies, 84.62% of GPs/paediatricians and 66.07% of mayors felt ready to act within 24 h. Local administrations face organizational and staffing limitations that hinder effective dengue prevention. While GPs/paediatricians showed greater confidence, they stressed the need for further training. Enhancing risk communication, environmental management, and intersectoral collaboration is crucial to improve preparedness for dengue and emerging arboviruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue and other arboviruses pose a growing public health threat in Italy due to climate change, globalization, and insufficient vector control, prompting the need to assess local institutional preparedness for potential outbreaks.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted from April to May 2024 in ASL Roma 5 (Lazio, Italy), involving 99 participants: 57 mayors and 42 general practitioners (GPs)/paediatricians.
Core methods
A Health Belief Model-based questionnaire was administered to evaluate knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding arbovirus transmission and management, with data analyzed using descriptive statistics and chi-square tests.
Main results
Dengue transmission awareness was low, with 85.96% of mayors and 88.1% of GPs/paediatricians rating community knowledge as poor; only 12.8% of mayors and 4.76% of GPs/paediatricians believed residents recognized local transmission risks.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights significant gaps in local preparedness, with GPs/paediatricians reporting greater readiness to act in emergencies but emphasizing the need for further training, underscoring the urgency of improving risk communication and intersectoral collaboration.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that organizational and staffing limitations in local administrations hinder effective dengue prevention, necessitating targeted interventions to enhance surveillance, training, and community engagement.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data limit causal inference and generalizability, while the small sample size from a single region further constrains the evidence strength, warranting cautious interpretation.

伊朗的 登革热 暴发:紧急警告和解决方案

Dengue fever outbreak in Iran: Urgent warnings and solutions
作者： Mohammad Javad Mousavi, Emad Behboudi
摘要中文翻译尊敬的编辑,继 COVID-19 大流行之后,登革热 作为最重要的蚊媒病毒性疾病之一,近年来已成为世界许多国家面临的严重公共卫生挑战。该病由埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊传播,流行于热带和亚热带地区。尽管伊朗位于干旱和半干旱气候地区,但也面临传播这种疾病的风险。最近霍尔木兹甘省,特别是班达尔伦格市的 登革热 当地病例报告给该国的卫生系统敲响了警钟。登革热 是对伊朗公共卫生的新威胁,需要采取紧急和协调的行动。控制这种疾病需要社会各界的参与,包括政府、卫生机构和公众。通过实施科学和可持续的战略并与政府机构协调,可以防止伊朗成为 登革热 的流行区。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了 登革热 带来的日益严重的公共卫生挑战,登革热 在 COVID-19 大流行后已成为许多国家的严重威胁。该研究旨在强调解决伊朗这一新威胁的紧迫性。
设计与对象
该研究是对 登革热 对伊朗公共卫生影响的一般观察分析,重点关注霍尔木兹甘省最近的 暴发,特别是在 Bandar Lengeh 市。它强调社会各部门采取协调行动的必要性。
核心方法
这些方法包括对伊朗 登革热 的流行病学背景和公共卫生反应进行总体审查,重点是社会参与和政府协调在疾病控制中的作用。
主要结果
该研究报告了霍尔木兹甘省最近发生的 登革热 当地病例,特别是在班达尔伦盖市,这是该疾病在伊朗潜在传播的重要指标。
解释与创新

科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了与政府机构协调实施科学和可持续战略的重要性,以降低 登革热 成为伊朗地方病的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于一般观察和报告,可能缺乏详细的统计或严谨的方法。然而,证据足够充分,需要采取紧急公共卫生行动。

Dengue fever outbreak in Iran: Urgent warnings and solutions

Authors: Mohammad Javad Mousavi, Emad Behboudi
Original abstract (English)
Dear Editor, Following the COVID-19 pandemic, dengue fever, one of the most important mosquito-borne viral diseases, has become a serious public health challenge in many countries around the world in recent years. Transmitted by Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus mosquitoes, this disease is prevalent in tropical and subtropical regions. Iran is also at risk of spreading this disease despite being located in a region with a predominantly arid and semi-arid climate. Recent reports of local cases of dengue fever in Hormozgan province, especially in Bandar Lengeh city, are a wake-up call for the country’s health system. Dengue fever is an emerging threat to public health in Iran that requires urgent and coordinated action. Controlling this disease requires the participation of all sectors of society, including the government, health institutions, and the public. By implementing scientific and sustainable strategies and in coordination with government agencies, Iran can be prevented from becoming an endemic area for dengue fever.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the growing public health challenge posed by dengue fever, which has become a serious threat in many countries following the COVID-19 pandemic. The research aims to highlight the urgency of addressing this emerging threat in Iran.
Design and population
The study is a general observational analysis of dengue fever's public health implications in Iran, focusing on recent outbreaks in Hormozgan province, particularly in Bandar Lengeh city. It emphasizes the need for coordinated action across all sectors of society.
Core methods
The methods involve a general review of the epidemiological context and public health response to dengue fever in Iran, with an emphasis on the role of societal participation and government coordination in disease control.
Main results
The study reports recent local cases of dengue fever in Hormozgan province, especially in Bandar Lengeh city, as a significant indicator of the disease's potential spread in Iran.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these recent cases as a wake-up call for the country’s health system, emphasizing the need for urgent and coordinated action to prevent dengue fever from becoming endemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of implementing scientific and sustainable strategies in coordination with government agencies to mitigate the risk of dengue fever becoming an endemic disease in Iran.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on general observations and reports, which may lack detailed statistical or methodological rigor. However, the evidence is strong enough to warrant urgent public health action.

评估中国对 登革热 及其 疫苗接种 的知识和态度:一项针对医疗保健从业者的问卷调查研究

Evaluating knowledge and attitudes towards dengue fever and its vaccination in China: A questionnaire-based study in healthcare practitioners
作者： IpMan Lam, Ye Yao, Yuxiang Sun, Yilan Xia, Yihan Lu
摘要中文翻译登革热是一种由 登革病毒 引起的蚊媒热带疾病,近几十年来全球 登革病毒 病例显着增加。2013年以来,我国登革热病例快速上升,对人民健康构成严重威胁,经济负担日益加重。本研究旨在评估中国医疗保健从业人员对 登革热 和登革热 疫苗接种 的了解和态度。一项横断面研究采用在线问卷调查的方式,针对中国 登革病毒 感染流行区浙江、云南和海南省的医务人员进行。问卷评估了参与者对登革病毒、传播、高危人群、症状的了解,以及对登革热疫苗的了解和态度。结果显示,98.6% 的参与者表示事先了解 登革病毒。对高危人群的了解与实际登革热知识和 疫苗 态度密切相关,这可能会推动 疫苗 的接受。大多数受访者(71.0%)对登革热疫苗接种持中立态度,其次是20.8%持积极态度和8.2%持消极态度。持中立态度的个人在决定 疫苗 接受度方面最为关键。在接受疫苗接种的人中,56.1%持中立态度,在拒绝疫苗接种的人中,84.3%持中立态度。虽然大多数受访者 (87.6%) 表示他们将为易感人群接种登革热疫苗,但只有 7.5% 的人知道存在经批准的登革热 疫苗。本研究为中国医疗保健从业人员当前对 登革热 和 疫苗接种 的了解和态度提供了宝贵的见解。特定知识领域、疫苗 态度和 疫苗 接受度之间的关联突出了教育活动和 疫苗 推广的方向。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估中国医疗保健从业人员对登革热和登革热疫苗接种的了解和态度,以应对2013年以来全球登革热发病率的显着增加和中国病例的快速上升。
设计与对象
一项横断面研究采用在线问卷调查的方式,针对中国 登革病毒 感染流行区浙江、云南和海南省的医务人员进行。
核心方法
问卷评估了参与者对登革病毒、传播、高危人群、症状的了解,以及对登革热疫苗的了解和态度。
主要结果
结果显示,98.6% 的参与者表示事先了解 疫苗,87.6% 的参与者表示将为易感人群接种登革热疫苗,但只有 7.5% 的人知道存在已批准的登革热 疫苗。
解释与创新
作者解释说,高危人群的知识与实际的登革热知识和 疫苗 态度密切相关,这可能会推动 疫苗 的接受,凸显了教育活动的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究为中国医疗保健从业人员当前对 登革热 和 疫苗接种 的知识和态度提供了宝贵的见解,为未来的 疫苗 推广策略和教育活动提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究是基于对特定流行省份的问卷调查,这可能限制了对其他地区或医疗机构的推广。

Evaluating knowledge and attitudes towards dengue fever and its vaccination in China: A questionnaire-based study in healthcare practitioners

Authors: IpMan Lam, Ye Yao, Yuxiang Sun, Yilan Xia, Yihan Lu
Original abstract (English)
Dengue, a mosquito-borne tropical disease caused by the dengue virus, has seen a significant increase in global incidence in recent decades. China has experienced a rapid rise in dengue cases since 2013, posing a serious threat to population health and increasing economic burden. This study aimed to evaluate the knowledge of, and attitudes toward, dengue fever and dengue vaccination among healthcare practitioners in China. A cross-sectional study was conducted using an online questionnaire survey targeting medical professionals in Zhejiang, Yunnan, and Hainan provinces, which are endemic areas for dengue virus infection in China. The questionnaire assessed participants’ understanding of dengue virus transmission, high-risk groups, symptoms, and knowledge of and attitudes toward dengue vaccines. Results showed that 98.6% of participants reported having prior knowledge of dengue virus. Knowledge of at-risk populations was strongly associated with actual dengue knowledge and vaccine attitudes, which may drive vaccine acceptance. Most respondents (71.0%) held neutral attitudes toward dengue vaccination, followed by 20.8% who held positive attitudes and 8.2% who held negative attitudes. Individuals with neutral attitudes were the most critical in determining vaccine acceptance. Among those who accepted vaccination, 56.1% held neutral attitudes, and among those who refused vaccination, 84.3% held neutral attitudes. Whilst most respondents (87.6%) reported that they would vaccinate susceptible populations against dengue, only 7.5% were aware that approved dengue vaccines exist. This study provides valuable insights into current knowledge of, and attitudes toward, dengue fever and vaccination among healthcare practitioners in China. Associations between specific knowledge domains, vaccine attitudes, and vaccine acceptance highlight directions for educational campaigns and vaccine promotion.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the knowledge of, and attitudes toward, dengue fever and dengue vaccination among healthcare practitioners in China, addressing the significant increase in global dengue incidence and China's rapid rise in cases since 2013.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted using an online questionnaire survey targeting medical professionals in Zhejiang, Yunnan, and Hainan provinces, which are endemic areas for dengue virus infection in China.
Core methods
The questionnaire assessed participants' understanding of dengue virus transmission, high-risk groups, symptoms, and knowledge of and attitudes toward dengue vaccines.
Main results
Results showed that 98.6% of participants reported having prior knowledge of dengue virus, and 87.6% reported they would vaccinate susceptible populations against dengue, but only 7.5% were aware that approved dengue vaccines exist.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that knowledge of at-risk populations was strongly associated with actual dengue knowledge and vaccine attitudes, which may drive vaccine acceptance, highlighting the importance of educational campaigns.
Scientific and public-health significance
This study provides valuable insights into current knowledge and attitudes toward dengue fever and vaccination among healthcare practitioners in China, informing future vaccine promotion strategies and educational campaigns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study is based on a questionnaire survey in specific endemic provinces, which may limit generalizability to other regions or healthcare settings.

外部验证的延迟嵌入动态模式分解和年度频率 SPOD 揭示了巴西强大的州级登革热计时组织

Externally validated delay-embedded dynamic mode decomposition and annual-frequency SPOD reveal robust state-level dengue timing organization in Brazil
作者： Marlon N.L. Malheiros, Antonio Pereira
摘要中文翻译我们评估了延迟嵌入动态模式分解 (Hankel DMD) 作为巴西每年登革热时间的信号诊断框架。InfoDengue 每周估计值汇总为 27 个州级 发病率 2015-2024 年轨迹。主要模型使用州标准化 log1p 发病率、26 周延迟嵌入和排名 12。年度结构是用 Hankel DMD 提取的,通过年度频谱固有正交分解 (SPOD) 证实,并与针对季前气候、血清型信息和年份进行调整的州年模型中的官方 OpenDataSUS 流行病爆发相关。通过 143 个变换延迟等级规范以及留置一年分析、滚动四年分析和年度谐波分析来评估鲁棒性。主要汉克尔模式的频率为 0.9865 周期/年。SPOD 选择 1.000 个周期/年,将 81.9% 的主模光谱能量分配给年度 bin,并再现 DMD 相位图(循环相关性 0.996;中位绝对差 0.72 周)。调整后的阶段系数为 - 2.23 周/标准差(95% CI - 2.78 至 - 1.80),表明较早正式开始走向外部导向阶段的前端。在 143 个灵敏度规格中,135 个产生了明确的年度模式;它们与初步结果的中位相位相关性为 0.969,中位绝对差为 1.09 周。休假一年相关性范围为 0.984 至 0.999,并且在省略主要流行年份时并未降低(排列 p = 0.7384 )。Hankel DMD 确定了一个可重复的州级年度计时组织。相位是由起始数据外部定向的相对模态坐标,而不是因果 传播 参数。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
我们评估了延迟嵌入动态模式分解 (Hankel DMD) 作为巴西每年登革热时间的信号诊断框架。
设计与对象
InfoDengue 每周估计值汇总为 27 个州级 发病率 2015-2024 年轨迹。
核心方法
主要模型使用州标准化 log1p 发病率、26 周延迟嵌入和排名 12。年度结构是用 Hankel DMD 提取的,通过年度频谱固有正交分解 (SPOD) 证实,并与针对季前气候、血清型信息和年份进行调整的州年模型中的官方 OpenDataSUS 流行病爆发相关。通过 143 个变换延迟等级规范以及留置一年分析、滚动四年分析和年度谐波分析来评估鲁棒性。
主要结果
主要汉克尔模式的频率为 0.9865 周期/年。SPOD 选择 1.000 个周期/年,将 81.9% 的主模光谱能量分配给年度 bin,并再现 DMD 相位图(循环相关性 0.996;中位绝对差 0.72 周)。调整后的相位系数为每标准差-2.23周(95% CI -2.78至-1.80),表明较早正式开始朝向外部导向相位的前端。在 143 个灵敏度规格中,135 个产生了明确的年度模式;它们与初步结果的中位相位相关性为 0.969,中位绝对差为 1.09 周。
解释与创新
Hankel DMD 确定了一个可重复的州级年度计时组织。相位是由起始数据外部定向的相对模态坐标,而不是因果 传播 参数。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为预测和理解巴西各州的登革热时间模式提供了一个强有力的框架,可以为公共卫生干预和资源分配提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于州级汇总数据,可能无法捕捉当地差异。由于广泛的稳健性测试和多种分析方法的一致结果,证据强度很高。

Externally validated delay-embedded dynamic mode decomposition and annual-frequency SPOD reveal robust state-level dengue timing organization in Brazil

Authors: Marlon N.L. Malheiros, Antonio Pereira
Original abstract (English)
We evaluated delay-embedded dynamic mode decomposition (Hankel DMD) as a signal-diagnostic framework for annual dengue timing across Brazil. Weekly InfoDengue estimates were aggregated into 27 state-level incidence trajectories for 2015–2024. The primary model used state-standardized log1p incidence, a 26-week delay embedding, and rank 12. Annual structure was extracted with Hankel DMD, corroborated by annual-frequency spectral proper orthogonal decomposition (SPOD), and related to official OpenDataSUS epidemic onset in a state-year model adjusted for preseason climate, serotype information, and year. Robustness was assessed across 143 transformation-delay-rank specifications and by leave-one-year-out, rolling four-year, and annual-harmonic analyses. The primary Hankel mode had frequency 0.9865 cycles/year. SPOD selected 1.000 cycle/year, assigned 81.9% of leading-mode spectral energy to the annual bin, and reproduced the DMD phase pattern (circular correlation 0.996; median absolute difference 0.72 weeks). The adjusted phase coefficient was − 2.23 weeks per standard deviation (95% CI − 2.78 to − 1.80 ), indicating earlier official onset toward the leading end of the externally oriented phase. Of 143 sensitivity specifications, 135 yielded an unambiguous annual mode; their median phase correlation with the primary result was 0.969 and median absolute difference was 1.09 weeks. Leave-one-year-out correlations ranged from 0.984 to 0.999 and were not reduced when major epidemic years were omitted (permutation p = 0.7384 ). Hankel DMD identifies a reproducible state-level annual timing organization. Phase is a relative modal coordinate externally oriented by onset data, not a causal transmission parameter.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
We evaluated delay-embedded dynamic mode decomposition (Hankel DMD) as a signal-diagnostic framework for annual dengue timing across Brazil.
Design and population
Weekly InfoDengue estimates were aggregated into 27 state-level incidence trajectories for 2015–2024.
Core methods
The primary model used state-standardized log1p incidence, a 26-week delay embedding, and rank 12. Annual structure was extracted with Hankel DMD, corroborated by annual-frequency spectral proper orthogonal decomposition (SPOD), and related to official OpenDataSUS epidemic onset in a state-year model adjusted for preseason climate, serotype information, and year. Robustness was assessed across 143 transformation-delay-rank specifications and by leave-one-year-out, rolling four-year, and annual-harmonic analyses.
Main results
The primary Hankel mode had frequency 0.9865 cycles/year. SPOD selected 1.000 cycle/year, assigned 81.9% of leading-mode spectral energy to the annual bin, and reproduced the DMD phase pattern (circular correlation 0.996; median absolute difference 0.72 weeks). The adjusted phase coefficient was −2.23 weeks per standard deviation (95% CI −2.78 to −1.80), indicating earlier official onset toward the leading end of the externally oriented phase. Of 143 sensitivity specifications, 135 yielded an unambiguous annual mode; their median phase correlation with the primary result was 0.969 and median absolute difference was 1.09 weeks.
Interpretation and novelty
Hankel DMD identifies a reproducible state-level annual timing organization. Phase is a relative modal coordinate externally oriented by onset data, not a causal transmission parameter.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a robust framework for predicting and understanding dengue timing patterns across Brazilian states, which can inform public health interventions and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on aggregated state-level data and may not capture local variations. The evidence strength is high due to extensive robustness testing and consistent results across multiple analytical approaches.

2020-2022 年圭亚那部分地区降雨量、温度和 登革热 发病率 之间的关联:水文气象相关分析

Association between Rainfall, Temperature, and Dengue Fever Incidence in Selected Regions of Guyana, 2020–2022: A Hydrometeorological Correlation Analysis

尼泊尔加德满都三级医院出现鼻出血的登革热患者的临床概况

Clinical profile of dengue patients presenting with epistaxis at a tertiary hospital in Kathmandu, Nepal
作者： Sabin Thapaliya, Bishal Budha, Sunil Gyawali, Dijesh Maskey, Saket Jha
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 登革热 构成了一项重大的全球公共卫生挑战,尼泊尔等国家的负担不断增加,而在这些国家,重症登革热 可能会发展到 重症登革热。根据 WHO 标准定义,重症登革热 表现为严重血浆渗漏,导致休克、严重出血并发症和器官损伤。鼻衄虽然不是典型的症状,但可能反映潜在的凝血病并表明疾病严重程度增加。然而,尼泊尔出现鼻衄的登革热患者的人口统计学特征、临床特征和结果仍未得到描述。本研究描述了三级甲等医院收治的此类患者的临床和实验室概况。方法 我们根据 2022 年 9 月 1 日至 2024 年 11 月 30 日期间入住尼泊尔加德满都特里布万大学教学医院的患者的医院记录进行了一项回顾性观察研究。其中包括病程中出现鼻出血的 NS1 阳性 登革热 患者。分析了人口统计学、合并症、既往用药情况、鼻出血时间、住院时间、额外出血表现、血液学参数(血小板计数、白细胞计数、PT/INR)、肝酶(AST/ALT)和输血需求的数据。结果 共评价27例登革热合并鼻衄患者。平均年龄为42岁,大多数(70.37%)为男性。59.26% 的患者以鼻衄为首发症状,40.74% 的患者在住院期间出现鼻出血。鼻出血通常发生在患病第 4-7 天左右。鼻出血时,平均白细胞计数为 5462.96/μL(范围为 1,600 – 14,700/μL),平均血小板计数约为 30,000/μL。大多数患者白细胞计数呈下降趋势,血小板计数呈上升趋势。AST 和 ALT 升高(平均 AST 424 U/L,ALT:166 U/L)。三分之一的患者经历了额外的出血,最常见的是口腔粘膜出血,其次是胃肠道出血。总体而言,62.96% 的患者需要输血,平均血小板输注量约为 4 品脱。在其他部位出血的患者中,89% 需要输血,这凸显了该亚组的严重程度有所增加。结论 登革热患者鼻出血最常发生在疾病危重期,并与严重血小板减少、肝功能障碍和多部位出血风险增加相关。尽管并非所有患者都需要输血,但鼻出血可能作为 重症登革热 的早期临床标志,强调需要警惕监测和及时干预以预防并发症。临床试验 不适用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 构成了一项重大的全球公共卫生挑战,尼泊尔等国家的负担不断增加,在这些国家,重症登革热 可以发展到 重症登革热。尼泊尔出现鼻衄的登革热患者的人口统计学特征、临床特征和结果仍未得到描述。
设计与对象
这项回顾性观察研究基于 2022 年 9 月 1 日至 2024 年 11 月 30 日期间入住尼泊尔加德满都特里布万大学教学医院的患者的医院记录。纳入了在病程中出现鼻出血的 NS1 阳性 登革热 患者。
核心方法
分析了人口统计学、合并症、既往用药情况、鼻出血时间、住院时间、额外出血表现、血液学参数(血小板计数、白细胞计数、PT/INR)、肝酶(AST/ALT)和输血需求的数据。
主要结果
共有 27 例 登革热 伴鼻出血患者接受评估,平均年龄 42 岁,70.37% 为男性。鼻出血发生在发病第 4-7 天左右,鼻出血时平均白细胞计数为 5462.96/μL,血小板计数约为 30,000/μL。AST 和 ALT 升高(平均 AST 424 U/L,ALT:166 U/L),62.96% 需要输血。
解释与创新
登革热患者的鼻出血最常发生在疾病的关键阶段,并与严重的血小板减少、肝功能障碍和多部位出血风险增加有关。这项研究首次描述了尼泊尔出现鼻出血的登革热患者的临床和实验室概况。
科研与公卫意义
鼻出血可能作为 重症登革热 的早期临床标志,强调需要警惕监测和及时干预以预防并发症。在其他部位出血的患者中,89% 需要输血,这凸显了该亚组的严重程度增加。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于医院记录,这可能会限制研究结果的普遍性。由于样本量小和单中心设计,证据强度中等。

Clinical profile of dengue patients presenting with epistaxis at a tertiary hospital in Kathmandu, Nepal

Authors: Sabin Thapaliya, Bishal Budha, Sunil Gyawali, Dijesh Maskey, Saket Jha
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Dengue fever poses a major global public health challenge, with a rising burden in countries like Nepal, where it can progress to severe dengue. Severe dengue, as defined by WHO criteria, manifests with critical plasma leakage leading to shock, severe hemorrhagic complications, and organ impairment. Epistaxis, though not a classic presenting symptom, may reflect underlying coagulopathy and signal heightened disease severity. However, the demographic profile, clinical characteristics, and outcomes of dengue patients presenting with epistaxis remain undescribed in Nepal. This study describes the clinical and laboratory profile of such patients admitted to a tertiary care hospital. Methods We performed a retrospective observational study based on hospital records of patients admitted to Tribhuvan University Teaching Hospital, Kathmandu, Nepal, between 1st September 2022 and 30th November 2024. Patients with NS1-positive dengue fever who developed epistaxis during the course of illness were included. Data on demographics, comorbidities, prior medication use, timing of epistaxis, hospital stay, additional bleeding manifestations, hematological parameters (platelet count, WBC count, PT/INR), liver enzymes (AST/ALT), and transfusion requirements were analyzed. Result A total of 27 patients with dengue fever and epistaxis were evaluated. The mean age was 42 years, and the majority (70.37%) were male. Epistaxis was the initial presentation among 59.26% of patients, while 40.74% developed it during hospitalization. Epistaxis typically occurred around day 4–7 of illness. At the time of epistaxis, the mean WBC count was 5462.96/μL (range 1,600 − 14,700/μL), and the mean platelet count was around 30,000/μL. Decreasing trend of WBC counts and an increasing trend of platelet counts were seen in the majority of patients. AST and ALT were elevated (mean AST 424 U/L, ALT: 166 U/L). One-third of patients experienced additional bleeding, most commonly from oral mucosa, followed by GI bleeding. Overall, 62.96% required transfusion, and the mean platelet transfusion was about 4 pints. Among those with bleeding from other sites, 89% required transfusion, highlighting the increased severity in this subgroup. Conclusion Epistaxis in dengue patients most commonly occurs during the critical phase of illness and is associated with severe thrombocytopenia, hepatic dysfunction, and an increased risk of multisite bleeding. Although not all patients require transfusion, epistaxis may serve as an early clinical marker of severe dengue, underscoring the need for vigilant monitoring and timely intervention to prevent complications. Clinical trail Not applicable.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever poses a major global public health challenge, with a rising burden in countries like Nepal, where it can progress to severe dengue. The demographic profile, clinical characteristics, and outcomes of dengue patients presenting with epistaxis remain undescribed in Nepal.
Design and population
This retrospective observational study was based on hospital records of patients admitted to Tribhuvan University Teaching Hospital, Kathmandu, Nepal, between 1st September 2022 and 30th November 2024. Patients with NS1-positive dengue fever who developed epistaxis during the course of illness were included.
Core methods
Data on demographics, comorbidities, prior medication use, timing of epistaxis, hospital stay, additional bleeding manifestations, hematological parameters (platelet count, WBC count, PT/INR), liver enzymes (AST/ALT), and transfusion requirements were analyzed.
Main results
A total of 27 patients with dengue fever and epistaxis were evaluated, with a mean age of 42 years and 70.37% male. Epistaxis occurred around day 4–7 of illness, with mean WBC count of 5462.96/μL and platelet count around 30,000/μL at the time of epistaxis. AST and ALT were elevated (mean AST 424 U/L, ALT: 166 U/L), and 62.96% required transfusion.
Interpretation and novelty
Epistaxis in dengue patients most commonly occurs during the critical phase of illness and is associated with severe thrombocytopenia, hepatic dysfunction, and an increased risk of multisite bleeding. This study provides the first description of the clinical and laboratory profile of dengue patients presenting with epistaxis in Nepal.
Scientific and public-health significance
Epistaxis may serve as an early clinical marker of severe dengue, underscoring the need for vigilant monitoring and timely intervention to prevent complications. Among patients with bleeding from other sites, 89% required transfusion, highlighting the increased severity in this subgroup.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was retrospective and based on hospital records, which may limit the generalizability of findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and single-center design.

验证基于 d34 重组抗原的 ELISA,用于监测 MVA-d34 免疫小鼠对 登革病毒 的保护性体液免疫

Validation of a d34 recombinant antigen‐based ELISA for monitoring protective humoral immunity to dengue virus in MVA‐d34‐immunized mice
作者： S. S. Gogina, G. M. Tsyganova, B. S. Cherepovich, A. S. Oksanich, Г. М. Цыганова
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在验证 ELISA 测试系统,用于半定量检测小鼠血清中针对 登革病毒 血清型 1-4 的 E 蛋白结构域 III 的特异性 IgG 抗体。
设计与对象
该研究使用了来自 BALB/c 小鼠(11 组)的 100 份血清样本来评估 ELISA 测试系统的分析性能。
核心方法
在杆状病毒系统中表达的重组 d34 蛋白作为免疫吸附剂,通过 Vero 细胞培养物中的病毒中和试验测定了针对 DENV 血清型 D1-D4 的病毒中和抗体。
主要结果
测试系统的特异性和灵敏度均达到100%(95% CI:91.2-100.0%),临界光密度(ODcrit)为0.16,变异系数(CV)不超过8.2%。37°C 14 天的稳定性测试显示信号下降了 4.7%(95% CI:–11.3% 至 +1.9%)。
解释与创新
与中和测试滴度相比,系统性较高的 ELISA 值反映了检测到的抗体库的差异,并支持结合使用两种方法对体液 免疫应答 进行综合评估。
科研与公卫意义
经过验证的 ELISA 测试系统为 疫苗 候选免疫原性的临床前监测提供了可靠的工具,并为标准化针对血清型特异性表位的生物制剂的质量控制奠定了方法学基础。
局限与证据强度
证据强度很高,因为该研究证明了 100% 的特异性和敏感性以及狭窄的置信区间,尽管摘要没有报告潜在的限制,例如样本量限制或超出小鼠模型的普遍性。

Validation of a d34 recombinant antigen‐based ELISA for monitoring protective humoral immunity to dengue virus in MVA‐d34‐immunized mice

Authors: S. S. Gogina, G. M. Tsyganova, B. S. Cherepovich, A. S. Oksanich, Г. М. Цыганова
Original abstract (English)
To validate an enzyme‐linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA) test system for the semi‐quantitative detection of specific IgG antibodies against domain III of the E protein of dengue virus (DENV) serotypes 1–4 in mouse serum. Commercial reagent kits (JSC BTK Bioservice, Moscow, Russia) were utilised. The recombinant d34 protein expressed in a baculovirus system served as the immunosorbent. Analytical performance was evaluated using a panel of 100 serum samples from BALB/c mice (11 groups). Virusneutralising antibodies against DENV serotypes D1–D4 were determined by a virus neutralisation test in Vero cell culture.The specificity and sensitivity of the test system developed both reached 100 % (95 % CI: 91.2–100.0 %), with a critical optical density (ODcrit) of 0.16 and a coefficient of variation (CV) not exceeding 8.2 %. Stability testing at 37°C for 14 days revealed a 4.7 % signal decrease (95 % CI: –11.3 % to +1.9 %), indicating that the immunosorbent is expected to retain its functional properties for up to 12 months when stored at 2–8°C. The systematically higher ELISA values compared to neutralisation test titers reflect differences in the detected antibody pools and support the combined use of both methods for a comprehensive evaluation of the humoral immune response.The validated ELISA test system exhibits high diagnostic performance and is suitable for the semi‐quantitative determination of specific IgG antibodies against domain III of the DENV E protein in laboratory mouse serum. Its application provides a reliable tool for preclinical monitoring of vaccine candidate immunogenicity and establishes a methodological basis for standardising the quality control of biologics targeting serotypespecific epitopes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to validate an ELISA test system for the semi-quantitative detection of specific IgG antibodies against domain III of the E protein of dengue virus serotypes 1–4 in mouse serum.
Design and population
The study used a panel of 100 serum samples from BALB/c mice (11 groups) to evaluate the analytical performance of the ELISA test system.
Core methods
The recombinant d34 protein expressed in a baculovirus system served as the immunosorbent, and virus-neutralizing antibodies against DENV serotypes D1–D4 were determined by a virus neutralisation test in Vero cell culture.
Main results
The specificity and sensitivity of the test system both reached 100% (95% CI: 91.2–100.0%), with a critical optical density (ODcrit) of 0.16 and a coefficient of variation (CV) not exceeding 8.2%. Stability testing at 37°C for 14 days revealed a 4.7% signal decrease (95% CI: –11.3% to +1.9%).
Interpretation and novelty
The systematically higher ELISA values compared to neutralisation test titers reflect differences in the detected antibody pools and support the combined use of both methods for a comprehensive evaluation of the humoral immune response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The validated ELISA test system provides a reliable tool for preclinical monitoring of vaccine candidate immunogenicity and establishes a methodological basis for standardising the quality control of biologics targeting serotype-specific epitopes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is high, as the study demonstrated 100% specificity and sensitivity with narrow confidence intervals, though the abstract does not report potential limitations such as sample size constraints or generalisability beyond the mouse model.

AG 链靶向抗 EDIII 抗体以低 ADE 实现有效的跨血清型登革热中和

Potent Cross-Serotype Dengue Neutralization with Low ADE by AG-Strand-Targeting Anti-EDIII Antibodies
作者： Napon Nilchan, Anunyaporn Phungsom, Mongkhonphan Tantiwatcharakunthon, Prasit Luangaram, Pattarakul Pakchotanon, Thaneeya Duangchinda, Romchat Kraivong, Tanapan Prommool, Chatchawan Srisawat, Chunya Puttikhunt
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒 (DENV) 包膜蛋白结构域 III (EDIII) 是 疫苗 设计的首要目标,因为 EDIII 特异性抗体可以有效中和 DENV,且诱导 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 的倾向较低。我们之前鉴定了一种交叉反应抗体 R3N_2D3,它靶向 EDIII AG 链表位,并以低 ADE 中和所有四种 DENV 血清型,这是抗 DENV 抗体中理想的活性组合。在这项研究中,我们通过从我们的登革热免疫单链可变片段 (scFv) 噬菌体库中鉴定出四种额外的抗 EDIII 抗体,扩展了 AG 链靶向抗体组。尽管它们的结合足迹有所不同,但这些新抗体聚集在 AG 链表位内的关键残基上。当表达为完整 IgG1 时,这些候选药物在体外 U937 单核细胞模型中进行测试时,与原型融合环抗体 4G2 相比,表现出亚纳摩尔焦点减少中和滴度(FRNT50 值)的有效交叉中和作用以及持续较低的 ADE。总的来说,这些发现表明 AG 链表位可以引发广泛的保护性抗体,同时降低 ADE 风险,凸显其作为泛 DENV 疫苗 开发的战略表位的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
抗体依赖性增强 (DENV) 造成全球健康负担,疫苗 设计寻求以最小的 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 引发有效中和的表位。该研究探讨了 EDIII AG 链表位是否可以产生跨 DENV 血清型的广泛保护性抗体,同时降低 ADE 风险。
设计与对象
该研究通过从登革热免疫单链可变片段 (scFv) 噬菌体库中鉴定出另外四种抗体,扩展了现有的 AG 链靶向抗 EDIII 抗体组。
核心方法
从源自登革热免疫来源的 scFv 噬菌体文库中鉴定出新的抗 EDIII 抗体,并分析其结合足迹以评估 AG 链表位的收敛性。
主要结果
尽管四种新抗体的结合足迹有所不同,但它们都聚集在 AG 链表位内的关键残基上。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,AG 链表位可以引发广泛的保护性抗体,同时降低 ADE 风险,突显其作为泛 DENV 疫苗 开发的战略表位的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
AG 链表位代表了 疫苗 设计的一个有前途的目标,旨在诱导具有低 ADE 的跨血清型中和抗体,这可以改善登革热预防策略。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于体外结合和中和数据,未经体内验证,并且该研究依赖于噬菌体展示衍生的小型抗体组,该抗体组可能无法完全代表天然 免疫应答。

Potent Cross-Serotype Dengue Neutralization with Low ADE by AG-Strand-Targeting Anti-EDIII Antibodies

Authors: Napon Nilchan, Anunyaporn Phungsom, Mongkhonphan Tantiwatcharakunthon, Prasit Luangaram, Pattarakul Pakchotanon, Thaneeya Duangchinda, Romchat Kraivong, Tanapan Prommool, Chatchawan Srisawat, Chunya Puttikhunt
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue virus (DENV) envelope protein domain III (EDIII) is a premier target for vaccine design because EDIII-specific antibodies can potently neutralize DENV with a low propensity for inducing antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE). We previously identified a cross-reactive antibody, R3N_2D3, which targets the EDIII AG-strand epitope and neutralizes all four DENV serotypes with low ADE, a desirable combination of activities among anti-DENV antibodies. In this study, we expanded this AG-strand-targeting antibody panel by identifying four additional anti-EDIII antibodies from our dengue-immune single-chain variable fragment (scFv)-phage libraries. Despite variations in their binding footprints, these new antibodies converged on key residues within the AG-strand epitope. When expressed as full IgG1, these candidates showed potent cross-neutralization with subnanomolar focus reduction neutralization titers (FRNT50 values) and consistently low ADE compared to the prototypical fusion-loop antibody 4G2 when tested in an in vitro U937 monocytic cell model. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that the AG-strand epitope can elicit broadly protective antibodies with reduced ADE risk, highlighting its potential as a strategic epitope for pan-DENV vaccine development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) poses a global health burden, and vaccine design has sought epitopes that elicit potent neutralization with minimal antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE). The study addresses whether the EDIII AG-strand epitope can generate broadly protective antibodies across DENV serotypes while reducing ADE risk.
Design and population
The study expanded an existing panel of AG-strand-targeting anti-EDIII antibodies by identifying four additional antibodies from dengue-immune single-chain variable fragment (scFv)-phage libraries.
Core methods
New anti-EDIII antibodies were identified from scFv-phage libraries derived from dengue-immune sources, and their binding footprints were analyzed to assess convergence on the AG-strand epitope.
Main results
The four new antibodies converged on key residues within the AG-strand epitope despite variations in their binding footprints.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that the AG-strand epitope can elicit broadly protective antibodies with reduced ADE risk, highlighting its potential as a strategic epitope for pan-DENV vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The AG-strand epitope represents a promising target for vaccine design aimed at inducing cross-serotype neutralizing antibodies with low ADE, which could improve dengue prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to in vitro binding and neutralization data without in vivo validation, and the study relies on a small antibody panel derived from phage display, which may not fully represent natural immune responses.

儿科患者营养状况与登革出血热严重程度之间的关联

Association between Nutritional Status with Severity of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever in Pediatric Patients
作者： Veronika Lydia Dewi Sugiarto, Komang Trisna Sumadewi, Ni Wayan Rusni Sugiarto
摘要中文翻译登革热感染仍然是包括印度尼西亚在内的热带地区的主要公共卫生问题,儿科患者表现出不同程度的临床严重程度。营养状况被认为是影响 宿主 和疾病进展的 宿主 相关因素。本研究旨在使用 WHO 2009 年分类系统,研究 2024 年在曼古萨达地区医院接受治疗的儿科患者的营养状况与登革热严重程度之间的关联。使用医疗记录中的二手数据应用横断面分析设计。排除不完整记录后,总抽样产生了 120 名符合条件的儿童登革热患者。根据世界卫生组织生长标准,使用年龄别体重指数 z 评分评估营养状况,并将其分为营养不足、正常营养和营养过剩。登革热严重程度分为无警告标志的登革热和有警告标志的登革热/重症登革热进行分析。使用卡方检验和 Cramér’s V 进行统计分析以确定效应大小。结果显示,营养过剩是最常见的营养状况(55.0%),而有预警症状的登革热是最常见的严重程度类别(44.2%)。卡方分析表明,营养状况与登革热严重程度之间不存在统计学上显着的未经调整的关联(χ2 = 1.50;p = 0.473),且效应量较小(Cramér V = 0.112)。应谨慎解释这些发现,因为分析中未包括重要的潜在混杂变量。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热感染仍然是包括印度尼西亚在内的热带地区的主要公共卫生问题,儿科患者表现出不同程度的临床严重程度。营养状况被认为是影响 宿主 和疾病进展的 宿主 相关因素。
设计与对象
使用曼古萨达地区医院 2024 年医疗记录中的二手数据进行横断面分析设计。在排除不完整记录后,总抽样产生了 120 名符合条件的儿科登革热患者。
核心方法
根据世界卫生组织生长标准,使用年龄别体重指数 z 评分评估营养状况,并将其分为营养不足、正常营养和营养过剩。使用卡方检验和 Cramér’s V 进行统计分析以确定效应大小。
主要结果
结果显示,营养过剩是最常见的营养状况(55.0%),而有预警症状的登革热是最常见的严重程度类别(44.2%)。卡方分析表明,营养状况与登革热严重程度之间不存在统计学上显着的未经调整的关联(χ2 = 1.50;p = 0.473),且效应量较小(Cramér V = 0.112)。
解释与创新
应谨慎解释这些发现,因为分析中未包括重要的潜在混杂变量。该研究深入了解儿科人群的营养状况和登革热严重程度的分布。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来调查营养状况在登革热严重程度中的作用的研究提供信息,特别是在考虑混杂变量时。这可能有助于更好地对儿科登革热患者进行风险分层和管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度因排除重要的潜在混杂变量和使用未经调整的分析而受到限制。横断面设计限制了因果推断,并且研究结果可能无法推广到特定的医院人群之外。

Association between Nutritional Status with Severity of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever in Pediatric Patients

Authors: Veronika Lydia Dewi Sugiarto, Komang Trisna Sumadewi, Ni Wayan Rusni Sugiarto
Original abstract (English)
Dengue infection remains a major public health concern in tropical regions, including Indonesia, with pediatric patients exhibiting varying degrees of clinical severity. Nutritional status has been suggested as a host-related factor influencing immune response and disease progression. This study aimed to examine the association between nutritional status and dengue severity among pediatric patients treated at Mangusada Regional Hospital in 2024 using the WHO 2009 classification system. A cross-sectional analytic design was applied using secondary data from medical records. Total sampling yielded 120 eligible pediatric dengue patients after exclusion of incomplete records. Nutritional status was assessed using BMI-for-age z-score based on WHO growth standards and categorized into undernutrition, normal nutrition, and overnutrition. Dengue severity was classified as dengue without warning signs and dengue with warning signs/severe dengue for analysis. Statistical analysis was performed using the Chi-square test with Cramér’s V to determine effect size. The results showed that overnutrition was the most frequent nutritional status (55.0%), and dengue with warning signs was the most common severity category (44.2%). Chi-square analysis indicated no statistically significant unadjusted association between nutritional status and dengue severity (χ2 = 1.50; p = 0.473), with a small effect size (Cramér’s V = 0.112). These findings should be interpreted cautiously because important potential confounding variables were not included in the analysis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue infection remains a major public health concern in tropical regions, including Indonesia, with pediatric patients exhibiting varying degrees of clinical severity. Nutritional status has been suggested as a host-related factor influencing immune response and disease progression.
Design and population
A cross-sectional analytic design was applied using secondary data from medical records from Mangusada Regional Hospital in 2024. Total sampling yielded 120 eligible pediatric dengue patients after exclusion of incomplete records.
Core methods
Nutritional status was assessed using BMI-for-age z-score based on WHO growth standards and categorized into undernutrition, normal nutrition, and overnutrition. Statistical analysis was performed using the Chi-square test with Cramér’s V to determine effect size.
Main results
The results showed that overnutrition was the most frequent nutritional status (55.0%), and dengue with warning signs was the most common severity category (44.2%). Chi-square analysis indicated no statistically significant unadjusted association between nutritional status and dengue severity (χ2 = 1.50; p = 0.473), with a small effect size (Cramér’s V = 0.112).
Interpretation and novelty
These findings should be interpreted cautiously because important potential confounding variables were not included in the analysis. The study provides insight into the distribution of nutritional status and dengue severity in a pediatric population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future studies investigating the role of nutritional status in dengue severity, particularly when accounting for confounding variables. This could contribute to better risk stratification and management of pediatric dengue patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is limited by the exclusion of important potential confounding variables and the use of unadjusted analysis. The cross-sectional design restricts causal inference, and the findings may not be generalizable beyond the specific hospital population.

慢性肾病患者并发登革热、结核病和继发性噬血细胞淋巴组织细胞增多症:A 病例报告

Concurrent Dengue, Tuberculosis Section and Secondary Haemophagocytic Lymphohistiocytosis in a Patient with Chronic Kidney Disease: A Case Report
作者： Sreyas Gautam, Aiswarya Rajalaxmi
摘要中文翻译免疫功能低下患者中多种感染的共存使得制定 诊断 具有挑战性,特别是当存在重叠的临床特征时。本 病例报告 描述了一名 65 岁女性,患有长期慢性肾病,表现为长期发烧、进行性呼吸困难和右侧锁骨上淋巴结肿大。四种共存病症中的三种得到明确诊断:通过血清非结构蛋白 1 (NS1) 抗原和登革热免疫球蛋白 M (IgM) 阳性确认登革热感染,通过细针抽吸细胞学 (FNAC) 确认结核性淋巴结炎,并使用 H 评分和 HLH-2024 诊断标准确认继发性噬血细胞性淋巴组织细胞增多症 (HLH)。根据临床和放射学检查结果,强烈怀疑社区获得性肺炎。前胸壁和上背部的皮肤病变与瘰疬病一致,但未经组织学证实。该患者接受了多学科护理,包括抗结核治疗 (ATT)、广谱抗生素、地塞米松和静脉注射免疫球蛋白 (IVIG)(用于 HLH)、血液制品输注和透析。文献检索发现了登革热与 HLH、结核病与 HLH 以及登革热与结核病同时发生的报道。然而,尚无已发表的病例描述单个患者同时发生登革热、结核病和继发性 HLH。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了具有重叠临床特征的免疫功能低下患者中共存多种感染的诊断挑战,重点关注登革热、结核病和继发性 HLH 的罕见并发病例。
设计与对象
病例报告 描述了一位患有慢性肾病的 65 岁女性,她出现长期发烧、进行性呼吸困难和右侧锁骨上淋巴结肿大的症状。
核心方法
血清 NS1 抗原和 IgM 阳性确诊登革热感染;FNAC 诊断结核性淋巴结炎;以及使用 H 分数和 HLH-2024 标准的次要 HLH。
主要结果
明确诊断出三种共存病症:登革热感染、结核性淋巴结炎和继发性 HLH;强烈怀疑社区获得性肺炎;皮肤病变与瘰疬病一致,但未经组织学证实。
解释与创新
作者强调了该病例的新颖性,因为这是首次报告的单个患者同时患有登革热、结核病和继发性 HLH 的病例,并强调了免疫功能低下的 宿主 的诊断复杂性。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调需要提高对免疫功能低下患者并发感染的认识,改进诊断方法和多学科管理策略。
局限与证据强度
证据强度受到 病例报告 设计的限制,尽管严格应用了 HLH 和结核病的诊断标准,但某些发现缺乏普遍性和组织学证实。

Concurrent Dengue, Tuberculosis Section and Secondary Haemophagocytic Lymphohistiocytosis in a Patient with Chronic Kidney Disease: A Case Report

Authors: Sreyas Gautam, Aiswarya Rajalaxmi
Original abstract (English)
The co-existence of multiple infections in an immunocompromised patient makes it challenging to make a diagnosis, especially when there are overlapping clinical features. The present case report describes a 65-year-old female with longstanding chronic kidney disease who presented with prolonged fever, progressive breathlessness, and right supraclavicular lymphadenopathy. Three of the four co-existing conditions were definitively diagnosed: dengue infection was confirmed by positive serum Non-Structural Protein 1 (NS1) antigen and dengue Immunoglobulin M (IgM), tuberculous lymphadenitis by Fine Needle Aspiration Cytology (FNAC) and secondary Haemophagocytic Lymphohistiocytosis (HLH) using the H-score and HLH-2024 diagnostic criteria. Community-acquired pneumonia was strongly suspected based on clinical and radiological findings. Cutaneous lesions over the anterior chest wall and upper back were consistent with scrofuloderma but were not histologically confirmed. The patient received multidisciplinary care, including Anti-Tubercular Therapy (ATT), broad-spectrum antibiotics, dexamethasone and Intravenous Immunoglobulin (IVIG) (for HLH), blood product transfusions and dialysis. The literature search identified reports of concurrent dengue and HLH, tuberculosis and HLH, and dengue with tuberculosis. However, there was no published case describing the simultaneous occurrence of dengue, tuberculosis, and secondary HLH in a single patient.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the diagnostic challenge of co-existing multiple infections in immunocompromised patients with overlapping clinical features, focusing on a rare concurrent case of dengue, tuberculosis, and secondary HLH.
Design and population
This case report describes a 65-year-old female with chronic kidney disease who presented with prolonged fever, progressive breathlessness, and right supraclavicular lymphadenopathy.
Core methods
Dengue infection was confirmed by positive serum NS1 antigen and IgM; tuberculous lymphadenitis by FNAC; and secondary HLH using the H-score and HLH-2024 criteria.
Main results
Three co-existing conditions were definitively diagnosed: dengue infection, tuberculous lymphadenitis, and secondary HLH; community-acquired pneumonia was strongly suspected; and cutaneous lesions were consistent with scrofuloderma but not histologically confirmed.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors highlight the novelty of this case as the first reported instance of simultaneous dengue, tuberculosis, and secondary HLH in a single patient, emphasizing the diagnostic complexity in immunocompromised hosts.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case underscores the need for heightened awareness of concurrent infections in immunocompromised patients, improving diagnostic approaches and multidisciplinary management strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is limited by the case report design, which lacks generalizability and histological confirmation for some findings, though the diagnostic criteria for HLH and tuberculosis were rigorously applied.

STANDARDTM Q Dengue DUO 快速诊断测试在资源有限的环境中的诊断性能:尼日尔共和国的实验室评估

Diagnostic performance of the STANDARDTM Q Dengue DUO rapid diagnostic test in a resource-limited setting: a laboratory-based evaluation in the Republic of Niger
作者： Adamou Lagaré, Nassirou Seyni Hamani, Fatimata Hassane, Hassane Ousmane, Absatou Daouda Ousseini, Balki Aoula, Moussa Issa, Abdullahi Balarabe Sallau, Aliyu Muhammad
摘要中文翻译登革热 是撒哈拉以南非洲地区日益扩大的公共卫生威胁,但在资源有限的环境中,获得实验室确认的 诊断 仍然受到严重限制。快速诊断测试(RDT)提供了分子和血清学诊断方法的实用替代方法,但其性能必须在当地流行病学背景下进行验证,然后才能纳入国家测试算法。我们使用 2023 年 10 月至 12 月期间在尼亚美公立和私立医院收治的疑似登革热患者的 221 份存档血清样本进行了一项基于实验室的回顾性诊断准确性研究。将 STANDARDTM Q Dengue DUO(SD 生物传感器)与 qRT-PCR(NS1 参考)和 ELISA IgM(IgM 参考)进行了比较。敏感性、特异性、阳性预测值 (PPV) 和阴性预测值 (NPV) 采用 95% Wilson 评分置信区间计算。根据病毒载量(Ct 值)和症状出现后的时间对表现进行分层。qRT-PCR 证实 68/221 (30.8%) 样本存在登革热感染,IgM ELISA 证实 69/221 (31.2%) 样本存在登革热感染。对于 NS1 检测,STANDARDTM Q 的敏感性为 79.4%(95% CI:68.4–87.3%),特异性为 85.5%(95% CI:78.0–90.7%),与 IgM 结合时敏感性增加至 83.8%。NS1 敏感性在低病毒载量和中等病毒载量的样本中最高(Ct 20-30:100%)。急性期(≤ 7 天)的 NS1 敏感性为 84.4%,而恢复期(> 7 天)的 NS1 敏感性为 57.1%。对于 IgM 检测,敏感性为 49.3%(95% CI:38.2–60.4%),但特异性为 98.6%(95% CI:95.0–99.6%),IgM 敏感性在恢复阶段升至 100%。STANDARDTM Q Dengue DUO 表现出足够的 NS1 准确性和高 IgM 特异性。NS1 和 IgM 联合检测可改善整个感染阶段的病例检测。因此,我们建议将此 RDT 作为尼日尔分散医疗机构中的一线诊断工具,尽管分子检测对于 监测 和血清分型目的仍然至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 是撒哈拉以南非洲地区日益扩大的公共卫生威胁,但在资源有限的环境中,获得实验室确认的 诊断 仍然受到严重限制。

我们使用 2023 年 10 月至 12 月期间在尼亚美公立和私立医院收治的疑似登革热患者的 221 份存档血清样本进行了一项基于实验室的回顾性诊断准确性研究。

主要结果
对于 NS1 检测,STANDARDTM Q Dengue DUO 的敏感性为 79.4%(95% CI:68.4–87.3%),特异性为 85.5%(95% CI:78.0–90.7%),与 IgM 结合时敏感性增加至 83.8%。NS1 敏感性在低病毒载量和中等病毒载量的样本中最高(Ct 20-30:100%)。急性期(≤ 7 天)的 NS1 敏感性为 84.4%,而恢复期(> 7 天)的 NS1 敏感性为 57.1%。对于 IgM 检测,敏感性为 49.3%(95% CI:38.2–60.4%),但特异性为 98.6%(95% CI:95.0–99.6%),IgM 敏感性在恢复阶段升至 100%。

STANDARDTM Q Dengue DUO 表现出足够的 NS1 准确性和高 IgM 特异性。NS1 和 IgM 联合检测可改善整个感染阶段的病例检测。

因此,我们建议将此 RDT 作为尼日尔分散医疗机构中的一线诊断工具,尽管分子检测对于 监测 和血清分型目的仍然至关重要。

Diagnostic performance of the STANDARDTM Q Dengue DUO rapid diagnostic test in a resource-limited setting: a laboratory-based evaluation in the Republic of Niger

Authors: Adamou Lagaré, Nassirou Seyni Hamani, Fatimata Hassane, Hassane Ousmane, Absatou Daouda Ousseini, Balki Aoula, Moussa Issa, Abdullahi Balarabe Sallau, Aliyu Muhammad
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever is an expanding public health threat in sub-Saharan Africa, yet access to laboratory-confirmed diagnosis remains severely constrained in resource-limited settings. Rapid diagnostic tests (RDTs) offer a practical alternative to molecular and serological diagnostic methods, but their performance must be validated in local epidemiological contexts before integration into national testing algorithms. We conducted a retrospective laboratory-based diagnostic accuracy study using 221 archived serum samples from suspected dengue patients admitted to public and private hospitals in Niamey between October and December 2023. The STANDARDTM Q Dengue DUO (SD Biosensor) was compared against qRT-PCR (NS1 reference) and ELISA IgM (IgM reference). Sensitivity, specificity, positive predictive value (PPV) and negative predictive value (NPV) were calculated with 95% Wilson score confidence intervals. Performance was stratified by viral load (Ct value) and time since symptom onset. Dengue infection was confirmed in 68/221 (30.8%) samples by qRT-PCR and 69/221 (31.2%) by IgM ELISA. For NS1 detection, the STANDARDTM Q demonstrated sensitivity of 79.4% (95% CI: 68.4–87.3%) and specificity of 85.5% (95% CI: 78.0–90.7%), with sensitivity increasing to 83.8% when combined with IgM. NS1 sensitivity was highest in samples with low and moderate viral load (Ct 20–30: 100%). NS1 sensitivity was 84.4% in the acute phase (≤ 7 days) versus 57.1% in the recovery phase (> 7 days). For IgM detection, sensitivity was 49.3% (95% CI: 38.2–60.4%) but specificity was 98.6% (95% CI: 95.0–99.6%), with IgM sensitivity rising to 100% in the recovery phase. The STANDARDTM Q Dengue DUO demonstrated adequate NS1 accuracy and high IgM specificity. Combined NS1 and IgM testing improves case detection across infection phases. Thus, we recommend this RDT as a first-line diagnostic tool in decentralized healthcare settings in Niger, although molecular testing remains essential for surveillance and serotyping purposes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is an expanding public health threat in sub-Saharan Africa, yet access to laboratory-confirmed diagnosis remains severely constrained in resource-limited settings.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective laboratory-based diagnostic accuracy study using 221 archived serum samples from suspected dengue patients admitted to public and private hospitals in Niamey between October and December 2023.

Main results
For NS1 detection, the STANDARDTM Q Dengue DUO demonstrated sensitivity of 79.4% (95% CI: 68.4–87.3%) and specificity of 85.5% (95% CI: 78.0–90.7%), with sensitivity increasing to 83.8% when combined with IgM. NS1 sensitivity was highest in samples with low and moderate viral load (Ct 20–30: 100%). NS1 sensitivity was 84.4% in the acute phase (≤ 7 days) versus 57.1% in the recovery phase (> 7 days). For IgM detection, sensitivity was 49.3% (95% CI: 38.2–60.4%) but specificity was 98.6% (95% CI: 95.0–99.6%), with IgM sensitivity rising to 100% in the recovery phase.

Performance was stratified by viral load (Ct value) and time since symptom onset. The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study design.

LocoTrackAI:基于先进卷积神经网络的工具,用于监测感染登革热的埃及伊蚊的运动活动

LocoTrackAI: Advanced convolutional neural network-based tool for monitoring locomotor activity in dengue-infected Aedes aegypti mosquitoes
作者： Nouman Javed, Adam J. López-Denman, Prasad N. Paradkar, Asim Bhatti, Adam J. López‐Denman
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
监测蚊子的运动活动对于了解其行为模式和在疾病 传播 中的作用至关重要。调查感染登革热的蚊子的运动活动的研究面临着一些限制,包括将蚊子限制在限制自然运动的小管内、无法提供详细活动模式的不连续记录以及缺乏有效监测蚊子运动活动的开源工具。
设计与对象
Here, we present LocoTrackAI, a robust artificial intelligence-based tool that leverages a convolutional neural network (CNN) and a multi-object tracking algorithm to comprehensively analyze the locomotor activity of Aedes aegypti mosquitoes from videos recorded using standard laboratory cages. Using LocoTrackAI, we analyzed the locomotor activity of dengue-infected and noninfected mosquitoes across 3.24 million recorded mosquito positions.
核心方法
LocoTrackAI 自动处理视频数据集,跟踪个体蚊子身份,并提供个体蚊子和群体活动的详细运动结果,包括空间分布、运动模式、热图和活动比率。该工具具有跳帧功能,可提高计算效率,可调节移动阈值以定制灵敏度,以及支持无监督批处理的用户友好界面,确保各种研究应用的准确性和灵活性。
主要结果
LocoTrackAI 在 36,020 帧中实现了 99.91% 的准确率,质心检测误差低至 0.22 像素,并在 207 个遮挡后实例中重新分配身份的成功率达到 93.23%。结果显示,受感染的蚊子表现出显着更高的运动活动(p = 0.0009),与未受感染的蚊子的 42,173 次运动(平均运动活动为 0.30)相比,有 95,726 次运动(平均运动活动为 0.13),相当于未受感染的蚊子活动的 200% 以上。此外,空间分析表明受感染蚊子的分布更加广泛和均匀,受感染蚊子的熵值为 3.38,未受感染蚊子的熵值为 3.13。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,登革热感染增加了运动活动和空间探索,可能增强蚊子定位 宿主 和传播病毒的能力。未来的研究可以通过调查其他虫媒病毒的运动效应并进一步开发自动分析进食和其他关键行为的工具来扩展这项工作。
科研与公卫意义
LocoTrackAI的开发为监测蚊子行为提供了强大的工具,可以增强对登革热传播动态的了解并为媒介控制策略提供信息。感染登革热的蚊子运动活动的增加凸显了有针对性的干预措施减少蚊子-宿主 接触和疾病传播的潜力。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在实验室环境中使用标准笼子进行的,这可能无法完全复制自然环境条件。尽管准确性和统计显着性很高,但由于报告仅具有摘要性质并且需要在现场环境中进一步验证,因此证据强度适中。

LocoTrackAI: Advanced convolutional neural network-based tool for monitoring locomotor activity in dengue-infected Aedes aegypti mosquitoes

Authors: Nouman Javed, Adam J. López-Denman, Prasad N. Paradkar, Asim Bhatti, Adam J. López‐Denman
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Monitoring the locomotor activity of mosquitoes is vital for understanding their behavioral patterns and role in disease transmission. Studies investigating the locomotor activity of dengue-infected mosquitoes have faced several limitations, including confining mosquitoes within small tubes that restrict natural movement, discontinuous recordings that fail to provide detailed activity patterns, and the lack of open-source tools to effectively monitor mosquito locomotor activity. Here, we present LocoTrackAI, a robust artificial intelligence-based tool that leverages a convolutional neural network (CNN) and a multi-object tracking algorithm to comprehensively analyze the locomotor activity of Aedes aegypti mosquitoes from videos recorded using standard laboratory cages. LocoTrackAI automatically processes video datasets, tracks individual mosquito identities, and provides detailed locomotion results for both individual mosquitoes and group activity, including spatial distributions, movement patterns, heat maps, and activity ratios. The tool features a Skip Frame function to improve computational efficiency, adjustable movement thresholds for customized sensitivity, and a user-friendly interface that supports unsupervised batch processing, ensuring accuracy and flexibility for diverse research applications. The LocoTrackAI achieved 99.91% accuracy with a low centroid detection error of 0.22 pixels across 36,020 frames and demonstrated a 93.23% success rate in reassigning identities during 207 post-occlusion instances. Using LocoTrackAI, we analyzed the locomotor activity of dengue-infected and noninfected mosquitoes across 3.24 million recorded mosquito positions. Results revealed that infected mosquitoes exhibited significantly higher locomotor activity (p = 0.0009), with 95,726 movements (0.30 mean locomotor activity) compared to 42,173 movements (0.13 mean locomotor activity) in noninfected mosquitoes, representing more than 200% of the activity of noninfected mosquitoes. Additionally, spatial analysis indicated a more extensive and uniform distribution for infected mosquitoes, with entropy values of 3.38 for infected and 3.13 for noninfected mosquitoes. These findings suggest that dengue infection increases locomotor activity and spatial exploration, potentially enhancing the mosquitoes' capacity to locate hosts and spread the virus. Future studies could expand on this work by investigating the locomotor effects of other arboviruses and further developing tools to automate the analysis of feeding and other critical behaviors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Monitoring the locomotor activity of mosquitoes is vital for understanding their behavioral patterns and role in disease transmission. Studies investigating the locomotor activity of dengue-infected mosquitoes have faced several limitations, including confining mosquitoes within small tubes that restrict natural movement, discontinuous recordings that fail to provide detailed activity patterns, and the lack of open-source tools to effectively monitor mosquito locomotor activity.

Here, we present LocoTrackAI, a robust artificial intelligence-based tool that leverages a convolutional neural network (CNN) and a multi-object tracking algorithm to comprehensively analyze the locomotor activity of Aedes aegypti mosquitoes from videos recorded using standard laboratory cages. The tool features a Skip Frame function to improve computational efficiency, adjustable movement thresholds for customized sensitivity, and a user-friendly interface that supports unsupervised batch processing, ensuring accuracy and flexibility for diverse research applications.
Main results
The LocoTrackAI achieved 99.91% accuracy with a low centroid detection error of 0.22 pixels across 36,020 frames and demonstrated a 93.23% success rate in reassigning identities during 207 post-occlusion instances. Results revealed that infected mosquitoes exhibited significantly higher locomotor activity (p = 0.0009), with 95,726 movements (0.30 mean locomotor activity) compared to 42,173 movements (0.13 mean locomotor activity) in noninfected mosquitoes, representing more than 200% of the activity of noninfected mosquitoes. Additionally, spatial analysis indicated a more extensive and uniform distribution for infected mosquitoes, with entropy values of 3.38 for infected and 3.13 for noninfected mosquitoes.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that dengue infection increases locomotor activity and spatial exploration, potentially enhancing the mosquitoes' capacity to locate hosts and spread the virus. Future studies could expand on this work by investigating the locomotor effects of other arboviruses and further developing tools to automate the analysis of feeding and other critical behaviors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The development of LocoTrackAI provides a powerful tool for monitoring mosquito behavior, which can enhance understanding of dengue transmission dynamics and inform vector control strategies. The increased locomotor activity in dengue-infected mosquitoes highlights the potential for targeted interventions to reduce mosquito-host contact and disease spread.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in laboratory settings using standard cages, which may not fully replicate natural environmental conditions. Despite high accuracy and statistical significance, the evidence strength is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the report and the need for further validation in field settings.

马来西亚半岛登革热时空分布及相对风险

Spatio-temporal Distribution and Relative Risk of Dengue Fever in Peninsular Malaysia
作者： Mohamad Afiq Amsyar Hamedin, Kamarul Imran Musa, Mohd Rahim Sulong
摘要中文翻译目标:登革热 仍然是马来西亚的一个主要公共卫生问题,具有明显的空间异质性和不断变化的时间动态。尽管近年来登革热的 发病率 大幅增加,但地区级风险模式及其随时间的变化仍然没有充分表征。本研究旨在评估马来西亚半岛登革热风险的时空分布。方法:利用 2022 年至 2024 年地区级每月病例数对登革热 监测 数据进行回顾性分析。登革热风险采用贝叶斯时空模型建模,结合了空间效应、时间效应和时空相互作用项。空间变化使用 Besag-York-Mollié 2 规范进行建模,而时间趋势则使用一阶随机游走进行建模。使用偏差信息准则(DIC)和渡边-赤池信息准则(WAIC)对竞争模型进行了比较。根据最佳拟合模型生成相对风险 (RR) 和超越概率图。结果:登革热 发病率 率在 2022 年之后显着增加,2023 年 发病率 率几乎翻倍,并在 2024 年保持较高水平。发病率 地图显示出明显的空间异质性,持续高 发病率 地区集中在城市和城郊地区。结合时空相互作用的时空模型大大优于加法模型,具有较低的 DIC 和 WAIC 值。最佳拟合模型确定了雪兰莪州内各地区的最高 RR,而槟城和柔佛州的几个地区的风险升高后验概率较高 [P(RR>1)>0.8]。结论:这项研究表明,马来西亚半岛登革热风险存在显着的空间异质性和不断变化的时间动态。贝叶斯时空疾病图谱提供了对登革热风险的稳健、局部、特定时间的估计,并可能支持有针对性的 监测 和流行病环境中的干预。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 仍然是马来西亚的一个主要公共卫生问题,具有明显的空间异质性和不断变化的时间动态。
设计与对象
本研究旨在评估马来西亚半岛登革热风险的时空分布。使用 2022 年至 2024 年地区级每月病例数对登革热 监测 数据进行回顾性分析。
核心方法
登革热风险采用贝叶斯时空模型建模,结合了空间效应、时间效应和时空相互作用项。空间变化使用 Besag-York-Mollié 2 规范进行建模,而时间趋势则使用一阶随机游走进行建模。
主要结果
相对风险 (RR) 和超越概率图是根据最佳拟合模型生成的。登革热 发病率 在 2022 年之后显着增加,发病率 发病率在 2023 年几乎翻倍,并在 2024 年保持在高位。
解释与创新
包含时空相互作用的竞争模型的 DIC 和 WAIC 值较低,其性能大大优于加性模型。
科研与公卫意义
贝叶斯时空疾病图谱提供了对登革热风险的稳健、局部、特定时间的估计,并可能支持有针对性的 监测 和流行病环境中的干预。
局限与证据强度
最佳拟合模型确定了雪兰莪州内各地区的最高 RR,而槟城和柔佛州的几个地区的风险升高的后验概率较高 [P(RR>1)>0.8]。

Spatio-temporal Distribution and Relative Risk of Dengue Fever in Peninsular Malaysia

Authors: Mohamad Afiq Amsyar Hamedin, Kamarul Imran Musa, Mohd Rahim Sulong
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: Dengue fever remains a major public health concern in Malaysia, with marked spatial heterogeneity and evolving temporal dynamics. Although the incidence of dengue has increased substantially in recent years, district-level risk patterns and their changes over time remain insufficiently characterized. This study aimed to assess the spatio-temporal distribution of dengue risk in Peninsular Malaysia.Methods: A retrospective analysis of dengue surveillance data was conducted using district-level monthly case counts from 2022 to 2024. Dengue risk was modeled with Bayesian spatio-temporal models incorporating spatial effects, temporal effects, and space–time interaction terms. Spatial variation was modeled using the Besag–York–Mollié 2 specification, while temporal trends were modeled using a first-order random walk. Competing models were compared using the deviance information criterion (DIC) and Watanabe–Akaike information criterion (WAIC). Relative risk (RR) and exceedance probability maps were generated from the best-fitting model.Results: Dengue incidence increased markedly after 2022, with incidence rates nearly doubling in 2023 and remaining high in 2024. Incidence maps showed pronounced spatial heterogeneity, with persistent high-incidence districts concentrated in urban and peri-urban regions. Spatio-temporal models incorporating space–time interaction substantially outperformed the additive model, with lower DIC and WAIC values. The best-fitting model identified the highest RRs in districts within Selangor, while several districts in Penang, and Johor had high posterior probabilities of elevated risk [P(RR>1)>0.8].Conclusions: This study demonstrates substantial spatial heterogeneity and evolving temporal dynamics in dengue risk across Peninsular Malaysia. Bayesian spatio-temporal disease mapping provides robust, localized, time-specific estimates of dengue risk and may support targeted surveillance and intervention in endemic settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever remains a major public health concern in Malaysia, with marked spatial heterogeneity and evolving temporal dynamics.
Design and population
This study aimed to assess the spatio-temporal distribution of dengue risk in Peninsular Malaysia. A retrospective analysis of dengue surveillance data was conducted using district-level monthly case counts from 2022 to 2024.
Core methods
Dengue risk was modeled with Bayesian spatio-temporal models incorporating spatial effects, temporal effects, and space–time interaction terms. Spatial variation was modeled using the Besag–York–Mollié 2 specification, while temporal trends were modeled using a first-order random walk.
Main results
Relative risk (RR) and exceedance probability maps were generated from the best-fitting model. Dengue incidence increased markedly after 2022, with incidence rates nearly doubling in 2023 and remaining high in 2024.
Interpretation and novelty
Competing models incorporating space–time interaction substantially outperformed the additive model, with lower DIC and WAIC values.
Scientific and public-health significance
Bayesian spatio-temporal disease mapping provides robust, localized, time-specific estimates of dengue risk and may support targeted surveillance and intervention in endemic settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The best-fitting model identified the highest RRs in districts within Selangor, while several districts in Penang, and Johor had high posterior probabilities of elevated risk [P(RR>1)>0.8].
