多毒株登革热模型中同源再感染的动态效应

Dynamical Effects of Homologous Reinfections in a Multi-Strain Dengue Model
作者： Akhil Kumar Srivastav, Vanessa Steindorf, Nico Stollenwerk, Bob W. Kooi, Maíra Aguiar, srivastav, A. K., Kooi, B. W.
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热 传播 是由多种病毒血清型、暂时交叉免疫 (TCI)、抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 以及流行人群中的反复暴露形成的。经典的多菌株模型通常假设终身保护免受相同血清型的再次感染。然而,最近的证据表明,同源登革热再感染虽然罕见,但却可能发生。它们对人口层面的影响仍然知之甚少。我们利用 TCI 和 ADE 介导的 传播 差异扩展了两种感染、两种菌株的登革热模型,以包括同源再感染。同源再感染由两个探索性参数表示:对相同血清型再感染的相对易感性和同源再感染期间的相对传染性。使用平衡分析、分岔图、模拟和相空间投影,我们研究了这些参数如何影响登革热动力学,以及在中等和长期 TCI 持续时间(有或没有季节性)下与 TCI 持续时间和季节性强迫相互作用。扩展模型表明,地方性登革热传播的定性动态特征主要在同源再感染的易感性较低时再现,因此同源再感染仍然很少见,但具有动态影响。较长的 TCI 拓宽了复杂振荡动力学的区域,而季节性改变了分叉结构,并使环面分叉成为复杂行为的中心路径。尽管当同源敏感性超出生物学意义范围时可能会发生后向分叉,但该结果应解释为数学机制而不是现实的登革热场景。这些结果表明,当与免疫史、TCI、ADE 介导的 传播 差异和季节变化相互作用时,罕见的同源再感染途径可以影响长期登革热动态。纳入此类途径可能会提高对高度暴露人群中复发性 暴发 和不规则 发病率 模式的理解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了人们对同源登革热再感染的人群水平后果知之甚少的问题,这种情况很少见,但却发生过,这对假设终身保护免受相同血清型再感染的经典多菌株模型提出了挑战。
设计与对象
该研究扩展了具有临时交叉免疫和 抗体依赖性增强 的两种感染、两种菌株登革热模型,以包括同源再感染,检查它们在各种条件下对登革热动态的影响。
核心方法
该研究利用平衡分析、分岔图、模拟和相空间投影,研究了同源再感染的易感性参数和再感染期间的传染性如何与临时交叉免疫持续时间和季节性强迫相互作用。
主要结果
扩展模型主要在同源再感染的敏感性较低时再现地方性登革热动态,使得再感染很少但具有动态影响;较长的临时交叉免疫拓宽了复杂振荡动力学的区域,而季节性则改变了分叉结构。
解释与创新
作者解释说,罕见的同源再感染途径在与免疫史、临时交叉免疫、抗体依赖性增强 介导的 抗体依赖性增强 差异和季节变化相互作用时,可以影响长期登革热动态,代表了对复发性 暴发 的新理解。
科研与公卫意义
纳入同源再感染途径可能会提高对高度暴露人群中复发性 暴发 和不规则 发病率 模式的理解,从而为 监测 和干预策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于数学模型而不是经验数据;向后分岔结果被解释为数学机制而不是现实的登革热场景,限制了普遍性。

Dynamical Effects of Homologous Reinfections in a Multi-Strain Dengue Model

Authors: Akhil Kumar Srivastav, Vanessa Steindorf, Nico Stollenwerk, Bob W. Kooi, Maíra Aguiar, srivastav, A. K., Kooi, B. W.
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue transmission is shaped by multiple viral serotypes, temporary cross-immunity (TCI), antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE), and repeated exposure in endemic populations. Classical multi-strain models usually assume lifelong protection against reinfection with the same serotype. However, recent evidence suggests that homologous dengue reinfections, although rare, can occur. Their population-level consequences remain poorly understood. We extend a two-infection, two-strain dengue model with TCI and ADE-mediated transmission differences to include homologous reinfections. Homologous reinfection is represented by two exploratory parameters: relative susceptibility to reinfection with the same serotype and relative infectiousness during homologous reinfection. Using equilibrium analysis, bifurcation diagrams, simulations, and phase-space projections, we examine how these parameters affect dengue dynamics and interact with TCI duration and seasonal forcing under intermediate and long TCI durations, with and without seasonality. The extended model shows that qualitative dynamics characteristic of endemic dengue transmission are reproduced mainly when susceptibility to homologous reinfection is low, so that homologous reinfections remain rare but dynamically influential. Longer TCI broadens regions of complex oscillatory dynamics, while seasonality shifts the bifurcation structure and makes torus bifurcations a central route to complex behavior. Although backward bifurcation can occur when homologous susceptibility exceeds the biologically meaningful range, this result should be interpreted as a mathematical mechanism rather than a realistic dengue scenario. These results indicate that rare homologous reinfection pathways can influence long-term dengue dynamics when interacting with immune history, TCI, ADE-mediated transmission differences, and seasonal variation. Incorporating such pathways may improve understanding of recurrent outbreaks and irregular incidence patterns in highly exposed populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the poorly understood population-level consequences of homologous dengue reinfections, which are rare but occur, challenging classical multi-strain models that assume lifelong protection against reinfection with the same serotype.
Design and population
The study extends a two-infection, two-strain dengue model with temporary cross-immunity and antibody-dependent enhancement to include homologous reinfections, examining their effects on dengue dynamics under various conditions.
Core methods
Using equilibrium analysis, bifurcation diagrams, simulations, and phase-space projections, the study examines how parameters for susceptibility to homologous reinfection and infectiousness during such reinfections interact with temporary cross-immunity duration and seasonal forcing.
Main results
The extended model reproduces endemic dengue dynamics mainly when susceptibility to homologous reinfection is low, making reinfections rare but dynamically influential; longer temporary cross-immunity broadens regions of complex oscillatory dynamics, while seasonality shifts bifurcation structure.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that rare homologous reinfection pathways can influence long-term dengue dynamics when interacting with immune history, temporary cross-immunity, antibody-dependent enhancement-mediated transmission differences, and seasonal variation, representing a novel understanding of recurrent outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
Incorporating homologous reinfection pathways may improve understanding of recurrent outbreaks and irregular incidence patterns in highly exposed populations, informing surveillance and intervention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on mathematical modeling rather than empirical data; backward bifurcation results are interpreted as mathematical mechanisms rather than realistic dengue scenarios, limiting generalizability.

HUBUNGAN 3M,OBAT NYAMUK DENGAN KEJADIAN DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE DI PUSKESMAS DEMPO PALEMBANG “3M”措施、驱蚊剂与巨港 Dempo 社区卫生中心登革出血热 发病率 之间的关系

HUBUNGAN 3M, OBAT NYAMUK DENGAN KEJADIAN DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE DI PUSKESMAS DEMPO PALEMBANG The Relationship Between the "3M" Measures, Mosquito Repellents, and the Incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever at the Dempo Community Health Center, Palembang
作者： Gerry Akino, Yazika Rimbawati, Ria Wulandari, Muhammad Romadhon
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革出血热(DBD)是一种由埃及伊蚊传播的严重传染病,如果不及时治疗可能致命。本研究旨在调查巨港 Dempo 社区卫生中心地区的“3M”预防措施与 DBD 发病率 驱蚊剂使用之间的关系。
设计与对象
2025 年,巨港 Dempo 社区健康中心进行了一项采用横断面设计的观察分析研究,涉及通过有目的抽样选出的 30 名受访者。
核心方法
通过问卷调查和观察收集数据,并使用卡方检验进行分析,以确定预防行为与 DBD 发病率 之间关联的显着性。
主要结果
发现“3M”行为与 DBD 发病率(p 值 0.001 < 0.05)之间以及驱蚊剂的使用与 DBD 发病率 之间(p 值 0.0002 < 0.05)之间存在显着关联。
解释与创新
作者解释说,持续应用“3M”措施并结合使用驱蚊剂可以降低 DBD 的风险,强调社区意识和纪律在预防中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持了促进“3M”行为和使用驱蚊剂作为社区卫生环境中预防登革热的有效策略的重要性。
局限与证据强度
由于样本量较小(30 名受访者)和横截面设计,证据强度中等,限制了因果推断。

HUBUNGAN 3M, OBAT NYAMUK DENGAN KEJADIAN DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE DI PUSKESMAS DEMPO PALEMBANG The Relationship Between the "3M" Measures, Mosquito Repellents, and the Incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever at the Dempo Community Health Center, Palembang

Authors: Gerry Akino, Yazika Rimbawati, Ria Wulandari, Muhammad Romadhon
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRAK Demam berdarah dengue (DBD) adalah penyakit menular akibat infeksi virus dengue yang ditularkan melalui gigitan nyamuk Aedes aegypti. Penyakit ini masih menjadi masalah kesehatan yang cukup serius karena dapat menimbulkan kondisi fatal bahkan menyebabkan kematian apabila tidak segera ditangani. Pencegahan sederhana dapat dilakukan dengan perilaku 3M, yakni menguras tempat penampungan air, menutup wadah air agar tidak menjadi sarang nyamuk, serta memanfaatkan atau mendaur ulang barang bekas. Selain itu, penggunaan obat nyamuk juga dianggap efektif untuk menurunkan risiko gigitan nyamuk penyebab DBD. Penelitian ini bertujuan mengetahui hubungan antara perilaku 3M dan kebiasaan penggunaan obat nyamuk dengan kejadian DBD di wilayah kerja Puskesmas Dempo Kota Palembang tahun 2025. Jenis penelitian menggunakan pendekatan analitik observasional dengan desain cross sectional. Sampel penelitian berjumlah 30 responden yang ditentukan melalui teknik purposive sampling. Data dikumpulkan dengan kuesioner dan observasi, lalu dianalisis menggunakan uji chi-square. Diperoleh hasil bahwa terdapat hubungan signifikan antara perilaku 3M dengan kejadian DBD (p-value 0,001 < 0,05). Begitu pula penggunaan obat nyamuk terbukti berhubungan signifikan dengan kejadian DBD (p-value 0,0002 < 0,05). Konsistensi dalam menerapkan perilaku 3M disertai penggunaan obat nyamuk dapat menurunkan risiko DBD. Masyarakat diharapkan lebih sadar serta disiplin dalam menjalankan langkah pencegahan.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue hemorrhagic fever (DBD) is a serious infectious disease transmitted by Aedes aegypti mosquitoes that can be fatal if not promptly treated. This study aimed to investigate the relationship between the "3M" prevention measures and mosquito repellent use with the incidence of DBD in the Dempo Community Health Center area, Palembang.
Design and population
An observational analytic study with a cross-sectional design was conducted in 2025 at the Dempo Community Health Center, Palembang, involving 30 respondents selected through purposive sampling.
Core methods
Data were collected using questionnaires and observation, and analyzed using the chi-square test to determine the significance of associations between preventive behaviors and DBD incidence.
Main results
A significant association was found between "3M" behavior and DBD incidence (p-value 0.001 < 0.05), and between mosquito repellent use and DBD incidence (p-value 0.0002 < 0.05).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that consistent application of "3M" measures combined with mosquito repellent use can reduce the risk of DBD, emphasizing the importance of community awareness and discipline in prevention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the importance of promoting "3M" behaviors and mosquito repellent use as effective strategies for dengue prevention in community health settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size (30 respondents) and the cross-sectional design, which limits causal inference.

病例报告:一名9岁儿童患者出现登革热伴消化道出血,并伴有疑似急性淋巴细胞白血病导致的全血细胞减少

Laporan Kasus: Demam Berdarah Dengue dengan Perdarahan Saluran Cerna pada Pasien Anak Usia 9 Tahun disertai Bisitopenia dengan Dugaan Leukemia Limfoblastik Akut
作者： Resti Ayu Jayanti, Herwanto Herwanto, Hesti Kartika Sari
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革出血热 (DHF) 仍然是热带国家儿科住院的主要原因,并可能发展为严重疾病,导致血浆渗漏、出血和循环性休克。然而,在无并发症的登革热感染中,严重的血细胞减少伴随外周血涂片的非典型表现并不常见,应提示进一步评估潜在的血液系统疾病。本 病例报告 旨在描述一名 DHF 儿科患者并发胃肠道出血和提示急性白血病的严重双细胞减少症的临床病程,说明初始评估期间非典型血液学表现的诊断挑战。使用患者的病历进行回顾性 病例报告,包括临床病史、体检、实验室检查、治疗 和临床结果。在有关 重症登革热 和儿童血液恶性肿瘤的当前证据的背景下,对研究结果进行了描述性分析和解释。一名 9 岁女孩有 5 天间歇性发热史,伴有恶心、呕吐、头痛、鼻衄、全身无力和黑便。实验室评估显示严重贫血(血红蛋白4.49 g/dL)、血小板减少(34 × 103/μL)、进行性白细胞减少,外周血涂片发现非典型单核细胞和污点细胞。患者诊断为DHF并发消化道出血,接受静脉液体复苏、浓缩红细胞输注、浓缩血小板输注及支持治疗。尽管进行了强化治疗,她还是出现了难治性休克、全身性癫痫发作、呼吸衰竭和心脏骤停。心肺复苏未成功,患者在住院24小时内死亡。严重的双细胞减少和异常的外周血涂片结果相结合,强烈怀疑潜在的急性血液恶性肿瘤,尽管由于临床迅速恶化而无法通过骨髓检查进行明确确认。DHF 儿科患者的严重贫血和双细胞减少症是非典型表现,需要对登革热相关并发症进行综合评估。早期识别提示潜在血液恶性肿瘤的血液学异常可能有助于及时诊断调查并优化类似病例的临床管理。关键词:登革出血热、胃肠道出血、双细胞减少、急性白血病、小儿 病例报告。摘要 Demam Berdarah 登革热 (DBD) masih menjadi salah satu penyebab utama rawat inap pada anak di negara tropis。Pada sebagian kasus、penyakit dapat Berkembang menjadi dengue berat yang ditandai dengan kebocoran Plasma、perdarahan、dan syok。登革热、登革热、登革热、血液病和血液病。为预防白血病的潜在临床症状,DBD 可能会导致血液减少和血液减少,并可能导致血液病的症状评估。请回顾我的病史、检查、检查、检查、检查和检查。数据分析包括桌面记录和相关文献,包括登革热和血液学分析。Seorang anak perempuan berusia 9 tahun datang dengan keluhan demam sejak lima hari sebelum masuk rumah sakit yang disertai mual、muntah、nyeri kepala、epistaksis、badan lemas、dan melena。Pemeriksaan 实验室菜单性贫血,血红蛋白 4,49 g/dL,血小板减少症 34.000/μL,白细胞减少症进展,单核细胞和污点细胞。诊断 DBD 的方法包括静脉内注射、输注浓缩红细胞 (PRC)、输注 trombosit konsentra、serta terapi suportif lainnya。请注意,请注意以下事项、注意事项、注意事项、注意事项。恢复健康后,会员会做出反应,并恢复健康。Adanya bisitopenia berat yang disertai temuan sel atipik pada darah tepi menimbulkan kuat terhadap keganasan hematologiberupa leukemia akut,meskipun konfirmasi Melalui pemeriksaan sumsum tulang belum sempat dilakukan karena perburukan klinis yang cepat.贫血和双体减少症会导致 DBD 升高,并影响血液学评估。本实验室提供了有关登革热治疗的详细信息,包括 诊断 和预防策略。Kata Kunci:Demam Berdarah 登革热、Perdarahan Saluran Cerna、Bisitopenia、Akut 白血病、Laporan Kasus。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革出血热 (DHF) 是热带地区儿科住院的主要原因,可发展为伴有出血和循环休克的严重疾病。该研究强调,在无并发症的登革热中,严重的血细胞减少和非典型外周血涂片检查结果并不常见,可能表明存在潜在的血液系统疾病。
设计与对象
使用单个儿科患者的病历进行回顾性 病例报告,包括临床病史、体检、实验室检查、治疗 和临床结果。患者为一名9岁女孩,患有DHF并发消化道出血和双细胞减少。
核心方法
该研究在 重症登革热 和儿童血液恶性肿瘤的当前证据背景下描述性分析了患者的临床病程和实验室检查结果。由于临床迅速恶化,未进行骨髓检查。
主要结果
实验室检查显示严重贫血(血红蛋白4.49 g/dL)、血小板减少(34 × 103/μL)、进行性白细胞减少,以及外周血涂片发现非典型单核细胞和涂抹细胞。尽管进行了强化治疗,患者仍发展为难治性休克、全身性癫痫发作、呼吸衰竭和心脏骤停,在住院后24小时内死亡。
解释与创新
作者将严重的双细胞减少和非典型外周血涂片结果解释为强烈提示潜在的急性血液恶性肿瘤,尽管不可能进行明确的确认。新颖之处在于阐明了 重症登革热 中非典型血液学表现所带来的诊断挑战。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要及早识别儿科 DHF 病例的血液学异常,以促进及时的诊断调查和优化临床管理。该病例强调了除登革热相关并发症之外的综合评估的重要性。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于单一回顾性 病例报告,这限制了普遍性并排除了因果推断。由于临床迅速恶化而缺乏骨髓检查进一步限制了结论的强度。该研究被评为低可信度。

Laporan Kasus: Demam Berdarah Dengue dengan Perdarahan Saluran Cerna pada Pasien Anak Usia 9 Tahun disertai Bisitopenia dengan Dugaan Leukemia Limfoblastik Akut

Authors: Resti Ayu Jayanti, Herwanto Herwanto, Hesti Kartika Sari
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF) remains a major cause of pediatric hospitalization in tropical countries and may progress to severe disease with plasma leakage, hemorrhage, and circulatory shock. However, profound cytopenia accompanied by atypical findings on peripheral blood smear is uncommon in uncomplicated dengue infection and should prompt further evaluation for underlying hematologic disorders. This case report aims to describe the clinical course of a pediatric patient with DHF complicated by gastrointestinal bleeding and severe bicytopenia suggestive of acute leukemia, illustrating the diagnostic challenge atypical hematological manifestations during the initial assessment. A retrospective case report was conducted using the patient's medical records, including clinical history, physical examination, laboratory investigations, treatment, and clinical outcomes. The findings were descriptively analyzed and interpreted in the context of current evidence regarding severe dengue and pediatric hematologic malignancies. A 9-year-old girl presented with a five-day history of intermittent fever accompanied by nausea, vomiting, headache, epistaxis, generalized weakness, and melena. Laboratory evaluation demonstrated severe anemia (hemoglobin 4.49 g/dL), thrombocytopenia (34 × 103/μL), progressive leukopenia, and peripheral blood smear findings of atypical mononuclear cells and smudge cells. The patient was diagnosed with DHF complicated by gastrointestinal bleeding and received intravenous fluid resuscitation, packed red blood cell transfusion, platelet concentrate transfusion, and supportive therapy. Despite intensive management, she developed refractory shock, generalized seizures, respiratory failure, and cardiac arrest. Cardiopulmonary resuscitation was unsuccessful, and the patient died within 24 hours of hospitalization. The combination of profound bicytopenia and abnormal peripheral blood smear findings raised a strong suspicion of an underlying acute hematologic malignancy, although definitive confirmation by bone marrow examination could not be performed because of the rapid clinical deterioration. Severe anemia and bicytopenia in pediatric patients with DHF represent atypical findings that warrant comprehensive evaluation beyond dengue-related complications. Early recognition of hematological abnormalities suggestive of an underlying hematologic malignancy may facilitate timely diagnostic investigation and optimize clinical management in similar cases. Keywords: Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever, Gastrointestinal Bleeding, Bicytopenia, Acute Leukemia, Pediatric Case Report. ABSTRAK Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) masih menjadi salah satu penyebab utama rawat inap pada anak di negara tropis. Pada sebagian kasus, penyakit dapat berkembang menjadi dengue berat yang ditandai dengan kebocoran plasma, perdarahan, dan syok. Adanya sitopenia berat yang tidak sesuai dengan gambaran klinis dengue pada umumnya perlu mendapat perhatian karena dapat mengindikasikan adanya kelainan hematologi yang mendasari. Melaporkan perjalanan klinis pasien anak dengan DBD disertai perdarahan saluran cerna dan bisitopenia yang mengarah pada suspek leukemia akut, serta menekankan pentingnya evaluasi lebih lanjut pada pasien dengan manifestasi hematologi yang tidak khas. Laporan kasus ini disusun secara retrospektif berdasarkan data rekam medis pasien yang meliputi anamnesis, pemeriksaan fisik, pemeriksaan penunjang, tata laksana, dan luaran klinis selama perawatan. Data dianalisis secara deskriptif dan dibandingkan dengan literatur terkini mengenai dengue berat dan keganasan hematologi pada anak. Seorang anak perempuan berusia 9 tahun datang dengan keluhan demam sejak lima hari sebelum masuk rumah sakit yang disertai mual, muntah, nyeri kepala, epistaksis, badan lemas, dan melena. Pemeriksaan laboratorium menunjukkan anemia berat dengan kadar hemoglobin 4,49 g/dL, trombositopenia 34.000/μL, leukopenia progresif, serta gambaran darah tepi berupa sel mononuklear atipik dan smudge cells. Pasien didiagnosis DBD dengan perdarahan saluran cerna dan mendapatkan terapi cairan intravena, transfusi packed red cells(PRC), transfusi trombosit konsentrat, serta terapi suportif lainnya. Selama perawatan, kondisi pasien memburuk dengan terjadinya syok refrakter, kejang umum, gagal nafas, dan henti jantung. Resusitasi jantung paru tidak memberikan respons sehingga pasien dinyatakan meninggal dunia. Adanya bisitopenia berat yang disertai temuan sel atipik pada darah tepi menimbulkan kecurigaan kuat terhadap keganasan hematologi berupa leukemia akut, meskipun konfirmasi melalui pemeriksaan sumsum tulang belum sempat dilakukan karena perburukan klinis yang cepat. Anemia berat dan bisitopenia pada pasien anak dengan DBD merupakan temuan yang tidak lazim dan memerlukan evaluasi lebih lanjut terhadap kemungkinan kelainan hematologi yang mendasari. Pengenalan dini terhadap temuan laboratorium yang tidak sesuai dengan perjalanan klinis dengue dapat membantu mempercepat penegakan diagnosis dan menentukan strategi penatalaksanaan yang lebih komprehensif. Kata Kunci: Demam Berdarah Dengue, Perdarahan Saluran Cerna, Bisitopenia, Leukemia Akut, Laporan Kasus.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF) is a leading cause of pediatric hospitalization in tropical regions and can progress to severe disease with hemorrhage and circulatory shock. The study highlights that profound cytopenia with atypical peripheral blood smear findings is uncommon in uncomplicated dengue and may indicate underlying hematologic disorders.
Design and population
A retrospective case report was conducted using a single pediatric patient's medical records, including clinical history, physical examination, laboratory investigations, treatment, and clinical outcomes. The patient was a 9-year-old girl with DHF complicated by gastrointestinal bleeding and bicytopenia.
Core methods
The study descriptively analyzed the patient's clinical course and laboratory findings in the context of current evidence on severe dengue and pediatric hematologic malignancies. No bone marrow examination was performed due to rapid clinical deterioration.
Main results
Laboratory evaluation revealed severe anemia (hemoglobin 4.49 g/dL), thrombocytopenia (34 × 103/μL), progressive leukopenia, and peripheral blood smear findings of atypical mononuclear cells and smudge cells. Despite intensive management, the patient developed refractory shock, generalized seizures, respiratory failure, and cardiac arrest, leading to death within 24 hours of hospitalization.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the profound bicytopenia and atypical peripheral blood smear findings as strongly suggestive of an underlying acute hematologic malignancy, though definitive confirmation was not possible. The novelty lies in illustrating the diagnostic challenge posed by atypical hematological manifestations in severe dengue.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the need for early recognition of hematological abnormalities in pediatric DHF cases to facilitate timely diagnostic investigation and optimize clinical management. This case underscores the importance of comprehensive evaluation beyond dengue-related complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a single retrospective case report, which restricts generalizability and precludes causal inference. The lack of bone marrow examination due to rapid clinical deterioration further constrains the strength of the conclusions. The study is rated as low confidence.

通过基于香茅叶的草药蚊香生产进行社区赋权以预防登革热(DBD)的社区赋权

Edukasi dan pelatihan PEMBERDAYAAN MASYARAKAT MELALUI PRODUKSI OBAT NYAMUK HERBAL BERBASIS DAUN SERAI PENCEGAHAN DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE (DBD) PEMBERDAYAAN MASYARAKAT MELALUI PRODUKSI OBAT NYAMUK HERBAL BERBASIS DAUN SERAI PENCEGAHAN DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE (DBD)
作者： Heldi Candra, Fifin Oktaviani, Aldhehita Ari Kusumawati, Alysa Dwirini, Marsyanda Azzahra Rizqy
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Demam Berdarah 登革热 (DBD) 仍然是巴淡市的一个公共卫生问题,特别是在卡苏岛沿海地区,因为获得医疗服务的机会有限,并且高度依赖含有敌敌畏和避蚊胺的合成驱蚊剂,这带来了 媒介 耐药性和健康问题的风险。
设计与对象
这项社区服务活动(PKM)于 2026 年 5 月 9 日在巴淡岛卡苏岛开展,对象包括库尔德工人党母亲、posyandu 干部和 Karang Taruna 等 30 名参与者,与 Puskesmas Belakang Padang 和 Kelurahan Pulau Kasu 合作。
核心方法
该实施采用社区赋权方法,分四个阶段进行:活动前、DBD 流行病学 和 3M Plus PSN 的互动咨询、通过浸渍制作 70% 乙醇驱蚊喷雾的培训,以及使用 15 项测试前/测试后问卷进行评估。
主要结果
活动显示,参与者的知识得分平均从 54.7 提高到 86.3,提高了 57.8%,所有参与者都成功生产了 600 毫升 15% 草药喷雾剂,具有良好的感官特性,每 100 毫升的生产成本为 Rp5,000–Rp7,000。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,通过以柠檬草 (Cymbopogon nardus L.) 为基础的草本驱蚊剂生产来增强社区能力,有效提高了预防 DBD 的知识、技能和自力更生,为发展基于社区的中小微企业作为可持续发展措施提供了机会。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为资源有限地区的 DBD 预防提供了一种可持续、低成本的替代方案,减少对合成驱虫剂的依赖,并通过社区企业促进当地经济发展。
局限与证据强度
由于样本量小、评估时间短,证据强度中等;然而,该研究展示了在类似环境下基于社区的登革热预防的实用且可扩展的方法。

Edukasi dan pelatihan PEMBERDAYAAN MASYARAKAT MELALUI PRODUKSI OBAT NYAMUK HERBAL BERBASIS DAUN SERAI PENCEGAHAN DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE (DBD) PEMBERDAYAAN MASYARAKAT MELALUI PRODUKSI OBAT NYAMUK HERBAL BERBASIS DAUN SERAI PENCEGAHAN DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE (DBD)

Authors: Heldi Candra, Fifin Oktaviani, Aldhehita Ari Kusumawati, Alysa Dwirini, Marsyanda Azzahra Rizqy
Original abstract (English)
Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) masih menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat di Kota Batam, khususnya wilayah pesisir Pulau Kasu, Kecamatan Belakang Padang, akibat keterbatasan akses layanan kesehatan dan tingginya ketergantungan terhadap obat nyamuk sintetis berbahan diklorvos dan DEET yang berisiko menimbulkan resistensi vektor serta gangguan kesehatan penggunanya. Kegiatan pengabdian kepada masyarakat (PKM) ini bertujuan meningkatkan pengetahuan dan keterampilan masyarakat pesisir dalam memproduksi obat nyamuk herbal berbasis daun serai (Cymbopogon nardus L.) sebagai alternatif pencegahan DBD yang aman, murah, dan berkelanjutan. Kegiatan dilaksanakan pada 9 Mei 2026 di Pulau Kasu, Kota Batam, dengan sasaran 30 peserta yang terdiri atas ibu-ibu PKK, kader posyandu, dan Karang Taruna, bermitra dengan Puskesmas Belakang Padang dan Kelurahan Pulau Kasu. Metode pelaksanaan menggunakan pendekatan pemberdayaan masyarakat (community empowerment) melalui empat tahap: pra-kegiatan, penyuluhan interaktif tentang epidemiologi DBD dan PSN 3M Plus, pelatihan pembuatan spray anti nyamuk metode maserasi etanol 70%, serta evaluasi pre-test/post-test menggunakan kuesioner 15 butir. Hasil kegiatan menunjukkan peningkatan rata-rata skor pengetahuan peserta dari 54,7 menjadi 86,3 atau meningkat sebesar 57,8%, dan seluruh peserta berhasil memproduksi 600 mL spray herbal konsentrasi 15% dengan karakteristik organoleptis yang baik serta harga pokok produksi Rp5.000–Rp7.000 per 100 mL. Disimpulkan bahwa pemberdayaan masyarakat melalui produksi obat nyamuk herbal berbasis daun serai efektif meningkatkan pengetahuan, keterampilan, dan kemandirian masyarakat pesisir dalam pencegahan DBD serta membuka peluang pengembangan UMKM warga sebagai bentuk keberlanjutan program.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) remains a public health problem in Batam City, particularly in the coastal area of Pulau Kasu, due to limited access to healthcare services and high dependence on synthetic mosquito repellents containing dichlorvos and DEET, which pose risks of vector resistance and health issues.
Design and population
This community service activity (PKM) was conducted on May 9, 2026, in Pulau Kasu, Batam City, targeting 30 participants consisting of PKK mothers, posyandu cadres, and Karang Taruna, in partnership with Puskesmas Belakang Padang and Kelurahan Pulau Kasu.
Core methods
The implementation used a community empowerment approach through four stages: pre-activity, interactive counseling on DBD epidemiology and 3M Plus PSN, training in making ethanol 70% mosquito repellent spray via maceration, and evaluation using a 15-item pre-test/post-test questionnaire.
Main results
The activity showed an average increase in participants' knowledge scores from 54.7 to 86.3, a 57.8% improvement, and all participants successfully produced 600 mL of 15% herbal spray with good organoleptic characteristics and a production cost of Rp5,000–Rp7,000 per 100 mL.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that empowering communities through herbal mosquito repellent production based on lemongrass (Cymbopogon nardus L.) effectively improved knowledge, skills, and self-reliance in DBD prevention, opening opportunities for developing community-based MSMEs as a sustainability measure.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a sustainable, low-cost alternative for DBD prevention in resource-limited areas, reducing reliance on synthetic repellents and fostering local economic development through community-based enterprises.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and short-term evaluation; however, the study demonstrates a practical, scalable approach for community-based dengue prevention in similar settings.

意大利朝鲜伊蚊 媒介 对 登革病毒 能力的调查

Investigation of Aedes koreicus vector competence for dengue virus in Italy
作者： Fabrizio Montarsi, Emanuela Dal Molin, Marco Di Luca, Claudia Fortuna, Federica Toniolo, Sofia Sgubin, Tamara Garziera, Francesco Gradoni, Monia Pacenti, Luisa Barzon
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
欧洲登革热 发病率 的增加,加上入侵蚊种的传播,引起了人们对新 媒介 出现的担忧。本研究旨在首次评估意大利伊蚊群体的 媒介 能力。koreicus 为 登革病毒 血清型 1 (DENV-1)。
设计与对象
实验室培养的 Ae。来自意大利东北部的雌性 koreicus 通过人工血粉经口感染 DENV-1。将蚊子维持在 26 ± 1 °C,并在感染后 7、14、21 和 28 天 (dpi) 进行分析。
核心方法
通过 RT-qPCR 和液滴数字 PCR 评估感染、传播和 传播,同时通过 Vero E6 细胞中的病毒分离证实唾液中感染性病毒的存在。
主要结果
Aedes koreicus was susceptible to DENV-1 infection, with infection rates ranging from 30 to 60% across time points. Viral dissemination to legs and wings was detected from 14 dpi onwards, reaching 100% at 21 and 28 dpi. DENV-1 RNA was detected in saliva at 28 days post-infection (dpi), indicating a relatively long extrinsic incubation period.
解释与创新
朝鲜伊蚊被证明对 DENV-1 感染敏感,并且能够传播病毒,但效率低且外源性潜伏期长。尽管存在这些限制,但其不断增加的丰度、寿命和以人类为食的能力表明,在有利的条件下,它可能充当潜在的登革热 媒介。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了将 Ae 纳入其中的必要性。koreicus 在欧洲昆虫学 监测 和登革热 传播 风险评估中的应用。
局限与证据强度
由于该研究基于实验室的性质以及观察到的 传播 效率相对较低,因此证据强度中等。该研究是在受控条件下进行的,可能无法完全反映现实世界的 传播 动态。

Investigation of Aedes koreicus vector competence for dengue virus in Italy

Authors: Fabrizio Montarsi, Emanuela Dal Molin, Marco Di Luca, Claudia Fortuna, Federica Toniolo, Sofia Sgubin, Tamara Garziera, Francesco Gradoni, Monia Pacenti, Luisa Barzon
Original abstract (English)
This study aimed to assess, for the first time, the vector competence of an Italian population of Ae. Methods Laboratory-reared Ae. Results Aedes koreicus was susceptible to DENV-1 infection, with infection rates ranging from 30 to 60% across time points. These findings highlight the need to include Ae. Graphical abstract Abbreviations - CHIKV: - Chikungunya virus - CI: - Confidence interval - CPE: - Cytopathic effect - Ct: - Cycle threshold - ddPCR: - Droplet digital PCR - DMEM: - Dulbecco’s Modified Eagle Medium - DENV: - Dengue virus - DENV-1: - Dengue virus serotype 1 - dpi: - Days post-infection - DR: - Dissemination rate - ECDC: - European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control - EIP: - Extrinsic incubation period - FBS: - Fetal bovine serum - IF: - Immunofluorescence - IR: - Infection rate - MOI: - Multiplicity of infection - NTC: - No-template control - PBS: - Phosphate saline buffer - PCR: - Polymerase chain reaction - RNA: - Ribonucleic acid - RT-PCR: - Reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction - RT-qPCR: - Reverse transcription quantitative polymerase chain reaction - TCID50 : - Tissue culture infectious dose 50 - TE: - Transmission efficiency - TR: - Transmission rate - VC: - Vector capacity - WHO: - World Health Organization - WNV: - West Nile virus - ZIKV: - Zika virus Acknowledgements We thank the colleagues of IZSVe for their assistance in mosquito rearing especially Lidia Iustina Danca, Sara Manzi, Sara Carlin, Elena Porcellato, and colleagues Maria Augusta Bozza and Franco Mutinelli for their assistance in BSL-3 activities. Abstract Background The increasing incidence of dengue in Europe, combined with the spread of invasive mosquito species, raises concerns about the emergence...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The increasing incidence of dengue in Europe, combined with the spread of invasive mosquito species, raises concerns about the emergence of new vectors. This study aimed to assess, for the first time, the vector competence of an Italian population of Ae. koreicus for dengue virus serotype 1 (DENV-1).
Design and population
Laboratory-reared Ae. koreicus females originating from northeastern Italy were orally infected with DENV-1 via an artificial blood meal. Mosquitoes were maintained at 26 ± 1 °C and analysed at 7, 14, 21 and 28 days post-infection (dpi).
Core methods
Infection, dissemination and transmission were evaluated by RT-qPCR and droplet digital PCR, while the presence of infectious virus in saliva was confirmed by virus isolation in Vero E6 cells.
Main results
Aedes koreicus was susceptible to DENV-1 infection, with infection rates ranging from 30 to 60% across time points. Viral dissemination to legs and wings was detected from 14 dpi onwards, reaching 100% at 21 and 28 dpi. DENV-1 RNA was detected in saliva at 28 days post-infection (dpi), indicating a relatively long extrinsic incubation period.
Interpretation and novelty
Aedes koreicus was shown to be susceptible to DENV-1 infection and capable of transmitting the virus, although with low efficiency and a long extrinsic incubation period. Despite these limitations, its increasing abundance, longevity and capacity to feed on humans suggest that it may act as a potential dengue vector under favourable conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need to include Ae. koreicus in entomological surveillance and risk assessments for dengue transmission in Europe.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the laboratory-based nature of the study and the relatively low transmission efficiency observed. The study was conducted under controlled conditions, which may not fully reflect real-world transmission dynamics.

登革热流行病学和社会人口学视角:米纳斯吉拉斯州的现状分析

A dengue sob uma perspectiva epidemiológica e sociodemográfica: uma análise sobre o estado de Minas Gerais
作者： Maria Cristina Vasconcelos Teixeira
摘要中文翻译巴西登革热流行病学背景是当地和流行病期间的变化,代表了公众的持续发展。我们对米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热流行病学和社会人口学的总体分析进行了客观分析,并与乌贝兰迪亚的市政部门、最近的科学文献证据的一部分进行了研究。本书是有关研究的文献、描述和探索,是在国家和国际基础上实现的。我们的结果是,在过去的一段时间内,我们与社会环境、地区因素以及对萨乌德系统产生了重大影响。结论是米纳斯吉拉斯州长期存在的登革热问题,是流行病学、控制媒介和相互间交流的综合策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了巴西登革热面临的持续挑战,其特点是流行期和流行病 暴发 之间的振荡,旨在分析米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热的流行病学和社会人口学概况,重点是乌贝兰迪亚。
设计与对象
这是一项描述性和探索性的书目研究,使用国家和国际数据库进行,以分析米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热的流行病学和社会人口学概况,重点关注乌贝兰迪亚。
核心方法
该研究采用描述性和探索性的文献研究方法,利用国家和国际数据库收集和分析有关登革热的最新科学证据。
主要结果
结果表明,在一年中的某些时段,登革热的 发病率 较高,与社会环境因素、区域不平等密切相关,并对统一卫生系统产生重大影响。
解释与创新
作者解释说,登革热仍然是米纳斯吉拉斯州的一个重大公共卫生问题,需要采取流行病学 监测、媒介 控制和跨部门行动的综合策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要采取综合的 监测 和控制策略,强调解决社会环境因素和区域不平等以减轻登革热对卫生系统影响的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对书目研究的依赖而受到限制,书目研究可能无法捕获所有当前或本地数据;然而,最近科学证据的使用为研究结果提供了适度的可信度。

A dengue sob uma perspectiva epidemiológica e sociodemográfica: uma análise sobre o estado de Minas Gerais

Authors: Maria Cristina Vasconcelos Teixeira
Original abstract (English)
O contexto epidemiológico da dengue no Brasil caracteriza-se por oscilações entre períodos endêmicos e surtos epidêmicos, representando um desafio contínuo à saúde pública. Este estudo tem como objetivo geral analisar o perfil epidemiológico e sociodemográfico da dengue no estado de Minas Gerais, com ênfase no município de Uberlândia, a partir de evidências da literatura científica recente. Trata-se de uma pesquisa bibliográfica, de caráter descritivo e exploratório, realizada em bases de dados nacionais e internacionais. Os resultados indicam elevada incidência da doença em determinados períodos do ano, forte associação com fatores socioambientais, desigualdades regionais e impacto significativo sobre o Sistema Único de Saúde. Conclui-se que a dengue permanece como um importante problema de saúde pública em Minas Gerais, exigindo estratégias integradas de vigilância epidemiológica, controle vetorial e ações intersetoriais.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the continuous challenge of dengue in Brazil, characterized by oscillations between endemic periods and epidemic outbreaks, aiming to analyze the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of dengue in Minas Gerais, with emphasis on Uberlândia.
Design and population
This is a descriptive and exploratory bibliographic research conducted using national and international databases to analyze the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of dengue in Minas Gerais, focusing on Uberlândia.
Core methods
The study employed a descriptive and exploratory bibliographic research approach, utilizing national and international databases to gather and analyze recent scientific evidence on dengue.
Main results
The results indicate a high incidence of dengue during certain periods of the year, a strong association with socio-environmental factors, regional inequalities, and a significant impact on the Unified Health System.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that dengue remains a significant public health problem in Minas Gerais, requiring integrated strategies for epidemiological surveillance, vector control, and intersectoral actions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for integrated surveillance and control strategies, highlighting the importance of addressing socio-environmental factors and regional inequalities to mitigate dengue's impact on the health system.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on bibliographic research, which may not capture all current or local data; however, the use of recent scientific evidence provides moderate confidence in the findings.

流行病学专家

De veranderende epidemiologie van vectorziekten
作者： P. Lacor
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了 媒介 传播疾病的 媒介 变化,重点关注欧洲外来蚊媒感染的出现以及气候变化在促进其传播和 媒介 传播疾病中的作用。
设计与对象
这是发表在 Tijdschrift voor Geneeskunde en Gezondheidszorg 上的叙述性评论文章,讨论了在没有原始数据收集的情况下 媒介 传播疾病的全球和欧洲流行病学趋势。
核心方法
该综述综合了有关 媒介 传播疾病的现有文献和 监测 数据,包括 MEMO 和 MEMO+ 等项目,以分析外来蚊子和本土 传播 模式在欧洲的传播。
主要结果
Vector-borne diseases represent 17% of all infectious diseases globally, causing over 700,000 deaths annually; Europe has seen a marked increase in incidence and distribution of diseases like dengue and chikungunya, with autochthonous transmission documented in Italy, Croatia, France, and Spain.
解释与创新
作者将气候变化解释为白纹伊蚊等外来 媒介 在欧洲建立的关键驱动因素,并指出 媒介 引入后 5-15 年出现当地 媒介 的模式,挑战了蚊媒感染仅作为“输入”疾病的看法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要修改发热性疾病的鉴别诊断,将本地传播的蚊媒感染纳入其中,并增强 监测 系统以预测非流行地区未来的 监测。
局限与证据强度
证据基于观察数据和文献综述,限制因果推断;然而,全球和欧洲数据的一致性支持对报告趋势进行适度评估。

De veranderende epidemiologie van vectorziekten

Authors: P. Lacor
Original abstract (English)
Het augustusnummer van het Tijdschrift voor Geneeskunde en Gezondheidszorg bevat een bijdrage over virusinfecties met “exotische” muggen in de rol van vector (1). Intuïtief maken wij dan de associatie met verre reizen. Maar, er is meer aan de hand. “Vector-borne diseases” of vectorziekten vertegenwoordigen 17% van alle infectieziekten wereldwijd en zijn verantwoordelijk voor meer dan 700.000 overlijdens per jaar (2, 3). Die vectoren zijn artropoden (zoals muggen en teken) die pathogene micro-organismen (virussen, parasieten of bacteriën) kunnen overdragen op een gastheer wanneer zij een bloedmaaltijd gebruiken (2). Het gaat over koortsige ziekten zoals bijvoorbeeld malaria, gele koorts, dengue, chikungunya en zika. Het risico om zo een infectie op te lopen, is het grootst in die delen van de wereld met een warm en vochtig klimaat (3). Maar, in de voorbije 2 decennia was er een merkelijke toename in incidentie en verdeling van sommige van deze ziekten in Europa (4, 5). Dat heeft deels te maken met de toenemende globalisering, meer intercontinentaal reisverkeer en handelstranport wereldwijd, wat de verspreiding van vectoren in de hand werkt (4, 6). Terugkerende reizigers kunnen inderdaad een door vectoren overgebrachte infectie oplopen als “importziekte” omdat zij tijdens hun verblijf in een endemische streek gestoken werden door exotische muggen. Maar toch wordt ook in Europa in toenemende mate “autochtone transmissie” van door muggen overdraagbare pathogenen beschreven (7). De transmissie gebeurt dan doordat een teruggekeerde reiziger met viremie in eigen land geprikt wordt door een mug die een geschikte vector is, daarbij het pathogene micro-organisme opneemt en het dan doorgeeft aan een nog niet geïnfecteerd iemand bij een volgende prik (6). Een essentiële voorwaarde voor dit mechanisme is uiteraard dat de vector zich in het nieuwe land gevestigd heeft en zich daar kan handhaven. Meer en meer wordt aangenomen dat de klimaatverandering in belangrijke mate dit process aandrijft (8, 9). Hogere temperaturen, andere neerslagpatronen en zachtere winters zouden de vestiging en verspreiding van vectorartropoden in voordien niet-endemische regio’s mogelijk maken. Het gaat dan vooral om muggen van het species Aedes, Anopheles en Culex, en “sandflies” van het species Phlebotomus (8). Het ectothermische karakter van deze artropoden, die de eigen lichaamstemperatuur niet kunnen reguleren, maakt hen zeer gevoelig aan externe klimaatomstandigheden. Daarnaast is de omgevingstemperatuur mee bepalend voor de maturatie en replicatie van de pathogenen die door die atropoden meegedragen worden (10, 11). Op dezelfde manier wordt overigens ook de natuurlijke epidemiologie beïnvloed van ziekten die reeds autochtoon waren in Europa, zoals leishmaniasis, met sandflies als vector, en infectie met West Nile-virus, met Culex-muggen als vector (8). Aedes albopictus of de Aziatische tijgermug, vector van arbovirussen zoals dengue en chikungunya, is een voorbeeld bij uitstek van een exotische muggensoort die zich gestaag blijft verspreiden over het Europese continent na daar voor het eerst verschenen te zijn in de late jaren 70. Zo werd de eerste autochtone uitbraak van chikungunya in Europa gedocumenteerd in Italië in 2007, en van dengue in Kroatië in 2010. Later volgden herhaalde uitbraken van deze ziekten in Italië, Frankrijk en Spanje (6, 12). België ligt op het pad van de verder noordwaartse verplaatsing van deze mug, die zich er in toenemende mate vestigt en ook overwintert. Dat is gebleken uit projecten van surveillance, zoals MEMO en MEMO+ (acroniem voor “Monitoring of Exotic Mosquitoes”) als onderdeel van het groter “National Environmental Health Action Plan” of NEHAP (13, 14, 15). Observaties in andere delen van de wereld hebben aangetoond dat plaatselijke uitbraken van virusziekte overgebracht door Aedes-muggen zich typisch voordeden met een interval van 5 tot 15 jaar nadat deze muggensoort zich gevestigd had (5, 16). Europese data lijken de mogelijkheid van een dergelijk patroon te ondersteunen, want zij tonen een sterke toename van autochtone gevallen van dengue in landen waar Aedes albopictus al sinds meerdere jaren gedijt (5). Deze bevindingen moeten ons er dus toe aanzetten om de kans op een ernstige infectieziekte overgebracht door muggen niet meer louter als een “ver-van-mijn-bedhypothese” weg te dringen wanneer in de anamnese een typische hint zoals “terugkeer van een verre reis” ontbreekt. Want hoewel nu misschien nog geen vaste prik in onze standaardlijst van differentiaaldiagnosen van een koortsige ziekte, kan de optie van een door muggen overdraagbare infectie in de toekomst mogelijk vaker het overwegen waard worden. Patrick Lacor, hoofdredacteur
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the changing epidemiology of vector-borne diseases, focusing on the emergence of exotic mosquito-borne infections in Europe and the role of climate change in facilitating their establishment and transmission.
Design and population
This is a narrative review article published in the Tijdschrift voor Geneeskunde en Gezondheidszorg, discussing global and European epidemiological trends of vector-borne diseases without primary data collection.
Core methods
The review synthesizes existing literature and surveillance data on vector-borne diseases, including projects like MEMO and MEMO+, to analyze the spread of exotic mosquitoes and autochthonous transmission patterns in Europe.
Main results
Vector-borne diseases represent 17% of all infectious diseases globally, causing over 700,000 deaths annually; Europe has seen a marked increase in incidence and distribution of diseases like dengue and chikungunya, with autochthonous transmission documented in Italy, Croatia, France, and Spain.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret climate change as a key driver for the establishment of exotic vectors like Aedes albopictus in Europe, noting a pattern of local outbreaks occurring 5-15 years after vector introduction, challenging the perception of mosquito-borne infections as solely 'imported' diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings underscore the need to revise differential diagnoses for febrile illnesses to include locally transmitted mosquito-borne infections and enhance surveillance systems to anticipate future outbreaks in non-endemic regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on observational data and literature review, limiting causal inference; however, the consistency of global and European data supports a moderate assessment of the reported trends.

Journal: bioRxiv  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-04  |  DOI: 10.64898/2026.08.04.742677  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window

序列同源性和组织向性决定了昆虫特异性 Binjari-Zika 病毒嵌合体介导的埃及伊蚊中寨卡病毒的重复感染排除

Sequence Homology and Tissue Tropism Determine Superinfection Exclusion of Zika virus in Aedes aegypti Mediated by an insect-specific Binjari-Zika Virus Chimera
作者： Willemsen, W., Peterson, A. J., Henkens, M., Smid, H. M., Rohlf, H. J., Visser, T. M., Koenraadt, C. J., Hall, R. A., van Oers, M. M., Hobson-Peters, J.
摘要中文翻译登革热、寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅病毒等虫媒病毒会引起广泛的疾病,并由于气候变化和 媒介 传播而继续扩大其地理范围。昆虫特异性黄病毒 (ISF) 是有希望的虫媒病毒生物防治候选者,因为最近的研究表明,先前感染 ISF 可以通过重复感染排除 (SIE) 减少蚊子中虫媒病毒的复制。然而,蚊子中 ISF 的感染途径、组织向性、发病机制 和 SIE 的机制仍不清楚。RNA 干扰 (RNAi) 是昆虫中一种有效的 抗病毒药物 反应,因此预计 ISF 和虫媒病毒之间的序列同源性将加强 SIE。在这里,我们使用 ISF Binjari 病毒和包含寨卡病毒结构蛋白 prME (BinJ-ZIKV) 的嵌合体作为模型系统。埃及伊蚊胸腔内注射 BinJ-ZIKV 导致快速全身感染,排除中肠,随后阻止 ZIKV 从中肠传播。SIE 在原发病毒复制最高的组织中最强。当 ISF 和虫媒病毒之间存在序列同源性时,SIE 的这种空间成分更强,并表现出强烈的 21nt siRNA 响应,表明 RNAi 对观察到的 SIE 做出了贡献。口服接种后,BinJ-ZIKV 在蚊子体内有效复制,并在多个组织和唾液中检测到。BinJ-ZIKV 在较低的口服滴度下也比 BinJV 具有更高的感染建立率。在口服暴露干扰随后的 ZIKV 中肠感染后,观察到 BinJ-ZIKV 感染的 SIE。总之,这些发现支持工程 ISF 嵌合体作为有价值的实验工具来剖析 SIE 的病毒决定因素并优化基于蚊子的虫媒病毒干扰策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究蚊子重复感染排除(SIE)的机制,特别关注序列同源性和组织向性如何影响虫媒病毒的SIE。登革病毒 会引起广泛的疾病,由于其地理范围不断扩大,引起了重大的公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
该研究使用了一个模型系统,涉及埃及伊蚊中的昆虫特异性 Binjari 病毒和 Binjari-Zika 病毒嵌合体 (BinJ-ZIKV)。该研究通过胸腔注射和口服接种BinJ-ZIKV来观察其对后续ZIKV感染的影响。
核心方法
研究人员采用 RNA 干扰 (RNAi) 作为一种机制来分析蚊子的 抗病毒药物 反应。他们胸腔内注射 BinJ-ZIKV 并口服接种,以评估其对 ZIKV 传播和复制的影响。
主要结果
胸腔内注射 BinJ-ZIKV 导致快速全身感染,排除中肠,从而阻止 ZIKV 传播。SIE 在原发病毒复制最高的组织中最强,并且当 ISF 和虫媒病毒之间存在序列同源性时,SIE 的空间成分更强。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,序列同源性和组织向性是决定 SIE 的关键因素,其中 RNAi 发挥着重要作用。这项研究为 SIE 的机制提供了新的见解,并强调了工程 ISF 嵌合体在理解 SIE 病毒决定因素方面的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对于开发基于蚊子的干扰虫媒病毒 传播 的策略具有重要意义,可能会改进针对登革热和其他相关病毒的生物防治方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象分析,可能无法捕获详细的实验程序或结果。证据是中等的,因为它基于一般观察,不包括具体的数值数据或统计分析。

Sequence Homology and Tissue Tropism Determine Superinfection Exclusion of Zika virus in Aedes aegypti Mediated by an insect-specific Binjari-Zika Virus Chimera

Authors: Willemsen, W., Peterson, A. J., Henkens, M., Smid, H. M., Rohlf, H. J., Visser, T. M., Koenraadt, C. J., Hall, R. A., van Oers, M. M., Hobson-Peters, J.
Original abstract (English)
Arboviruses such as dengue, Zika, and chikungunya viruses cause widespread disease and continue to expand their geographical range due to climate change and vector spread. Insect-specific flaviviruses (ISFs) are promising biocontrol candidates of arboviruses, due to recent studies showing that prior infection with an ISF can reduce arbovirus replication in mosquitoes through superinfection exclusion (SIE). However, the route of infection, tissue tropism, pathogenesis and the mechanisms underlying SIE of ISFs in mosquitoes remain unclear. RNA interference (RNAi) is a potent antiviral response in insects, therefore it is expected that sequence homology between the ISF and the arbovirus will strengthen SIE. Here, we used ISF Binjari virus and a chimera containing the Zika virus structural proteins prME (BinJ-ZIKV) as a model system. Intrathoracic injection of BinJ-ZIKV in Aedes aegypti led to rapid systemic infection that excluded the midgut, subsequently blocking ZIKV dissemination from the midgut. SIE was strongest in tissues where primary-virus replication was highest. This spatial component of SIE was stronger when there was sequence homology between the ISF and arbovirus and displayed a strong 21nt siRNA response, suggesting RNAi contributed to the observed SIE. Upon oral inoculation, BinJ-ZIKV replicated efficiently in mosquitoes, was detected across multiple tissues, and saliva. BinJ-ZIKV also had higher infection establishment than BinJV at lower oral titres. SIE was observed for BinJ-ZIKV infection after oral exposure interfered with subsequent ZIKV midgut infection. Together, these findings support engineered ISF-chimeras as valuable experimental tools to dissect viral determinants of SIE and to optimize mosquito-based arbovirus interference strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the mechanisms of superinfection exclusion (SIE) in mosquitoes, particularly focusing on how sequence homology and tissue tropism influence SIE of arboviruses. Dengue viruses, which cause widespread disease, are of significant public health concern due to their expanding geographical range.
Design and population
The study used a model system involving the insect-specific Binjari virus and a Binjari-Zika virus chimera (BinJ-ZIKV) in Aedes aegypti mosquitoes. The research was conducted through intrathoracic injection and oral inoculation of BinJ-ZIKV to observe its effects on subsequent ZIKV infection.
Core methods
The researchers employed RNA interference (RNAi) as a mechanism to analyze the antiviral response in mosquitoes. They injected BinJ-ZIKV intrathoracically and orally inoculated it to assess its impact on ZIKV dissemination and replication.
Main results
Intrathoracic injection of BinJ-ZIKV led to rapid systemic infection that excluded the midgut, thereby blocking ZIKV dissemination. SIE was strongest in tissues where primary-virus replication was highest, and the spatial component of SIE was stronger when there was sequence homology between the ISF and arbovirus.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that sequence homology and tissue tropism are critical factors in determining SIE, with RNAi playing a significant role. This study provides novel insights into the mechanisms of SIE and highlights the potential of engineered ISF-chimeras for understanding viral determinants of SIE.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results have implications for developing mosquito-based strategies to interfere with arbovirus transmission, potentially leading to improved biocontrol methods against dengue and other related viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis, which may not capture detailed experimental procedures or outcomes. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on general observations and does not include specific numerical data or statistical analyses.

分子分析和计算模型揭示了内皮细胞中 DENV 诱导的可溶性因子触发的时间上可分离的反应

Molecular analysis and computational modeling reveal temporally separable responses triggered by DENV-induced soluble factors in endothelial cells
作者： Jenny Paola Alfaro-García, Julieta María Ramírez-Mejía, Paola Rojas-Estevez, Diego Alejandro Álvarez-Díaz, Geysson Javier Fernández, Carlos Alberto Orozco-Castaño, Boris Anghelo Rodríguez-Rey, Juan Carlos Gallego-Gómez, Miguel Vicente-Manzanares, Paola Rojas-Estévez
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 (DENV) 代表着日益严重的全球健康挑战,数十亿人面临风险。重症登革热 (SD) 是 DENV 感染的一种并发症,涉及全身出血,至少部分是由内皮功能障碍引起的。内皮功能障碍是指由于炎症、机械损伤和/或内皮细胞遗传程序的改变而导致的通透性增加。先前的研究表明,将内皮细胞暴露于来自 DENV 感染细胞 (CMDV) 的条件培养基中会增加通透性和细胞硬度,抑制内皮标记并诱导间充质基因。然而,这些事件对 SD 发病的普遍性、程度、机制和最终影响仍然难以捉摸。在这里,我们将内皮细胞的体外 治疗 分析与含有 UV 灭活 DENV 的培养基与计算模型相结合,以研究 CMDV 诱导的内皮改变的关键特征及其对内皮功能障碍的潜在影响。我们发现CMDV增加了SNA1和CDH2的表达,同时抑制了内皮基因OCLN和CDH5。全球转录组学分析显示,CMDV 引发短暂的促炎症反应,随后诱导选定的组织修复基因和基质重塑。非定向异步网络模型 (NDAM-CMDV) 确定 IL6 和 FN1 是 DENV 诱导的内皮转分化的中心节点,为预测疾病的 进化 并确定潜在的治疗靶点提供了新的分子见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
重症登革热 (DENV) 与由内皮功能障碍驱动的 重症登革热 (SD) 构成了重大的全球健康负担,但将 DENV 诱导的可溶性因子与内皮改变和 SD 发病联系起来的机制仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
该研究使用内皮细胞的体外 治疗 和来自 DENV 感染细胞 (CMDV) 的条件培养基,并将转录组学与计算模型相结合来研究内皮反应。
核心方法
将内皮细胞暴露于 CMDV,并进行全局转录组分析以评估基因表达变化,然后构建非定向异步网络模型 (NDAM-CMDV) 以识别中央调节节点。
主要结果
CMDV 暴露增加了 SN​​A1 和 CDH2 的表达,同时抑制内皮基因 OCLN 和 CDH5,并引发短暂的促炎症反应,随后诱导组织修复和基质重塑基因。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 DENV 诱导的可溶性因子驱动内皮转分化的证据,其中 IL6 和 FN1 被确定为预测疾病 进化 和潜在治疗靶点的网络模型中的中心节点。
科研与公卫意义
该研究表明,靶向 IL6 和 FN1 通路可能会减轻登革热的内皮功能障碍,为未来的治疗策略和 重症登革热 的 监测 方法提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于体外模型和计算预测,缺乏体内验证或临床相关性,需要谨慎解释和进一步的实验证实。

Molecular analysis and computational modeling reveal temporally separable responses triggered by DENV-induced soluble factors in endothelial cells

Authors: Jenny Paola Alfaro-García, Julieta María Ramírez-Mejía, Paola Rojas-Estevez, Diego Alejandro Álvarez-Díaz, Geysson Javier Fernández, Carlos Alberto Orozco-Castaño, Boris Anghelo Rodríguez-Rey, Juan Carlos Gallego-Gómez, Miguel Vicente-Manzanares, Paola Rojas-Estévez
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) represents a growing global health challenge with billions of people at risk. Severe Dengue (SD), a complication of DENV infection that involves generalized hemorrhage, is driven, at least in part, by endothelial dysfunction. Endothelial dysfunction refers to increased permeability due to inflammation, mechanical injury and/or modification of the genetic program of endothelial cells. Previous work showed that exposure of endothelial cells to conditioned media from DENV-infected cells (CMDV) increased permeability and cellular stiffness, repressed endothelial markers and induced mesenchymal genes. However, the generality, extent, mechanism and ultimate impact of these events in the onset of SD remain elusive. Here, we integrate analysis from in vitro treatment of endothelial cells with media containing UV-inactivated DENV with computational modeling to investigate the key features of CMDV-induced endothelial alterations and their potential impact on endothelial dysfunction. We found that CMDV increased SNA1 and CDH2 expression, while suppressing endothelial genes OCLN and CDH5 . Global transcriptomics analysis revealed that CMDV triggered a transient pro-inflammatory response, followed by induction of selected tissue repair genes and matrix remodeling. A non-directed asynchronous network model (NDAM-CMDV) identified IL6 and FN1 as central nodes of DENV-induced endothelial trans-differentiation, providing new molecular insights that predict the evolution of the disease and identify potential therapeutic targets.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) poses a major global health burden with severe dengue (SD) driven by endothelial dysfunction, yet the mechanisms linking DENV-induced soluble factors to endothelial alterations and SD onset remain poorly understood.
Design and population
The study used in vitro treatment of endothelial cells with conditioned media from DENV-infected cells (CMDV) and integrated transcriptomics with computational modeling to investigate endothelial responses.
Core methods
Endothelial cells were exposed to CMDV, and global transcriptomic profiling was performed to assess gene expression changes, followed by construction of a non-directed asynchronous network model (NDAM-CMDV) to identify central regulatory nodes.
Main results
CMDV exposure increased SNA1 and CDH2 expression while suppressing endothelial genes OCLN and CDH5, and triggered a transient pro-inflammatory response followed by induction of tissue repair and matrix remodeling genes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that DENV-induced soluble factors drive endothelial trans-differentiation, with IL6 and FN1 identified as central nodes in a network model that predicts disease evolution and potential therapeutic targets.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study suggests that targeting IL6 and FN1 pathways may mitigate endothelial dysfunction in dengue, informing future therapeutic strategies and surveillance approaches for severe dengue.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to in vitro models and computational predictions, lacking in vivo validation or clinical correlation, warranting cautious interpretation and further experimental confirmation.

母体虫媒病毒感染后胎盘结构和转运蛋白表达的病毒特异性改变。

Virus-specific alterations in placental structure and transporter expression following maternal arbovirus infection.
作者： Cherley Borba Vieira de Andrade, Enrrico Bloise, Natália L C Silva, Jair R. S. Braga, Sharton Vinícius Antunes Coelho, Verônica Müller de Oliveira Nascimento, Guinever E. Imperio, Camila O. S. Nunes, Cunha AJLA, Marcelo A Pascoal-Xavier
摘要中文翻译我们假设虫媒病毒会损害与胎儿大脑发育有关的关键功能标记物的胎盘表达。对受基孔肯雅热(CHIKV,n = 12)、登革热(DENV,n = 8)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV,n = 15)或健康对照(HC,n = 15)感染的单胎胎盘(n = 54)进行了分析。对关键转运系统、细胞周转标记物以及与细胞外基质重塑、血管维护和营养传感相关的信号通路进行免疫组织化学定量。与 HC 相比,CHIKV/ZIKV 孕妇的母亲年龄和 BMI 较低,而 DENV 妊娠的出生体重较低。CHIKV/ZIKV 表现出合体滋养层面积减少,CHIKV/DENV 表现出合胞结面积减少,而 DENV 表现出胎儿血管增加和结缔组织面积减少。组织病理学检测到各组主要有轻度血管和间质病变,100% 的病例中 DENV 胎盘表现出中度充血。所有组中均检测到 P-gp、BCRP、ABCA1 和 SNAT2 表达下降。CHIKV/ZIKV 也表现出 GLUT1 表达减少,而 ZIKV 表现出 SNAT1 表达减少。MCT8 表达在 CHIKV 中显着升高。CHIKV/ZIKV 中胎盘 Ki-67 增加,表明滋养层周转发生改变。在所有暴露于虫媒病毒的胎盘中,VEGFB 表达减少,p-mTOR 信号传导增加,而 MMP9 表达没有变化。主成分分析(PCA)根据与胎儿大脑发育相关的胎盘功能标记物(而不是组织形态计量学)区分感染组和未感染组。虫媒病毒感染的胎盘表现出明显的结构重塑和转运蛋白表达的改变,即使没有新生儿症状,也可能增加胎儿大脑的脆弱性和长期神经发育风险。要点:检测到病毒特异性胎盘组织形态学变化,包括合体滋养层/合胞结、结缔组织和胎儿血管区域的改变。虫媒病毒减少胎盘流出转运蛋白 P-糖蛋白 (P-gp) 和乳腺癌抗性蛋白 (BCRP),并破坏 ABCA1、SNAT2、GLUT1 和 SNAT4 营养转运蛋白的表达,而 ZIKV 还降低 SNAT1 表达。胎盘对虫媒病毒感染的功能适应包括胎盘增殖标记物 (Ki-67)、血管生成 (VEGFB) 和营养感应 (p-mTOR) 表达的变化。虫媒病毒暴露的胎盘表现出更大的功能改变,而不是明显的结构病理学改变,这表明即使在没有重大新生儿异常的情况下,也可能对胎儿神经发育产生潜在的亚临床影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了母体虫媒病毒感染,包括 登革病毒 (DENV),是否会改变与胎儿大脑发育相关的胎盘结构和转运蛋白表达,其动机是对病毒特异性胎盘病理学的理解存在差距。
设计与对象
对受基孔肯雅热(CHIKV,n = 12)、登革热(DENV,n = 8)、寨卡病毒(ZIKV,n = 15)或健康对照(HC,n = 15)影响的妊娠的单胎胎盘(n = 54)进行了分析。
核心方法
对转运系统、细胞周转标记物、信号通路以及与细胞外基质重塑、血管维护和营养传感相关的组织形态学特征进行免疫组织化学定量。
主要结果
与HC相比,DENV妊娠的出生体重降低,且DENV胎盘表现出胎儿血管面积增加和结缔组织面积减少;所有组中均检测到 P-gp、BCRP、ABCA1 和 SNAT2 表达下降。
解释与创新
作者解释说,暴露于虫媒病毒的胎盘表现出病毒特异性的结构重塑和转运蛋白表达的改变,这表明即使没有新生儿症状,也可能对胎儿神经发育产生潜在的亚临床影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,DENV 感染可能会导致胎盘变化,从而影响胎儿生长和长期神经发育风险,为 监测 和研究重点提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小、横截面设计以及缺乏机制或纵向神经发育随访的限制,需要谨慎解释和进一步研究。

Virus-specific alterations in placental structure and transporter expression following maternal arbovirus infection.

Authors: Cherley Borba Vieira de Andrade, Enrrico Bloise, Natália L C Silva, Jair R. S. Braga, Sharton Vinícius Antunes Coelho, Verônica Müller de Oliveira Nascimento, Guinever E. Imperio, Camila O. S. Nunes, Cunha AJLA, Marcelo A Pascoal-Xavier
Original abstract (English)
We hypothesized that arboviruses impair the placental expression of key functional markers implicated in fetal brain development. Singleton placentae (n = 54) affected by chikungunya (CHIKV, n = 12), dengue (DENV, n = 8), zika (ZIKV, n = 15), or healthy controls (HC, n = 15) were analysed. Immunohistochemical quantification of key transporter systems, cell turnover markers, and signalling pathways related to extracellular matrix remodelling, vascular maintenance, and nutrient sensing was undertaken. Maternal age and BMI were lower in CHIKV/ZIKV, whereas birth weight was reduced in DENV pregnancies compared with HC. CHIKV/ZIKV exhibited reduced syncytiotrophoblast area, CHIKV/DENV showed reduced syncytial knot area, whereas DENV showed increased fetal blood vessel and reduced connective tissue areas. Histopathology detected predominantly mild vascular and stromal lesions across groups, with DENV placentae exhibiting moderate congestion in 100% of cases. Decreased expression of P-gp, BCRP, ABCA1 and SNAT2 were detected in all groups. CHIKV/ZIKV also exhibited decreased GLUT1, while ZIKV showed reduced SNAT1 expression. MCT8 expression was significantly elevated in CHIKV. Placental Ki-67 was increased in CHIKV/ZIKV, indicating altered trophoblast turnover. VEGFB expression was reduced and p-mTOR signalling was increased across all arbovirus-exposed placentae, while MMP9 expression was unchanged. Principal components analysis (PCA) distinguished infected from uninfected groups based on placental functional markers related to fetal brain development, but not on histomorphometry. Arboviral-infected placentae showed distinct structural remodelling and altered transporter expression, potentially increasing fetal brain vulnerability and long-term neurodevelopmental risks, even in the absence of neonatal symptoms. KEY POINTS: Virus-specific placental histomorphometric changes were detected, including altered syncytiotrophoblast/syncytial knot, connective tissue and fetal blood vessel areas. Arboviruses reduced the placental efflux transporters P-glycoprotein (P-gp) and breast cancer resistance protein (BCRP) and disrupted the expression of ABCA1, SNAT2, GLUT1 and SNAT4 nutrient transporters, while ZIKV additionally reduced SNAT1 expression. Placental functional adaptation to arboviral infection comprised changes in the expression of placental markers of proliferation (Ki-67), angiogenesis (VEGFB), and nutrient sensing (p-mTOR). Arbovirus-exposed placentae exhibited greater functional alterations rather than overt structural pathology, suggesting potential subclinical effects on fetal neurodevelopment, even in the absence of major neonatal abnormalities.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed whether maternal arbovirus infection, including dengue virus (DENV), alters placental structure and transporter expression relevant to fetal brain development, motivated by gaps in understanding virus-specific placental pathology.
Design and population
Singleton placentae (n = 54) from pregnancies affected by chikungunya (CHIKV, n = 12), dengue (DENV, n = 8), zika (ZIKV, n = 15), or healthy controls (HC, n = 15) were analysed.
Core methods
Immunohistochemical quantification of transporter systems, cell turnover markers, signalling pathways, and histomorphometric features related to extracellular matrix remodelling, vascular maintenance, and nutrient sensing was performed.
Main results
Birth weight was reduced in DENV pregnancies compared with HC, and DENV placentae showed increased fetal blood vessel area and reduced connective tissue area; decreased expression of P-gp, BCRP, ABCA1 and SNAT2 was detected in all groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that arbovirus-exposed placentae exhibit virus-specific structural remodelling and altered transporter expression, suggesting potential subclinical effects on fetal neurodevelopment even without neonatal symptoms.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings imply that DENV infection may contribute to placental changes that could influence fetal growth and long-term neurodevelopmental risk, informing surveillance and research priorities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size, cross-sectional design, and lack of mechanistic or longitudinal neurodevelopmental follow-up, warranting cautious interpretation and further research.

用于标准化且经济高效的自适应免疫受体库测序 (AIRRseq) 的等深度池化

Equi-depth pooling for standardized and cost-effective Adaptive Immune Receptor Repertoire sequencing (AIRRseq)
作者： Thomas-Wolf Verdonckt, Sahulcik M, Caroline Struyfs, Anne-Sofie Vermeersch, Koen Bartholomeeusen, Filip Van Nieuwerburgh, Ole Lagatie, Arien KK, Marek Sahulčík, Kevin K. Ariën
摘要中文翻译批量 B 和 T 细胞适应性免疫受体 (BCR/TCR) 全谱测序 (AIRRseq) 能够全面分析适应性免疫多样性,但在异质临床样本中实现平衡的测序深度仍然是主要的成本驱动因素。测序前的标准等摩尔合并会不成比例地分配读数,导致序列丰度较高的文库测序不足,而序列较少的文库则会过采样。这里描述了一种定量合并策略,其中基于估计的目标特异性唯一分子标识符 (UMI) 计数而不是文库摩尔浓度来合并文库,确保跨样本的每个目标序列 UMI 的支持读取数量一致。使用 NEBNext 免疫测序 (IS) 试剂盒从人类 RNA 中制备文库,并在第二个扩增 (PCR2) 步骤中通过 qPCR 进行定量。使用来自登革热 1 人类感染模型 (DHIM1) 临床研究的深度测序样本来模拟 PCR2 循环阈值 (Ct) 值和检测到的序列计数之间的对数线性关系。然后将该模型应用于来自登革热 3 人类感染模型 (DHIM3) 临床研究的第二批独立样本,以预测 UMI 丰度并指导测序前的等深度合并。这些等深度池的测序证明了样本之间几乎均匀的每序列读取比率,证实了这种方法可以在不过度采样的情况下以降低的成本实现平衡的深度。对反映真实世界样本池的一系列样本间 UMI 计数分布进行的模拟表明,与等摩尔池相比,等深度池预计可将所需测序读取总数减少约 70%,并且随着文库大小分布的扩大,节省量也会增加。除了成本效率之外,等深度合并还消除了等摩尔合并固有的复杂性相关采样偏差,确保了小型和大型文库的统一测序深度和可比较的 UMI 回收率。对于新的工作流程或样本类型,实施需要现有的校准数据集或试点测序实验来建立 PCR2 Ct 值和相对 UMI 丰度之间的关系。因此,等深度方法为文库大小差异很大的批量 AIRRseq 研究提供了一种稳健、可扩展且经济高效的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了在批量 B 和 T 细胞适应性免疫受体库测序 (AIRRseq) 中实现跨异质临床样本的平衡测序深度的挑战,这仍然是主要的成本驱动因素。
设计与对象
该研究使用来自登革热1人类感染模型(DHIM1)临床研究的深度测序样本来模拟PCR2循环阈值(Ct)值和检测到的序列计数之间的关系,并将该模型应用于来自登革热3人类感染模型(DHIM3)临床研究的独立批次样本。
核心方法
使用 NEBNext 免疫测序 (IS) 试剂盒从人类 RNA 中制备文库,并在第二个扩增 (PCR2) 步骤中通过 qPCR 进行定量。采用定量合并策略,根据估计的目标特异性唯一分子标识符 (UMI) 计数合并文库,以确保跨样本的每个目标序列 UMI 的读数一致。
主要结果
等深度池的测序证明了样本之间近乎均匀的每序列读取比率,从而以降低的成本确认了平衡的深度,而无需过采样。模拟显示,与等摩尔合并相比,等深度合并将所需测序读取的总数减少了约 70%,并且随着文库大小扩展,节省的金额也在增加。
解释与创新
作者将等深度池方法解释为一种稳健、可扩展且经济高效的批量 AIRRseq 研究策略,其中文库大小差异很大,消除了等摩尔池固有的复杂性相关的采样偏差。
科研与公卫意义
这种方法可实现经济高效且标准化的 AIRRseq 分析,通过确保一致的测序深度和跨样本的可比较的 UMI 恢复率,可以增强登革热等传染病的免疫库研究。
局限与证据强度
实施需要现有的校准数据集或试点测序实验来建立新工作流程或样本类型的 PCR2 Ct 值与相对 UMI 丰度之间的关系。由于依赖建模和模拟数据,证据强度中等。

Equi-depth pooling for standardized and cost-effective Adaptive Immune Receptor Repertoire sequencing (AIRRseq)

Authors: Thomas-Wolf Verdonckt, Sahulcik M, Caroline Struyfs, Anne-Sofie Vermeersch, Koen Bartholomeeusen, Filip Van Nieuwerburgh, Ole Lagatie, Arien KK, Marek Sahulčík, Kevin K. Ariën
Original abstract (English)
Bulk B- and T-cell adaptive immune receptor (BCR/TCR) repertoire sequencing (AIRRseq) enables comprehensive analysis of adaptive immune diversity, but achieving balanced sequencing depth across heterogeneous clinical samples remains a major cost driver. Standard equimolar pooling prior to sequencing disproportionately allocates reads, leading to undersequencing of libraries with high sequence abundance and oversampling of those with few sequences. Here, a quantitative pooling strategy is described in which libraries are pooled based on estimated target-specific unique molecular identifier (UMI) counts rather than library molarity, ensuring a uniform number of supporting reads per target sequence UMI across samples. Libraries were prepared from human RNA using the NEBNext Immune Sequencing (IS) kit and quantified by qPCR during the second amplification (PCR2) step. Deeply sequenced samples from a dengue 1 human infection model (DHIM1) clinical study were used to model the log-linear relationship between PCR2 cycle threshold (Ct) values and detected sequence counts. This model was then applied to a second, independent batch of samples from a dengue 3 human infection model (DHIM3) clinical study to predict UMI abundance and guide equi-depth pooling prior to sequencing. Sequencing of these equi-depth pools demonstrated a near-uniform reads-per-sequence ratio across samples, confirming that this approach achieves balanced depth without oversampling and at reduced cost. Simulations across a range of between-sample UMI-count distributions reflecting real-world sample pools showed that equi-depth pooling is expected to reduce the total number of required sequencing reads by approximately 70% compared with equimolar pooling, with savings increasing as the spread of library sizes grows. Beyond cost efficiency, equi-depth pooling eliminates the complexity-dependent sampling bias inherent to equimolar pooling, ensuring uniform sequencing depth and comparable UMI recovery across small and large libraries. For new workflows or sample types, implementation requires either an existing calibration dataset or a pilot sequencing experiment to establish the relationship between PCR2 Ct values and relative UMI abundance. The equi-depth method thus provides a robust, scalable, and cost-efficient strategy for bulk AIRRseq studies where library sizes vary widely.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the challenge of achieving balanced sequencing depth across heterogeneous clinical samples in bulk B- and T-cell adaptive immune receptor repertoire sequencing (AIRRseq), which remains a major cost driver.
Design and population
The research used deeply sequenced samples from a dengue 1 human infection model (DHIM1) clinical study to model the relationship between PCR2 cycle threshold (Ct) values and detected sequence counts, and applied this model to an independent batch of samples from a dengue 3 human infection model (DHIM3) clinical study.
Core methods
Libraries were prepared from human RNA using the NEBNext Immune Sequencing (IS) kit and quantified by qPCR during the second amplification (PCR2) step. A quantitative pooling strategy was employed where libraries were pooled based on estimated target-specific unique molecular identifier (UMI) counts to ensure uniform reads per target sequence UMI across samples.
Main results
Sequencing of equi-depth pools demonstrated a near-uniform reads-per-sequence ratio across samples, confirming balanced depth without oversampling at reduced cost. Simulations showed equi-depth pooling reduces the total number of required sequencing reads by approximately 70% compared with equimolar pooling, with savings increasing as the spread of library sizes grows.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the equi-depth pooling method as a robust, scalable, and cost-efficient strategy for bulk AIRRseq studies where library sizes vary widely, eliminating complexity-dependent sampling bias inherent to equimolar pooling.
Scientific and public-health significance
This approach enables cost-effective and standardized AIRRseq analysis, which can enhance immune repertoire studies in infectious diseases like dengue by ensuring uniform sequencing depth and comparable UMI recovery across samples.
Limitations and evidence strength
Implementation requires either an existing calibration dataset or a pilot sequencing experiment to establish the relationship between PCR2 Ct values and relative UMI abundance for new workflows or sample types. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on modeling and simulation data.

埃及伊蚊近亲繁殖、适应性和细菌微生物组之间的相互作用。

Interactions Between Inbreeding, Fitness and the Bacterial Microbiome in Aedes aegypti Mosquitoes.
作者： Perran A. Ross, Nadieh de Jonge, Qiong Yang, Véronique Paris, Torsten N. Kristensen, Ary A. Hoffmann
摘要中文翻译摘要 实验室和野外昆虫种群可能分别由于近交衰退和遗传漂变而经历适应性下降和遗传多样性丧失。相关个体之间的交配和小种群规模也可能影响昆虫 宿主 微生物组,从而对健康产生影响。在登革热 媒介 蚊子(埃及伊蚊)中,细菌微生物组很大程度上是由环境决定的,但最近的研究也揭示了 宿主 的遗传成分。我们从伊蚊的两个创始远交种群中生成了一组 55 个近交系。埃及伊蚊使用 ddRADseq 和细菌 16S rRNA 基因测序对蚊子池测试生活史特征、近交、等位基因多样性和微生物组组成之间的关联。近亲繁殖的影响是多种多样的,尽管遗传多样性水平相似,但许多品系的综合适应度成本很高,但其他品系的成本却很小。我们发现近交系的主要生活史性状之间没有很强的关系,这表明近交造成的任何成本都是性状特异性的。对来自部分品系子集的汇集样本进行的细菌微生物组分析揭示了跨品系的常见微生物,其中伊丽莎白金氏菌属、气单胞菌属和罗尔斯通氏菌属最丰富。尽管细菌组成差异很大,但微生物组组成与适应性或种群起源之间没有明确的关系。然而,不同品系微生物类群的相对丰度之间存在一些显着的正相关性。我们的结果表明,近亲繁殖对蚊子种群的适应性有多种影响,但对微生物组的影响有限。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了由于近交抑制和遗传漂变导致的昆虫种群适应性下降和遗传多样性丧失,以及这些因素如何影响细菌微生物组,从而对适应性产生影响。
设计与对象
该研究从埃及伊蚊的两个远交种群中产生了 55 个近交系,以测试生活史特征、近交、等位基因多样性和微生物组组成之间的关联。
核心方法
该研究使用 ddRADseq 和细菌 16S rRNA 基因测序对蚊子池进行分析,分析生活史特征、近亲繁殖、等位基因多样性和微生物组组成之间的关联。
主要结果
近亲繁殖的影响是多种多样的,尽管遗传多样性水平相似,但许多品系的综合适应度成本很高,但其他品系的成本却很小;跨自交系的主要生活史特征之间没有很强的关系,细菌微生物组分析揭示了跨系的常见微生物,而微生物组组成与适应性或群体起源之间没有明确的关系。
解释与创新
作者解释说,近亲繁殖对蚊子种群的适应性有多种影响,但对微生物组的影响有限,这表明近亲繁殖造成的任何成本都是特定于性状的。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于了解近亲繁殖如何影响蚊子的适应性和微生物组组成,这与埃及伊蚊的 媒介 控制策略和管理 登革病毒 传播 相关。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于来自两个创始种群的近交系,虽然观察到的近交对健康的影响有强有力的证据,但其与微生物组组成的关系尚不明确,这表明微生物组研究结果的证据强度中等。

Interactions Between Inbreeding, Fitness and the Bacterial Microbiome in Aedes aegypti Mosquitoes.

Authors: Perran A. Ross, Nadieh de Jonge, Qiong Yang, Véronique Paris, Torsten N. Kristensen, Ary A. Hoffmann
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Laboratory and field populations of insects can experience a decline in fitness and loss of genetic diversity due to inbreeding depression and genetic drift, respectively. Matings among related individuals and small population size may also influence insect host microbiomes with consequences for fitness. In the dengue vector mosquito, Aedes aegypti , the bacterial microbiome is largely environmentally determined, but recent studies have also revealed host genetic components. We generated a panel of 55 inbred lines from either of two founding outbred populations of Ae. aegypti to test for associations between life history traits, inbreeding, allelic diversity, and microbiome composition using ddRADseq and bacterial 16S rRNA gene sequencing on pools of mosquitoes. Effects of inbreeding were diverse, with severe composite fitness costs in many lines but minimal costs in others despite similar low levels of genetic diversity. We found no strong relationship between major life history traits across inbred lines, suggesting that any costs due to inbreeding were trait specific. Bacterial microbiome analysis of pooled samples from a subset of lines revealed common microbes across lines, with Elizabethkingia , Aeromonas , and Ralstonia being the most abundant. Despite bacterial composition varying widely, there was no clear relationship between microbiome composition and fitness or population origin. However, there were several significant positive correlations between the relative abundance of different microbial taxa across lines. Our results demonstrate diverse impacts of inbreeding on the fitness of mosquito populations but with limited impacts on the microbiome.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the decline in fitness and loss of genetic diversity in insect populations due to inbreeding depression and genetic drift, and how these factors might influence the bacterial microbiome with consequences for fitness.
Design and population
The research generated a panel of 55 inbred lines from two founding outbred populations of Aedes aegypti mosquitoes to test associations between life history traits, inbreeding, allelic diversity, and microbiome composition.
Core methods
The study used ddRADseq and bacterial 16S rRNA gene sequencing on pools of mosquitoes to analyze associations between life history traits, inbreeding, allelic diversity, and microbiome composition.
Main results
Effects of inbreeding were diverse, with severe composite fitness costs in many lines but minimal costs in others despite similar low levels of genetic diversity; there was no strong relationship between major life history traits across inbred lines, and bacterial microbiome analysis revealed common microbes across lines with no clear relationship between microbiome composition and fitness or population origin.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that inbreeding has diverse impacts on the fitness of mosquito populations but with limited impacts on the microbiome, demonstrating that any costs due to inbreeding were trait specific.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform understanding of how inbreeding affects mosquito fitness and microbiome composition, which is relevant for vector control strategies and managing dengue virus transmission by Aedes aegypti mosquitoes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to inbred lines from two founding populations, and while the evidence is strong for the observed effects of inbreeding on fitness, the relationship with microbiome composition was less clear, suggesting moderate evidence strength for microbiome findings.

非洲之角登革热和基孔肯雅热的 患病率:系统回顾和荟萃分析

Prevalence of Dengue and Chikungunya in the Horn of Africa: A Systematic Review and Meta-analysis
作者： Aamir Muse Osman, Mercy Mwenya, Francis Oketch Ochieng, Hellen Ayilo Akengo, Joel Lutomiah, Konongoi S. Limbaso, Ahmed Abdulkadir Hassan-Kadle, Fredrick Ouma Odhiambo, Marian Muse Osman, Bernard Bett
摘要中文翻译背景:登革病毒(DENV)和基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)是重要的蚊媒虫媒病毒,其全球分布不断扩大。尽管自 2000 年代初以来,非洲之角就经常发生 暴发,但其 流行病学 和负担的特征仍不清楚。这项系统回顾和荟萃分析估计了 ​​DENV 和 CHIKV 的 患病率、它们的国家分布、诊断方法、流行的 DENV 血清型和蚊子 媒介。方法:按照 PRISMA 指南,对 PubMed、Web of Science、Scopus、SpringerLink、Nature Portfolio 和 Google Scholar 搜索截至 2025 年 10 月发表的研究。符合条件的研究报告了肯尼亚、埃塞俄比亚或索马里的人类或蚊子感染 DENV 和/或 CHIKV。进行随机效应荟萃分析,并按国家、人口、环境和诊断方法进行亚组分析。结果:在 3,351 项记录中,有 65 项研究符合纳入标准,其中大多数来自肯尼亚 (63.1%),其次是埃塞俄比亚 (30.8%) 和索马里 (6.1%)。汇集的 DENV 患病率 IgG 为 20%,IgM 为 10%,NS1 为 6%,PCR 为 4%。相应的 CHIKV 患病率 对于 IgG 为 5%,对于 IgM 为 8%,通过 PCR 检测为 4%。各国之间的 DENV NS1 (p<0.0001) 和 PCR 确诊感染 (p=0.01) 存在显着差异。与发热患者 (6%) 和蚊子 媒介 (1%) 相比,DENV 患病率 在一般人群中最高 (16%) (p=0.033),而 CHIKV 患病率 在农村地区最高 (15%) (p=0.0002)。所有四种 DENV 血清型均在肯尼亚和埃塞俄比亚传播,而 DENV-2 和 DENV-3 则在索马里报告。埃及伊蚊是主要的 媒介,92.3% 的 媒介 研究均报告了这一情况。结论:DENV 和 CHIKV 对全球公共卫生和非洲之角来说是重要且可能未被充分认识的虫媒病毒威胁,有证据表明 传播 持续存在且广泛暴露。需要加强 监测、诊断能力、监测 控制和区域合作,以减轻其日益增长的公共卫生影响。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注非洲之角,特别是肯尼亚、埃塞俄比亚和索马里的 登革病毒 (DENV) 和基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV)。核心问题是估计这些虫媒病毒的 患病率、它们的国家分布、诊断方法、流行的 DENV 血清型和蚊子 媒介。
证据基础与方法
遵循 PRISMA 指南进行的系统回顾和荟萃分析,搜索 PubMed、Web of Science、Scopus、SpringerLink、Nature Portfolio 和 Google Scholar,查找截至 2025 年 10 月发表的研究。符合条件的研究报告了肯尼亚、埃塞俄比亚或索马里的人类或蚊子感染 DENV 和/或 CHIKV。随机效应荟萃分析按国家、人口、环境和诊断方法进行亚组分析。
共识与结论
DENV 和 CHIKV 是非洲之角重要且可能未被充分认识的虫媒病毒威胁,有证据表明 传播 持续存在且广泛暴露。汇集的 DENV 患病率 IgG 为 20%,IgM 为 10%,NS1 为 6%,PCR 为 4%。IgG 的 CHIKV 患病率 为 5%,IgM 的 CHIKV 患病率 为 8%,PCR 为 4%。所有四种 DENV 血清型均在肯尼亚和埃塞俄比亚传播,而 DENV-2 和 DENV-3 则在索马里报告。埃及伊蚊是主要的 媒介。
争议与缺口
各国之间的 DENV NS1 (p<0.0001) 和 PCR 确诊感染 (p=0.01) 存在显着差异。与发热患者 (6%) 和蚊子 媒介 (1%) 相比,DENV 患病率 在一般人群中最高 (16%) (p=0.033),而 CHIKV 患病率 在农村地区最高 (15%) (p=0.0002)。该审查强调了 监测 的差距以及该地区这些病毒的特征。
科研与实践启示
需要加强 监测、诊断能力、媒介 控制和区域合作,以减轻 DENV 和 CHIKV 对非洲之角日益增长的公共卫生影响。

Prevalence of Dengue and Chikungunya in the Horn of Africa: A Systematic Review and Meta-analysis

Authors: Aamir Muse Osman, Mercy Mwenya, Francis Oketch Ochieng, Hellen Ayilo Akengo, Joel Lutomiah, Konongoi S. Limbaso, Ahmed Abdulkadir Hassan-Kadle, Fredrick Ouma Odhiambo, Marian Muse Osman, Bernard Bett
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue virus (DENV) and Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) are important mosquito-borne arboviruses with expanding global distributions. Although recurrent outbreaks have occurred in the Horn of Africa since the early 2000s, their epidemiology and burden remain poorly characterized. This systematic review and meta-analysis estimated the prevalence of DENV and CHIKV, their distribution by country, diagnostic methods, circulating DENV serotypes, and mosquito vectors. Methods: Following PRISMA guidelines, PubMed, Web of Science, Scopus, SpringerLink, Nature Portfolio, and Google Scholar were searched for studies published up to October 2025. Eligible studies reported DENV and/or CHIKV infections in humans or mosquitoes in Kenya, Ethiopia, or Somalia. Random-effects meta-analyses were performed, with subgroup analyses by country, population, setting, and diagnostic method. Results: Of 3,351 records, 65 studies met the inclusion criteria, most from Kenya (63.1%), followed by Ethiopia (30.8%) and Somalia (6.1%). Pooled DENV prevalence was 20% for IgG, 10% for IgM, 6% for NS1, and 4% by PCR. Corresponding CHIKV prevalence was 5% for IgG, 8% for IgM, and 4% by PCR. Significant differences between countries were observed for DENV NS1 (p<0.0001) and PCR-confirmed infections (p=0.01). DENV prevalence was highest in the general population (16%) compared with febrile patients (6%) and mosquito vectors (1%) (p=0.033), whereas CHIKV prevalence was highest in rural settings (15%) (p=0.0002). All four DENV serotypes circulated in Kenya and Ethiopia, while DENV-2 and DENV-3 were reported in Somalia. Aedes aegypti was the predominant vector, reported in 92.3% of vector studies. Conclusions: DENV and CHIKV are important and likely underrecognized arboviral threats to global public health and in the Horn of Africa, with evidence of ongoing transmission and widespread exposure. Strengthened surveillance, diagnostic capacity, vector control, and regional collaboration are needed to mitigate their growing public health impact.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on dengue virus (DENV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) in the Horn of Africa, specifically in Kenya, Ethiopia, and Somalia. The central question is to estimate the prevalence of these arboviruses, their distribution by country, diagnostic methods, circulating DENV serotypes, and mosquito vectors.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review and meta-analysis following PRISMA guidelines, searching PubMed, Web of Science, Scopus, SpringerLink, Nature Portfolio, and Google Scholar for studies published up to October 2025. Eligible studies reported DENV and/or CHIKV infections in humans or mosquitoes in Kenya, Ethiopia, or Somalia. Random-effects meta-analyses were performed with subgroup analyses by country, population, setting, and diagnostic method.
Consensus and conclusions
DENV and CHIKV are important and likely underrecognized arboviral threats in the Horn of Africa, with evidence of ongoing transmission and widespread exposure. Pooled DENV prevalence was 20% for IgG, 10% for IgM, 6% for NS1, and 4% by PCR. CHIKV prevalence was 5% for IgG, 8% for IgM, and 4% by PCR. All four DENV serotypes circulated in Kenya and Ethiopia, while DENV-2 and DENV-3 were reported in Somalia. Aedes aegypti was the predominant vector.
Controversies and gaps
Significant differences between countries were observed for DENV NS1 (p<0.0001) and PCR-confirmed infections (p=0.01). DENV prevalence was highest in the general population (16%) compared with febrile patients (6%) and mosquito vectors (1%) (p=0.033), whereas CHIKV prevalence was highest in rural settings (15%) (p=0.0002). The review highlights gaps in surveillance and characterization of these viruses in the region.
Research and practice implications
Strengthened surveillance, diagnostic capacity, vector control, and regional collaboration are needed to mitigate the growing public health impact of DENV and CHIKV in the Horn of Africa.

在阿拉伯联合酋长国迪拜的埃及伊蚊中检测到 登革病毒

Detection of Dengue Virus in Aedes aegypti Mosquitoes, Dubai, United Arab Emirates
作者： Norbert Nowotny, Noushad Karuvantevida, Elmahi El Tilib Gubran, Naseem Mohammed Rafee, Adel Abdulla AlKarrani, Ghanim Al Hammadi, Nasna Nassir, Dia Advani, Mohammed J Uddin, Jeremy V. Camp
摘要中文翻译继 2024 年 登革病毒 暴发 后,我们在阿拉伯联合酋长国迪拜进行了为期 1 年的蚊子调查。我们收集了 1,598 只埃及伊蚊,并在 5 个蚊子数量少于 20 只的池中检测到了 登革病毒。我们的研究结果强调了持续 媒介 监测 对于 登革病毒 在干旱城市环境中的重要性。
